<h2>Verb Details</h2>

### 1. abandon <a id="1-abandon"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *abandoner* (mettre à bandon — "to put under control"), from *à bandon* ("at will, under control"), from *bandon* ("jurisdiction, power").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *abandoner*（mettre à bandon — "置于控制之下"），源自 *à bandon*（"随意，控制之下"），源自 *bandon*（"管辖权，权力"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to leave completely and finally; to give up
- CN: 放弃；遗弃；抛弃

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Used with a direct object: abandon sth/sb — 与直接宾语连用：abandon sth/sb
- "Abandon oneself to sth" means to give in completely to an emotion — "Abandon oneself to sth" 意为完全沉溺于某种情感
- Stronger connotation than "give up" — implies finality — 语气比 "give up" 更强，暗示最终性

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- abandon hope
- abandon ship
- abandon a plan/project
- abandon one's post
- completely abandon
- abandon one's family
- abandon a search


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 放弃希望
- 弃船
- 放弃计划/项目
- 擅离职守
- 完全放弃
- 抛弃家人
- 放弃搜索
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- abandon oneself to despair
- abandon sth to sb
- abandon sb to sth
- abandon sth for sth
- abandon hope of
- abandon in favor of


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 陷入绝望
- 把某物遗弃给某人
- 把某人遗弃给某境
- 放弃某物而选择他物
- 放弃…的希望
- 放弃…而选择…
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The captain ordered the crew to abandon the sinking ship. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: He abandoned his research after years of fruitless effort. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Many residents were forced to abandon their homes due to the volcanic eruption. (TOEFL TPO 31)
- Academic: The study had to be abandoned due to insufficient funding. (Nature 2019)
- Common: Don't abandon your dreams just because of one setback.
- 介词搭配: The company abandoned its traditional manufacturing model for a fully automated system.
- 介词搭配: After losing the championship, he abandoned himself to despair for weeks.
- 介词搭配: The reckless driver abandoned his passengers to their fate when the car broke down in the middle of the desert.
- 介词搭配: The young man abandoned hope of ever finding his biological parents after years of unsuccessful searching.
- 介词搭配: The committee abandoned the original proposal in favor of a more cost-effective alternative.
- 介词搭配: The villagers abandoned their livestock to the floodwaters as they fled to higher ground.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 船长命令船员弃船。
- 经过多年徒劳的努力，他放弃了研究。
- 许多居民因火山爆发被迫撤离家园。
- 该研究因资金不足而不得不中止。
- 不要因为一次挫折就放弃梦想。
- 该公司放弃传统的制造模式，转而采用全自动化系统。
- 失去冠军后，他连续数周陷入绝望。
- 当汽车在沙漠中抛锚时，该鲁莽的司机弃乘客于不顾，任其听天由命。
- 经过多年的寻找未果，这个年轻人放弃了找到亲生父母的希望。
- 委员会放弃了最初的提案，转而选择了一个更具成本效益的方案。
- 村民们逃往高地时，把牲畜遗弃给了洪水。
</details>

### 2. abide <a id="2-abide"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old English *ābīdan* ("remain, wait"), from *a-* (intensive) + *bīdan* ("stay, continue").

**词源：** 源自古英语 *ābīdan*（"停留，等待"），源自 *a-*（加强前缀）+ *bīdan*（"停留，继续"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to accept or act in accordance with; to tolerate; to remain
- CN: 遵守；忍受；停留

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Abide by" (phrasal verb, intransitive) means to follow rules or decisions — "Abide by" (短语动词，不及物) 意为遵守规则或决定
- In negative constructions: "can't abide" = strongly dislike — 在否定结构中："can't abide" = 非常讨厌
- "Abiding" (adj.) means long-lasting — "Abiding" (形容词) 意为持久的

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- can't abide sb/sth
- abide the consequences


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 无法忍受某人/某事
- 承担后果
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- abide by the rules
- abide by the law
- abide by a decision
- abide by the contract
- abide by the terms
- strictly abide by
- abide by the agreement
- abide by the court's ruling


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 遵守规则
- 遵守法律
- 服从决定
- 遵守合同
- 遵守条款
- 严格遵守
- 遵守协议
- 服从法院裁决
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: All members must abide by the regulations set by the committee. (CET-4 2022-06)
- 考研: He could not abide the constant criticism from his colleagues. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Visitors are expected to abide by local customs and traditions. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: Researchers must abide by strict ethical guidelines. (Science 2021)
- Common: If you join the club, you agree to abide by its rules.
- 介词搭配: All employees must strictly abide by the company's code of conduct.
- 介词搭配: The losing party has no choice but to abide by the court's ruling.
- 介词搭配: All citizens are expected to abide by the rules and regulations of the community.
- 介词搭配: All citizens, regardless of their status, must abide by the law of the land.
- 介词搭配: The shareholders agreed to abide by the board's final decision regarding the merger.
- 介词搭配: Failure to abide by the contract may result in legal action and financial penalties.
- 介词搭配: All parties involved in the contract must abide by the terms agreed upon during the negotiation.
- 介词搭配: Both countries pledged to abide by the agreement on climate change reduction targets.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 所有成员必须遵守委员会制定的规定。
- 他无法忍受同事不断的批评。
- 游客应遵守当地风俗和传统。
- 研究人员必须遵守严格的伦理准则。
- 如果你加入俱乐部，就同意遵守其规则。
- 所有员工必须严格遵守公司的行为准则。
- 败诉方别无选择，只能服从法院裁决。
- 所有公民都应遵守社区的规章制度。
- 所有公民，不论地位如何，都必须遵守国家法律。
- 股东们同意服从董事会关于合并的最终决定。
- 未能遵守合同可能导致法律诉讼和经济处罚。
- 合同各方必须遵守谈判期间商定的条款。
- 两国承诺遵守关于气候变化减排目标的协议。
</details>

### 3. abolish <a id="3-abolish"></a>
**Etymology:** From Middle French *aboliss-*, stem of *abolir* ("to abolish"), from Latin *abolēre* ("to destroy, cause to die out").

**词源：** 源自中古法语 *aboliss-*，*abolir* 的词干（"废除"），源自拉丁语 *abolēre*（"毁灭，使消亡"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to officially put an end to something (a system, practice, institution)
- CN: 废除；废止；取消

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Used with formal/legal/institutional objects (laws, slavery, taxes) — 与正式/法律/制度类宾语连用（法律、奴隶制、税收）
- Cannot be used with informal objects (don't "abolish a meeting" — use "cancel")
- Stronger/more formal than "cancel" — 比 "cancel" 语气更强/更正式

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- abolish slavery
- abolish the death penalty
- abolish a law
- abolish a system
- abolish tariffs
- abolish discrimination
- abolish a policy
- completely abolish
- abolish the monarchy
- abolish restrictions


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 废除奴隶制
- 废除死刑
- 废除一项法律
- 废除一个制度
- 废除关税
- 废除歧视
- 废除一项政策
- 完全废除
- 废除君主制
- 废除限制
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- abolish sth by law
- abolish sth through legislation
- abolish sth in favor of
- abolish across the board


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 依法废除
- 通过立法废除
- 废除…以支持…
- 全面废除
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Many countries have abolished the death penalty in recent decades. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The government decided to abolish outdated regulations to boost economic growth. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The movement aimed to abolish discriminatory practices in the workplace. (TOEFL TPO 28)
- Academic: The amendment effectively abolished segregation in public schools. (Nature Human Behaviour 2020)
- Common: Students campaigned to abolish the strict dress code policy.
- 介词搭配: The government abolished the outdated tax system through new legislation.
- 介词搭配: The school abolished final exams in favor of continuous assessment.
- 介词搭配: The death penalty was abolished by law in that country after decades of heated debate.
- 介词搭配: The government abolished import tariffs across the board to stimulate international trade.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 政府决定废除过时的法规以促进经济增长。
- 该运动旨在废除工作场所的歧视性做法。
- 该修正案有效地废除了公立学校的种族隔离。
- 学生们发起运动要求废除严格的着装规定。
- 政府通过新立法废除了过时的税收制度。
- 学校废除了期末考试，转而采用持续性评估。
- 经过数十年的激烈辩论，该国依法废除了死刑。
- 政府全面取消了进口关税以刺激国际贸易。
</details>

### 4. abort <a id="4-abort"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *abortus*, past participle of *aboriri* ("to miscarry, to disappear"), from *ab-* ("away") + *oriri* ("to rise, be born").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *abortus*，*aboriri*（"流产，消失"）的过去分词，源自 *ab-*（"离开"）+ *oriri*（"升起，出生"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to terminate prematurely; to cause something to end before completion
- CN: 中止；使流产；放弃（任务）

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Commonly used in computing (abort a program), medicine (abort a pregnancy), and aviation/space (abort a mission) — 常用于计算机（中止程序）、医学（终止妊娠）和航空/航天（中止任务）
- Can be used intransitively: The mission aborted. — 可以不及物使用：The mission aborted.

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- abort a mission
- abort a pregnancy
- abort a program/process
- abort a takeoff
- abort a plan
- abort a launch
- abort a flight
- abort an operation
- automatically abort
- abort an experiment


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 中止任务
- 终止妊娠
- 中止程序/进程
- 中止起飞
- 放弃计划
- 中止发射
- 中止飞行
- 中止操作
- 自动中止
- 中止实验
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- abort due to
- abort in progress
- abort at launch
- abort for safety reasons


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 因…而中止
- 在进行中中止
- 在发射时中止
- 因安全原因中止
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The pilot was forced to abort the takeoff due to an engine malfunction. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The experiment had to be aborted when the equipment overheated. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The space agency decided to abort the launch minutes before the scheduled time. (TOEFL TPO 40)
- Academic: The clinical trial was aborted after severe side effects were reported. (The Lancet 2021)
- Common: If the software freezes, press Ctrl+C to abort the operation.
- 介词搭配: The flight was aborted due to severe weather conditions at the destination airport.
- 介词搭配: The engineers decided to abort the test for safety reasons after detecting a pressure leak.
- 介词搭配: The software installation was aborted in progress due to a critical system error.
- 介词搭配: The space agency had to abort the rocket at launch when telemetry data showed abnormal readings.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 飞行员因发动机故障被迫中止起飞。
- 设备过热时，实验不得不中止。
- 航天局在预定发射时间前几分钟决定中止发射。
- 在报告严重副作用后，该临床试验被中止。
- 如果软件卡住，按Ctrl+C中止操作。
- 航班因目的地机场的恶劣天气条件而被取消。
- 工程师们因检测到压力泄漏而出于安全原因决定中止测试。
- 由于系统严重错误，软件安装在进行中被中止。
- 当遥测数据显示异常读数时，航天局不得不在发射时中止火箭发射。
</details>

### 5. absorb <a id="5-absorb"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *absorbir*, from Latin *absorbere* ("to swallow up, devour"), from *ab-* ("from, away") + *sorbere* ("to suck in").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *absorbir*，源自拉丁语 *absorbere*（"吞没，吞噬"），源自 *ab-*（"从，离开"）+ *sorbere*（"吸入"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to take in or soak up; to engage fully; to assimilate
- CN: 吸收；吸引；使全神贯注

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Can be literal (liquid/light) or figurative (knowledge/information) — 可以是字面意义（液体/光）或比喻意义（知识/信息）- "Be absorbed in" = be completely focused on — "Be absorbed in" = 全神贯注于
- Often used in passive voice — 常用于被动语态

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- absorb knowledge/information
- absorb heat/light/moisture
- absorb the impact/shock
- absorb costs/losses
- absorb nutrients
- absorb a lesson
- absorb water


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 吸收知识/信息
- 吸收热量/光/水分
- 缓冲冲击/震动
- 承担成本/损失
- 吸收营养
- 吸取教训
- 吸收水分
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be absorbed in thought
- be absorbed into
- absorb sth into sth
- absorb sth from sth
- absorb through sth
- absorb in sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 陷入沉思
- 被并入/吸收进
- 将某物吸收进…中
- 从…中吸收某物
- 通过…吸收
- 吸收进…
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Plants absorb carbon dioxide and release oxygen during photosynthesis. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: The young writer was completely absorbed in crafting her novel. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Sponges are highly efficient at absorbing water and retaining it. (TOEFL TPO 33)
- Academic: The ocean absorbs approximately 30% of the carbon dioxide emitted by human activities. (Nature Climate Change 2022)
- Common: She sat quietly, absorbed in reading her favorite book.
- 介词搭配: Smaller companies were gradually absorbed into the larger corporation after the merger.
- 介词搭配: Plants absorb nutrients from the soil through their root systems.
- 介词搭配: The company plans to absorb the new technology into its existing production processes.
- 介词搭配: The sponge can absorb a large amount of water in a very short time.
- 介词搭配: The old man sat by the window, completely absorbed in thought, oblivious to the world around him.
- 介词搭配: The roots of the plant absorb water and minerals from the surrounding soil through an efficient capillary system.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 植物在光合作用中吸收二氧化碳并释放氧气。
- 这位年轻作家全神贯注地创作她的小说。
- 海绵在吸收和保持水分方面非常高效。
- 海洋吸收了人类活动排放的约30%的二氧化碳。
- 她静静地坐着，全神贯注地读着她最喜欢的书。
- 合并后，较小的公司逐渐被并入这家大企业。
- 植物通过根系从土壤中吸收养分。
- 该公司计划将这项新技术吸收到现有的生产流程中。
- 海绵能在极短时间内吸收大量水分。
- 老人坐在窗边，完全陷入沉思，对周围的世界浑然不觉。
- 植物的根通过高效的毛细系统从周围的土壤中吸收水分和矿物质。
</details>

### 6. abstract <a id="6-abstract"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *abstractus*, past participle of *abstrahere* ("to drag away, divert"), from *ab-* ("away") + *trahere* ("to draw, pull").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *abstractus*，*abstrahere*（"拖走，转移"）的过去分词，源自 *ab-*（"离开"）+ *trahere*（"拉，拖"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to remove or separate; to consider in theory apart from concrete realities; to summarize
- CN: 提取；抽象化；摘要

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- In academic writing, "to abstract" means to write a summary — 在学术写作中，"to abstract" 意为写摘要
- "Abstract from" means to separate from context — "Abstract from" 意为脱离上下文
- More commonly used as an adjective or noun in everyday English — 在日常英语中更常用作形容词或名词

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- abstract a concept
- abstract the main points
- abstract information
- abstract principles
- abstract relevant features


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 抽象出一个概念
- 提取要点
- 提取信息
- 抽象出原则
- 提取相关特征
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- abstract data from
- abstract from reality
- abstract metal from ore
- be abstracted from context
- abstract sth from sth
- abstract away from
- abstract into categories
- abstract beyond


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从…提取数据
- 脱离现实
- 从矿石中提取金属
- 脱离上下文
- 从…中抽象出某物
- 从…中抽象出来
- 归类抽象
- 抽象超越
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: It is difficult to abstract general principles from such diverse cases. (CET-6 2019-06)
- 考研: Philosophers often abstract ideas from concrete experiences. (考研英语 2017)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The researcher abstracted key findings from the raw survey data. (TOEFL TPO 52)
- Academic: The algorithm abstracts relevant features from the input image. (IEEE Transactions 2021)
- Common: Can you abstract the main points from that lengthy article?
- 介词搭配: The chemical process abstracts pure iron from the mined ore through high-temperature smelting.
- 介词搭配: The researcher abstracted the survey responses into three main categories for analysis.
- 介词搭配: The philosopher's argument is too abstracted from reality to be applicable to everyday life.
- 介词搭配: It is important to abstract the key data from the raw dataset before performing statistical analysis.
- 介词搭配: The artist's style has gradually abstracted away from traditional representation toward pure form and color.
- 介词搭配: When the politician's remarks are abstracted from context, they can easily be misinterpreted by the media.
- 介词搭配: The philosopher's latest theory abstracts beyond conventional ethical frameworks into entirely new moral territory.
- 介词搭配: The analyst was able to abstract meaningful patterns from the vast dataset using advanced algorithms.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 从如此多样的案例中抽象出一般原则是困难的。
- 哲学家常常从具体经验中抽象出思想。
- 研究人员从原始调查数据中提取了关键发现。
- 该算法从输入图像中提取相关特征。
- 你能从那篇冗长的文章中提取要点吗？
- 该化学过程通过高温冶炼从开采的矿石中提取纯铁。
- 研究人员将调查回复归纳为三个主要类别进行分析。
- 这位哲学家的论点过于脱离现实，无法应用于日常生活。
- 在进行统计分析前，从原始数据集中提取关键数据很重要。
- 这位艺术家的风格已逐渐从传统具象中抽象出来，转向纯粹的形式与色彩。
- 当这位政治人物的言论脱离上下文时，很容易被媒体误解。
- 这位哲学家的最新理论超越了传统伦理框架，进入了全新的道德领域。
- 分析师能够使用先进算法从庞大的数据集中提取有意义的模式。
</details>

### 7. abuse <a id="7-abuse"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *abuser* ("to misuse, deceive"), from Latin *abūsī* ("to use up, misuse"), from *ab-* ("away, wrongly") + *ūtī* ("to use").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *abuser*（"滥用，欺骗"），源自拉丁语 *abūsī*（"用完，滥用"），源自 *ab-*（"离开，错误地"）+ *ūtī*（"使用"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to use improperly or excessively; to treat cruelly or violently
- CN: 滥用；虐待；辱骂

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Covers three areas: substance abuse, verbal abuse, physical abuse — 涵盖三个方面：药物滥用、言语辱骂、身体虐待
- Very strong negative connotation — 非常强烈的负面含义
- Noun form is common: "child abuse," "drug abuse" — 名词形式很常见："child abuse," "drug abuse"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- abuse power/authority
- abuse alcohol/drugs
- child abuse
- verbally/physically abuse
- abuse one's position
- abuse the system
- abuse a privilege
- systematically abuse
- substance abuse
- abuse a trust


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 滥用权力/权威
- 酗酒/滥用药物
- 虐待儿童
- 言语/身体虐待
- 滥用职位
- 滥用制度
- 滥用特权
- 系统性虐待
- 药物滥用
- 辜负信任
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- abuse of power
- abuse of trust
- abuse of authority
- abuse through neglect


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 滥用权力（名词用法）
- 辜负信任（名词用法）
- 滥用职权（名词用法）
- 因忽视而虐待
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Officials who abuse their power should be held accountable. (CET-4 2020-09)
- 考研: The study revealed that many teenagers abuse prescription drugs without understanding the risks. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The documentary exposed how the company abused environmental regulations for profit. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: Abused children are more likely to develop psychological disorders later in life. (The American Journal of Psychiatry 2020)
- Common: No one should have to endure a relationship where they are constantly abused.
- 介词搭配: The elderly patient was abused through neglect by the nursing home staff who failed to provide basic care.
- 介词搭配: She felt deeply betrayed when her closest friend abused her trust by revealing a confidential secret.
- 介词搭配: The investigation revealed systemic abuse of power within the government agency.
- 介词搭配: The inspector was found guilty of abuse of authority for demanding bribes from local businesses.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 滥用职权的官员应被追究责任。
- 研究显示，许多青少年在未了解风险的情况下滥用处方药。
- 纪录片揭露了该公司如何为牟利而滥用环境法规。
- 受虐待的儿童在以后的生活中更可能出现心理障碍。
- 没有人应该忍受一段不断被虐待的关系。
- 这位老年患者因养老院工作人员未能提供基本护理而受到忽视虐待。
- 当她最亲密的朋友泄露机密而辜负她的信任时，她感到深深被背叛。
- 调查揭露了该政府机构内系统性的滥用权力行为。
- 该检查员因向当地企业索贿而被判滥用职权罪。
</details>

### 8. accelerate <a id="8-accelerate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *accelerātus*, past participle of *accelerāre* ("to hasten, quicken"), from *ad-* ("toward") + *celer* ("swift, fast").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *accelerātus*，*accelerāre*（"加速，加快"）的过去分词，源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *celer*（"迅速的"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to increase speed; to cause to happen faster
- CN: 加速；促进；提前

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Can be used transitively (accelerate growth) and intransitively (the car accelerates) — 可以及物（加速增长）和不及物（汽车加速）使用
- Opposite: decelerate — 反义词：decelerate（减速）
- Common in scientific/business contexts — 在科学/商业语境中常见

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- accelerate growth
- accelerate development
- accelerate change
- accelerate a process
- accelerate innovation
- accelerate progress
- significantly accelerate
- accelerate reform
- accelerate production


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 加速增长
- 加速发展
- 加速变革
- 加速进程
- 加速创新
- 加速进步
- 显著加速
- 加速改革
- 加速生产
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- accelerate the pace of
- accelerate toward sth
- accelerate at a rate
- accelerate beyond
- accelerate from zero to
- accelerate into


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 加快…的步伐
- 向…加速
- 以…速率加速
- 加速超过…
- 从零加速到…
- 加速进入…
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Economic reforms have accelerated the country's development. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: Climate change is accelerating at an alarming rate due to human activity. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The car accelerated smoothly from 0 to 60 miles per hour in just six seconds. (TOEFL TPO 36)
- Academic: Global warming is accelerating the melting of polar ice caps. (Nature Geoscience 2022)
- Common: If you press the gas pedal harder, the car will accelerate more quickly.
- 介词搭配: The new government initiative aims to accelerate the pace of technological innovation in renewable energy.
- 介词搭配: The high-speed train can accelerate from zero to 300 kilometers per hour in under sixty seconds.
- 介词搭配: The economy is accelerating toward a potential recession if interest rates continue to rise.
- 介词搭配: The particle accelerates at a rate of 9.8 meters per second squared in free fall.
- 介词搭配: The car accelerated beyond the speed limit as it raced down the highway.
- 介词搭配: The spacecraft will accelerate into orbit once it clears the atmosphere.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 经济改革加速了该国的发展。
- 由于人类活动，气候变化正以惊人的速度加速。
- 汽车在短短六秒内从0加速到每小时60英里。
- 全球变暖正在加速极地冰盖的融化。
- 如果你用力踩油门，汽车会加速更快。
- 新的政府举措旨在加快可再生能源技术创新的步伐。
- 这辆高速列车能在60秒内从零加速到每小时300公里。
- 如果利率继续上升，经济正向潜在衰退加速发展。
- 该粒子在自由落体中以每秒9.8米的速度加速。
- 汽车在高速公路上疾驰时加速超过了限速。
- 航天器一旦冲出大气层，将加速进入轨道。
</details>

### 9. accept <a id="9-accept"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *accepter*, from Latin *acceptāre* (frequentative of *accipere* "to receive"), from *ad-* ("to") + *capere* ("to take, seize").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *accepter*，源自拉丁语 *acceptāre*（*accipere* "接收"的反复动词），源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *capere*（"拿，取"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to receive willingly; to agree to; to believe to be true
- CN: 接受；同意；承认

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Very common verb with broad usage — 非常常见的动词，用法广泛
- "Accept" vs. "except" are commonly confused — "Accept" 与 "except" 常被混淆
- "Accept that + clause" = to acknowledge — "Accept that + 从句" = 承认

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- accept an invitation
- accept responsibility
- accept the fact that
- accept an offer
- accept a challenge
- accept the consequences
- accept criticism
- willingly accept
- accept a proposal
- accept terms and conditions


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 接受邀请
- 承担责任
- 接受…的事实
- 接受提议/录取
- 接受挑战
- 接受后果
- 接受批评
- 欣然接受
- 接受提议
- 接受条款和条件
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- accept sth from sb
- accept sb into sth
- accept as true
- accept in principle
- accept with pleasure


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从某人处接受某物
- 接纳某人进入…
- 认为是真的
- 原则上接受
- 欣然接受
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: She decided to accept the job offer despite the lower salary. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: It took him a long time to accept the reality of the situation. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The university accepted 30% more applicants this year compared to last year. (TOEFL TPO 42)
- Academic: The scientific community quickly accepted the new theory. (Nature Physics 2021)
- Common: I accept full responsibility for my actions.
- 介词搭配: Despite her initial reservations, she was accepted into the doctoral program at Cambridge University.
- 介词搭配: The board accepted the merger proposal in principle but requested further financial analysis.
- 介词搭配: She reluctantly accepted the award from the committee on behalf of her entire research team.
- 介词搭配: The scientific community now accepts the theory as true after years of experimental verification.
- 介词搭配: We accept your generous invitation with great pleasure.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 尽管薪水较低，她还是决定接受这份工作。
- 他花了很长时间才接受现实。
- 该大学今年比去年多录取了30%的申请者。
- 科学界迅速接受了这一新理论。
- 我对自己的行为承担全部责任。
- 尽管最初有所顾虑，她还是被剑桥大学的博士项目录取了。
- 董事会原则上接受了合并提案，但要求进行进一步的财务分析。
- 她代表整个研究团队勉强从委员会那里接受了奖项。
- 经过多年的实验验证，科学界现在认为该理论是真实的。
- 我们非常高兴地接受您的盛情邀请。
</details>

### 10. acclaim <a id="10-acclaim"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *acclāmāre* ("to cry out at, shout approval"), from *ad-* ("to, toward") + *clamāre* ("to cry out, shout").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *acclāmāre*（"对…大喊，欢呼"），源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *clamāre*（"喊叫"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to praise enthusiastically and publicly
- CN: 称赞；欢呼；为…喝彩

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used in passive voice: "was acclaimed as" — 常用于被动语态："was acclaimed as"
- More formal than "praise" — 比 "praise" 更正式
- Noun form: "acclaim" (universal acclaim, critical acclaim) — 名词形式："acclaim"（普遍赞誉，评论界赞誉）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- critical acclaim
- universal acclaim
- public acclaim
- international acclaim
- widespread acclaim
- receive acclaim
- earn acclaim


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 评论界赞誉
- 普遍赞誉
- 公众赞誉
- 国际赞誉
- 广泛赞誉
- 获得赞誉
- 赢得赞誉
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be acclaimed as
- win acclaim for
- be acclaimed by
- acclaim sb/sth as sth
- acclaim for achievement
- acclaim by critics
- acclaim throughout the world


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 被誉为
- 因…赢得赞誉
- 受到…的赞誉
- 称赞某人/某物为…
- 因成就而受赞誉
- 受评论家赞誉
- 受全世界赞誉
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The novel was widely acclaimed by critics and readers alike. (CET-6 2020-12)

- TOEFL/IELTS: The architect's design was acclaimed as a masterpiece of modern engineering. (TOEFL TPO 49)
- Academic: The paper was acclaimed by the scientific community for its innovative methodology. (Science 2021)
- Common: The film received widespread acclaim at the international festival.
- 介词搭配: Critics acclaimed the young filmmaker as a groundbreaking voice in contemporary cinema.
- 介词搭配: The renowned architect won international acclaim for her innovative and sustainable building designs.
- 介词搭配: The novel was immediately acclaimed as a masterpiece of modern American literature upon its publication.
- 介词搭配: The young pianist was acclaimed by the international music community for her extraordinary technical skill.
- 介词搭配: The actor won critical acclaim for his powerful portrayal of a war veteran.
- 介词搭配: The author's work has received acclaim throughout the world and been translated into dozens of languages.
- 介词搭配: The director's latest film has received acclaim by critics for its innovative storytelling and visual effects.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这位建筑师的设计被誉为现代工程的杰作。

- 这位演员因其对退伍军人的有力刻画而赢得评论界赞誉。
- 这位作家的作品受到全世界的赞誉，并被翻译成数十种语言。

### 11. accommodate <a id="11-accommodate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *accommodātus*, past participle of *accommodāre* ("to make fit, adapt"), from *ad-* ("to") + *commodus* ("fit, convenient").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *accommodātus*，*accommodāre*（"使适合，适应"）的过去分词，源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *commodus*（"合适的，方便的"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to provide lodging or room for; to adapt to; to satisfy
- CN: 容纳；提供住宿；适应；满足

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used with a direct object: accommodate guests, accommodate needs — 常与直接宾语连用：accommodate guests, accommodate needs
- "Accommodate to" = adapt to (though direct use is more common) — "Accommodate to" = 适应（虽然直接使用更常见）
- Note double "c" and double "m" in spelling (commonly misspelled) — 注意拼写中有双 "c" 和双 "m"（常被拼错）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- accommodate guests
- accommodate someone's needs
- accommodate changes
- accommodate a request
- accommodate differences
- accommodate a growing population
- accommodate a schedule
- comfortably accommodate
- accommodate diversity


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 容纳/接待客人
- 满足某人的需求
- 适应变化
- 满足请求
- 容纳差异
- 容纳不断增长的人口
- 调整日程
- 舒适地容纳
- 容纳多样性
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- accommodate sb with
- accommodate sb with sth
- accommodate to sth
- accommodate in sth
- accommodate for differences
- accommodate by doing


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 为某人提供
- 为某人提供某物
- 适应…
- 容纳在某物中
- 为差异而调整
- 通过做…来适应
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The hotel can accommodate up to 500 guests. (CET-4 2022-06)
- 考研: The curriculum was redesigned to accommodate students with different learning styles. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The auditorium accommodates 2,000 people comfortably. (TOEFL TPO 35)
- Academic: The ecosystem must accommodate shifts in temperature and precipitation. (Nature Ecology & Evolution 2022)
- Common: We'll do our best to accommodate your dietary restrictions.
- 介词搭配: The airline accommodated us with a complimentary hotel stay after the flight was canceled.
- 介词搭配: Immigrants must accommodate to the legal and social systems of their new country.
- 介词搭配: The new stadium can accommodate over 80,000 spectators in its state-of-the-art facilities.
- 介词搭配: The teacher modified the test format to accommodate for differences in students' learning abilities.
- 介词搭配: The company accommodated the disabled employee by providing ergonomic office equipment.
- 介词搭配: New employees must accommodate to the company's corporate culture and work environment during their first few weeks.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这家酒店最多可容纳500位客人。
- 课程经过重新设计，以适应不同学习风格的学生。
- 礼堂可舒适地容纳2000人。
- 生态系统必须适应温度和降水的变化。
- 我们会尽力满足您的饮食限制。
- 航班取消后，航空公司为我们免费提供了住宿。
- 移民必须适应新国家的法律和社会体系。
- 这座新体育场的先进设施可容纳超过8万名观众。
- 老师修改了考试形式以适应学生学习能力的差异。
- 公司通过提供符合人体工学的办公设备来适应残障员工的需求。
- 新员工在最初几周必须适应公司的企业文化和工作环境。
</details>

### 12. accompany <a id="12-accompany"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *accompagner*, from *a-* ("to") + *compaignon* ("companion"), from Latin *companiō* ("bread fellow").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *accompagner*，源自 *a-*（"向"）+ *compaignon*（"同伴"），源自拉丁语 *companiō*（"面包伙伴"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to go with or be associated with; to play a musical instrument supporting another performer
- CN: 陪伴；伴随；为…伴奏

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Accompany" is formal; "go with" is more common in speech — "Accompany" 较正式；口语中 "go with" 更常见
- In music, "accompany on + instrument" — 在音乐中，"accompany on + 乐器"
- "Be accompanied by" is passive form — "Be accompanied by" 是被动形式

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- accompany a letter
- accompany the text
- accompany a singer
- always accompany
- accompany the development


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 附上一封信
- 随附文本
- 为歌手伴奏
- 总是伴随
- 伴随发展
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- accompany sb to
- be accompanied by
- accompany on the piano
- accompany a meal with wine
- accompany a speech with gestures
- accompany sb to sth
- accompany sb on sth
- accompany by sth
- accompany with sth
- accompany through sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 陪伴某人去…
- 由…陪同
- 用钢琴伴奏
- 用餐时配酒
- 演讲时配以手势
- 陪伴某人去…
- 用…为某人伴奏
- 由…伴随
- 伴随以…
- 陪伴某人度过…
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Children under 12 must be accompanied by an adult. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: Rising unemployment was accompanied by widespread social unrest. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The singer was accompanied on stage by a full orchestra. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The symptoms are often accompanied by a high fever and severe headache. (The New England Journal of Medicine 2021)
- Common: Will you accompany me to the supermarket this afternoon?
- 介词搭配: The chef recommended accompanying the grilled fish with a crisp white wine.
- 介词搭配: The politician accompanied his controversial speech with elaborate hand gestures to emphasize his points.
- 介词搭配: The young pianist accompanied the famous singer on the piano during the charity concert.
- 介词搭配: Please accompany me to the airport tomorrow morning as I have a lot of luggage.
- 介词搭配: The patient's recovery was accompanied by gradual improvements in both physical and mental health.
- 介词搭配: The experienced mentor accompanied the young entrepreneur through every difficult stage of launching her first startup.
- 介词搭配: The security guard accompanied the VIP guest to the conference hall through a private entrance.
- 介词搭配: The program coordinator accompanied the international students to the orientation session on the first day of the semester.
- 介词搭配: The announcement of the new policy was accompanied by a detailed explanation of its benefits and implementation timeline.
- 介词搭配: The formal dinner was accompanied with live classical music performed by a string quartet.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 在慈善音乐会上，这位年轻的钢琴家用钢琴为这位著名歌手伴奏。

- 患者的康复伴随着身心健康方面的逐步改善。
- 经验丰富的导师陪伴这位年轻企业家度过了创办第一家初创公司的每一个艰难阶段。
- 保安陪同贵宾通过私人入口前往会议厅。
- 项目协调员在学期第一天陪同国际学生参加了迎新会。
- 新政策的宣布附带了对其好处和实施时间表的详细解释。
- 正式晚宴伴有弦乐四重奏现场演奏的古典音乐。
</details>

### 13. accomplish <a id="13-accomplish"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *accomplir* ("to fulfill, complete"), from Latin *ad* ("to") + *complēre* ("to fill up, complete").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *accomplir*（"完成，实现"），源自拉丁语 *ad*（"向"）+ *complēre*（"填满，完成"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to achieve or complete successfully
- CN: 完成；实现；达成

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Focuses on successful completion, not just effort — 强调成功完成，而不仅仅是努力
- Stronger than "complete" — implies a sense of achievement
- Common in goal-oriented contexts — 常用于目标导向的语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- accomplish a goal
- accomplish a task
- accomplish a mission
- accomplish one's objective
- accomplish great things
- accomplish a purpose
- accomplish a feat
- successfully accomplish
- accomplish nothing
- accomplish more


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 实现目标
- 完成任务
- 完成任务
- 实现某人的目标
- 完成大事
- 达到目的
- 完成壮举
- 成功完成
- 一事无成
- 完成更多
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- accomplish through sth
- accomplish by doing sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 通过…完成
- 通过做…而实现
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: With determination and hard work, you can accomplish anything. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: The team accomplished the project ahead of schedule. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: She accomplished a great deal during her time as director. (TOEFL TPO 50)
- Academic: The mission accomplished its primary objective of collecting soil samples. (Space Research Today 2022)
- Common: I feel like I didn't accomplish much today.
- 介词搭配: The team accomplished the challenging project through meticulous planning and effective collaboration.
- 介词搭配: She accomplished her academic goals by maintaining a strict study schedule throughout the semester.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 团队提前完成了项目。
- 她在担任主任期间取得了很大成就。
- 任务完成了收集土壤样本的主要目标。
- 我觉得今天没做成什么事。
- 团队通过周密的计划和有效的协作完成了这个具有挑战性的项目。
- 她通过整个学期保持严格的学习计划实现了学术目标。
</details>

### 14. accord <a id="14-accord"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *acorder* ("to agree, be in harmony"), from Latin *ad-* ("to") + *cor* ("heart"), literally "to bring heart to heart."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *acorder*（"同意，和谐"），源自拉丁语 *ad-*（"向"）+ *cor*（"心"），字面意为"心连心"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to give or grant; to be consistent or in harmony
- CN: 给予；授予；使一致

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Accord with" (intransitive) = to match/agree — "Accord with"（不及物）= 与…一致/相符
- "Accord sb sth" or "accord sth to sb" (transitive, formal) — "Accord sb sth" 或 "accord sth to sb"（及物，正式）
- Often used in formal/legal contexts — 常用于正式/法律语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- accord sb permission
- be accorded respect
- accord sb a right
- accord recognition


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 授予某人许可
- 受到尊重
- 授予某人权利
- 给予认可
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- accord with
- in accordance with
- of one's own accord
- accord status to
- fully accord with
- accord importance to
- accord with sth
- accord sth to sb
- accord sb sth
- accord in meaning
- accord by tradition


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 与…一致
- 按照；与…一致
- 出于自愿
- 授予…地位
- 完全符合
- 重视
- 与…一致
- 授予某人某物
- 授予某人某物
- 在意义上一致
- 按传统授予
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: His behavior does not accord with his principles. (CET-6 2021-06)
- 考研: The government accorded the refugees temporary protection. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The findings accord well with previous research in this field. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The committee accorded him the highest honor in recognition of his contributions. (PNAS 2020)
- Common: She left the company of her own accord; nobody forced her.
- 介词搭配: Modern educational systems accord great importance to the development of critical thinking skills.
- 介词搭配: The defendant's testimony does not accord with the security camera footage presented by the prosecution.
- 介词搭配: The university accords full scholarship status to students who achieve perfect scores on the entrance exam.
- 介词搭配: These two ancient manuscripts accord closely in meaning despite being written in different languages.
- 介词搭配: By tradition, the eldest son was accorded the right to inherit the family estate.
- 介词搭配: All experiments were conducted in accordance with the ethical guidelines approved by the university review board.
- 介词搭配: The suspect surrendered to the police of his own accord before the investigation had even officially begun.
- 介词搭配: His political views do not accord with the principles of democracy and equality.
- 介词搭配: The company's new environmental policy fully accords with international sustainability standards.
- 介词搭配: The experimental results accord with the theoretical predictions made by the research team.
- 介词搭配: The community accords its elders great respect and authority in local decision-making.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 他的行为与他的原则不一致。
- 政府给予难民临时保护。
- 这些发现与该领域的先前研究高度一致。
- 委员会授予他最高荣誉以表彰他的贡献。
- 她自愿离开了公司；没有人强迫她。
- 现代教育体系非常重视批判性思维能力的培养。
- 被告的证词与检方提供的监控录像不一致。
- 大学授予在入学考试中取得满分的学生全额奖学金。
- 这两份古代手稿尽管用不同语言写成，但意义高度一致。
- 按照传统，长子被授予继承家族财产的权利。
- 所有实验均按照大学审查委员会批准的伦理准则进行。
- 嫌疑人在调查正式开始前就主动向警方自首了。
- 他的政治观点与民主和平等的原则不一致。
- 公司的新环保政策完全符合国际可持续发展标准。
- 实验结果与研究团队所作的理论预测一致。
- 社区给予长辈在地方决策中极大的尊重和权威。
</details>

### 15. account <a id="15-account"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *acont* ("account, reckoning"), from *a-* ("to") + *conte* ("count, calculation"), from Latin *computāre* ("to calculate").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *acont*（"账目，计算"），源自 *a-*（"向"）+ *conte*（"计数，计算"），源自拉丁语 *computāre*（"计算"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to consider or regard; to give a report or explanation
- CN: 认为；解释；说明；占（比例）

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Account for" (intransitive phrasal verb) = explain, or constitute a proportion of — "Account for"（不及物短语动词）= 解释，或占（比例）- "Account sb/sth + adj" (transitive, formal): I account myself lucky. — "Account sb/sth + 形容词"（及物，正式）：I account myself lucky.
- Noun form is very common — 名词形式非常常见

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- account oneself happy/fortunate
- account sth a success


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 认为自己幸福/幸运
- 认为某事是成功的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- account for
- account for (percentage)
- be accounted as
- account for the difference
- account for the delay
- account for roughly/approximately
- account in full
- be accounted for
- account for sth
- account to sb
- account as sth
- account with a bank


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 解释；说明；占
- 占（百分比）
- 被视为
- 解释差异
- 解释延误
- 约占
- 全额说明
- 被解释/被说明
- 解释…；占…比例
- 向某人解释
- 被视为…
- 在银行有账户
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Exports account for nearly 40% of the country's GDP. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: How do you account for the sudden drop in sales? (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The professor's research accounts for the variations observed in the data. (TOEFL TPO 53)
- Academic: Renewable energy accounts for an increasing share of global electricity generation. (Nature Energy 2022)
- Common: I account myself lucky to have survived the accident.
- 介词搭配: The CEO must account to the shareholders for the company's declining profits this fiscal year.
- 介词搭配: His groundbreaking discovery is accounted as one of the most significant contributions to modern medicine.
- 介词搭配: Online sales now account for roughly thirty percent of the company's total annual revenue.
- 介词搭配: The treasurer was required to account in full for every expense incurred during the conference.
- 介词搭配: All missing items from the inventory must be accounted for before the annual audit.
- 介词搭配: The analyst struggled to account for the significant difference between projected and actual quarterly earnings.
- 介词搭配: The project manager was asked to account for the delay in the delivery of the critical software update.
- 介词搭配: The manager had to account for the significant budget overrun at the quarterly board meeting.
- 介词搭配: The manager had to account for the missing funds during the audit investigation.
- 介词搭配: International students account for nearly twenty percent of the university's total enrollment.
- 介词搭配: The technology sector accounts for roughly forty percent of the country's total economic output.
- 介词搭配: The company opened an account with a major international bank to facilitate overseas transactions.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 出口占该国GDP的近40%。
- 你如何解释销售额的突然下降？

- 可再生能源在全球发电中所占份额越来越大。
- 我认为自己在事故中幸存下来很幸运。
- CEO必须就公司本财年的利润下降向股东作出说明。
- 他的突破性发现被视为对现代医学最重要的贡献之一。
- 线上销售现在约占该公司年度总收入的30%。
- 财务主管被要求对会议期间产生的每一笔费用进行全额说明。
- 库存中所有丢失的物品都必须在年度审计前解释清楚。
- 分析师难以解释预计和实际季度收益之间的显著差异。
- 项目经理被要求解释关键软件更新交付延迟的原因。
- 经理必须在季度董事会上解释预算严重超支的原因。
- 经理必须在审计调查中对失踪的资金做出解释。
- 国际学生约占该大学总招生人数的20%。
- 科技行业约占该国经济总产出的40%。
- 该公司在一家大型国际银行开设了账户以方便海外交易。
</details>

### 16. accumulate <a id="16-accumulate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *accumulātus*, past participle of *accumulāre* ("to heap up, amass"), from *ad-* ("to") + *cumulus* ("a heap, mass").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *accumulātus*，*accumulāre*（"堆积，积聚"）的过去分词，源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *cumulus*（"堆，团"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to gather or collect over time; to increase in quantity
- CN: 积累；积聚；堆积

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Can be used transitively (accumulate wealth) or intransitively (dust accumulates) — 可以及物（积累财富）或不及物（灰尘积累）使用
- Implies gradual increase over time — 暗示随时间逐渐增加
- Often used in financial, scientific, and environmental contexts — 常用于金融、科学和环境语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- accumulate wealth/fortunes
- accumulate knowledge
- accumulate evidence
- accumulate debt
- accumulate experience
- accumulate data
- accumulate points
- rapidly accumulate
- accumulate capital


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 积累财富
- 积累知识
- 积累证据
- 积累债务
- 积累经验
- 积累数据
- 积累积分
- 迅速积累
- 积累资本
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- accumulate over time
- accumulate in sth
- accumulate through experience
- accumulate from sources
- accumulate within a system


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 随时间积累
- 在某处积累
- 通过经验积累
- 从来源积累
- 在系统内积累
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Over the years, he accumulated a vast collection of ancient coins. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: Evidence has been accumulating that links air pollution to cognitive decline. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Snow accumulates on the mountain peaks throughout the winter months. (TOEFL TPO 41)
- Academic: Microplastics accumulate in marine organisms and enter the food chain. (Science 2021)
- Common: If you don't pay your bills on time, late fees will accumulate quickly.
- 介词搭配: Harmful heavy metals can accumulate in the body's tissues over many years of exposure.
- 介词搭配: Practical wisdom is not acquired overnight but accumulated through years of real-world experience.
- 介词搭配: The finance department will accumulate data from various sources to generate the quarterly report.
- 介词搭配: Toxic waste can accumulate within a closed ecosystem and cause long-term environmental damage.
- 介词搭配: Harmful toxins can accumulate in the body over many years of exposure to polluted air and water.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 多年来，他积累了大量的古钱币收藏。
- 越来越多的证据将空气污染与认知能力下降联系起来。
- 整个冬季，雪在山顶上不断积累。
- 微塑料在海洋生物中积累并进入食物链。
- 如果不按时付账单，滞纳金会迅速累积。
- 有害重金属在多年接触后可在身体组织中积累。
- 实践智慧并非一蹴而就，而是通过多年的实际经验积累而来的。
- 财务部门将从各种来源收集数据以生成季度报告。
- 有毒废物会在封闭的生态系统中积累并造成长期环境破坏。
- 在多年接触污染的空气和水后，有害毒素可在体内积累。
</details>

### 17. accuse <a id="17-accuse"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *acuser* ("to accuse, indict"), from Latin *accūsāre* ("to call to account, accuse"), from *ad-* ("to") + *causa* ("cause, case, lawsuit").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *acuser*（"指控，起诉"），源自拉丁语 *accūsāre*（"传唤，指控"），源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *causa*（"原因，案件，诉讼"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to charge someone with a fault or crime
- CN: 指控；控告；指责

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Pattern: "accuse sb of (doing) sth" — 句型："accuse sb of (doing) sth"
- NOT "accuse sb to do sth" (common learner error) — 不是 "accuse sb to do sth"（常见学习者错误）
- Stronger than "blame" — 比 "blame" 语气更强

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- falsely accuse
- publicly accuse
- wrongly accuse
- accuse each other


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 诬告
- 公开指控
- 错误指控
- 互相指责
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- accuse sb of lying
- accuse sb of theft
- accuse sb of negligence
- accuse sb of murder
- accuse sb of fraud
- accuse sb of corruption
- accuse sb of sth
- accuse sb before the court
- accuse in public


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 指责某人撒谎
- 控告某人盗窃
- 指责某人疏忽
- 指控某人谋杀
- 指控某人欺诈
- 指控某人腐败
- 指控某人某事
- 在法庭上指控某人
- 公开指责
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: He was accused of cheating on the final exam. (CET-4 2020-09)
- 考研: The opposition party accused the government of corruption. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The manager was accused of discriminating against female employees. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: Several researchers were accused of manipulating data to support their conclusions. (Nature 2020)
- Common: Are you accusing me of stealing your phone?
- 介词搭配: The passengers accused the airline of negligence after the flight was delayed for over twelve hours without explanation.
- 介词搭配: The former CEO was accused before the court of embezzling company funds for personal use.
- 介词搭配: The opposition party accused the government of corruption during the parliamentary debate.

- 介词搭配: The prosecutor presented compelling DNA evidence to accuse the defendant of murder.
- 介词搭配: The prosecution will accuse the defendant of first-degree murder in the upcoming trial.
- 介词搭配: Several investors accused the financial advisor of fraud after losing their life savings.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">

- 检方将在即将进行的审判中指控被告犯有一级谋杀罪。

### 18. accustom <a id="18-accustom"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *acostumer* ("to become accustomed"), from *a-* ("to") + *costume* ("custom, habit"), from Latin *consuētūdō*.

**词源：** 源自古法语 *acostumer*（"使习惯"），源自 *a-*（"向"）+ *costume*（"习惯"），源自拉丁语 *consuētūdō*。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make someone familiar with or used to something
- CN: 使习惯；使适应

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Usually in passive or reflexive: "be accustomed to" or "accustom oneself to" — 通常用于被动或反身："be accustomed to" 或 "accustom oneself to"
- Followed by preposition "to" + noun/gerund — 后接介词 "to" + 名词/动名词
- "Accustomed to" = used to — "Accustomed to" = 习惯于

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- quickly become accustomed


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 很快适应
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be accustomed to sth
- become accustomed to doing
- accustom oneself to
- get accustomed to
- grow accustomed to
- be accustomed to the idea
- become accustomed to the climate
- accustom sb to
- well accustomed to
- accustom sb to sth
- be accustomed to sth
- become accustomed to
- accustom by practice


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 习惯于某事
- 变得习惯于做…

- 习惯于

- 习惯这个想法
- 适应气候
- 使某人习惯于
- 非常习惯于
- 使某人习惯于…
- 习惯于…
- 变得习惯于…
- 通过练习适应
</details>

- 介词搭配: Students must become accustomed to the rigorous academic standards of the university.
- 介词搭配: The experienced teacher accustomed the young children to a structured daily routine through consistent practice.
- 介词搭配: After living in Japan for over a decade, she is now fully accustomed to the local customs and traditions.

- 介词搭配: It took the tropical plants several months to become accustomed to the colder climate of the northern greenhouse.
- 介词搭配: After twenty years of teaching, Professor Wang is well accustomed to adapting his lectures to different student levels.
- 介词搭配: The pianist accustomed her fingers to the complex piece by practicing the difficult passages repeatedly.
</details>

### 19. ache <a id="19-ache"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old English *acan* ("to ache, suffer pain"), from Proto-Germanic *akaną*. Cognate with Dutch *achen* and German *achen*.

**词源：** 源自古英语 *acan*（"疼痛"），源自原始日耳曼语 *akaną*。与荷兰语 *achen* 和德语 *achen* 同源。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to feel a continuous dull pain; to yearn deeply
- CN: 疼痛；渴望

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Continuous/dull pain (vs. "pain" which is sharper, or "hurt" which is more general) — 持续性/隐痛（与更尖锐的 "pain" 或更一般的 "hurt" 相对）- "Ache for" = to yearn for someone/something — "Ache for" = 渴望某人/某物
- Can be used as a noun: headache, stomachache, toothache — 可用作名词：headache, stomachache, toothache

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- make sb's heart ache
- muscles ache
- ache badly
- feet ache
- still ache


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使某人心痛
- 肌肉酸痛
- 疼得厉害
- 脚疼
- 仍然疼痛
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- ache all over
- ache for sb/sth
- ache with fatigue
- ache with pain
- ache to do sth
- ache with pain/fatigue
- ache from exercise
- ache in one's muscles


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 全身疼痛
- 渴望某人/某物
- 因疲劳而疼痛
- 因疼痛而难受
- 渴望做某事
- 因疼痛/疲劳而难受
- 因锻炼而酸痛
- 肌肉酸痛
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: After the long hike, my legs were aching terribly. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: She ached for her homeland after years of living abroad. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: My head began to ache from the constant noise in the factory. (TOEFL TPO 39)
- Academic: Patients reported aching joints as the most common symptom. (The BMJ 2020)
- Common: I've been studying all day and my back really aches.
- 介词搭配: My entire body was aching from the strenuous workout at the gym yesterday.
- 介词搭配: Having been separated from his family for years due to work, he ached to return home and reunite with them.
- 介词搭配: After the marathon, his legs ached all over and he could barely walk.
- 介词搭配: The lonely old woman ached for her deceased husband even years after his passing.
- 介词搭配: His muscles ached from the intense training session at the gym the day before.
- 介词搭配: Having been away from home for years, the homesick soldier ached for his family and the familiar sights of his hometown.
- 介词搭配: After working double shifts for three consecutive days, every muscle in her body ached with fatigue.
- 介词搭配: He could feel a dull ache in his muscles as he climbed the final steep section of the mountain trail.
</details>

- 患者报告关节疼痛是最常见的症状。

- 我昨天在健身房进行了高强度锻炼，全身都在酸痛。

- 即使丈夫去世多年，这位孤独的老妇人仍然深深地思念着他。
- 他的肌肉因前一天在健身房的高强度训练而酸痛。
- 离家多年，思乡的士兵渴望见到家人和家乡熟悉的景象。
- 连续三天轮双班后，她身上的每一块肌肉都因疲劳而酸痛。
- 攀登山路最后一个陡峭路段时，他能感到肌肉隐隐作痛。
</details>

### 20. achieve <a id="20-achieve"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *achever* ("to finish, accomplish"), from *à chef* ("to a head, to a conclusion"), from Latin *caput* ("head").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *achever*（"完成，实现"），源自 *à chef*（"到头，到结论"），源自拉丁语 *caput*（"头"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to successfully reach or accomplish by effort
- CN: 实现；达到；取得

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Always used with an object (achieve success, achieve a goal) — 总是与宾语连用（achieve success, achieve a goal）
- Cannot be used intransitively (NOT "I achieved") — 不能不及物使用（不是 "I achieved"）
- Implies effort and skill, not luck — 暗示努力和技能，而非运气

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- achieve success
- achieve a goal
- achieve one's dreams
- achieve results
- achieve balance
- achieve an objective
- achieve a breakthrough
- achieve excellence
- achieve one's potential
- achieve a high score


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 获得成功
- 实现目标
- 实现某人的梦想
- 取得成果
- 达到平衡
- 实现目标
- 取得突破
- 追求卓越
- 发挥潜力
- 获得高分
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- achieve through hard work
- achieve in spite of
- achieve by means of
- achieve with ease


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 通过努力工作实现
- 尽管…仍实现
- 通过…方式实现
- 轻松实现
</details>

<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- 考研: The company achieved record profits this quarter. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Through years of training, he achieved an extraordinary level of skill. (TOEFL TPO 51)
- Academic: The researchers achieved a major breakthrough in cancer treatment. (Cell 2022)
- Common: You can achieve anything if you set your mind to it.
- 介词搭配: The company achieved record-breaking profits through aggressive marketing and cost-cutting measures.
- 介词搭配: The expedition team achieved their summit goal in spite of severe weather conditions and limited oxygen supply.
- 介词搭配: The company achieved significant cost reductions by means of automated production processes and supply chain optimization.
- 介词搭配: The talented musician achieved international recognition with ease at a remarkably young age.
</details>

- 尽管天气条件恶劣且氧气供应有限，探险队还是实现了登顶目标。
- 该公司通过自动化生产流程和供应链优化实现了显著的成本降低。

### 21. acknowledge <a id="21-acknowledge"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old English *oncnāwan* + later influence from Middle English *knowlechen*. Modern form from *ac-* (intensive) + *knowledge*.

**词源：** 源自古英语 *oncnāwan* + 后来的中古英语 *knowlechen* 的影响。现代形式源自 *ac-*（加强前缀）+ *knowledge*。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to accept or admit the existence/truth of; to recognize; to express gratitude
- CN: 承认；确认；致谢

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Can take a gerund: "acknowledge doing sth" — 可接动名词："acknowledge doing sth"
- Can take a that-clause — 可接 that 从句
- Formal register — 正式语体
- Common in academic writing (acknowledgments section) — 常见于学术写作（致谢部分）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- acknowledge a problem
- acknowledge one's mistake
- acknowledge contributions
- acknowledge the need
- acknowledge defeat
- openly acknowledge
- acknowledge a fact
- acknowledge the importance


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 承认问题
- 承认某人的错误
- 感谢贡献
- 承认必要性
- 承认失败
- 公开承认
- 承认事实
- 承认重要性
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- acknowledge receipt of
- widely acknowledged as
- acknowledge sb as sth
- acknowledge sb for sth
- acknowledge by the public
- acknowledge with gratitude
- acknowledge in a speech

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 感激地承认
- 在演讲中致谢
</details>

- 考研: He refused to acknowledge that he had made a mistake. (考研英语 2019)

### 22. acquaint <a id="22-acquaint"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *acointier* ("to make known, inform"), from Latin *accognitāre*, from *ad-* ("to") + *cognōscere* ("to know, recognize").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *acointier*（"使知道，告知"），源自拉丁语 *accognitāre*，源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *cognōscere*（"知道，认识"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make someone aware of or familiar with
- CN: 使熟悉；使了解；介绍

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Usually passive: "be acquainted with" — 通常被动："be acquainted with"- "Acquaint oneself with" = to familiarize oneself — "Acquaint oneself with" = 使自己熟悉
- "Acquaint sb with sth" = to introduce/inform — "Acquaint sb with sth" = 介绍/告知
- Common error: "acquaint with" without object — 常见错误：缺少宾语的 "acquaint with"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- get acquainted
- thoroughly acquaint
- be well acquainted
- become personally acquainted
- first acquaint


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 熟悉起来
- 充分了解
- 很熟悉
- 亲自认识
- 初次了解
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be acquainted with
- become acquainted with
- acquaint oneself with
- acquaint sb with the facts
- acquaint the reader with
- acquaint sb with sth
- be acquainted with sb/sth
- become acquainted with
- acquaint through mutual friends


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 熟悉；与…相识
- 开始熟悉
- 使自己熟悉
- 让某人了解事实
- 让读者了解
- 使某人熟悉…
- 熟悉…
- 变得熟悉…
- 通过共同朋友认识
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Are you acquainted with the new software system? (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The training program aims to acquaint new employees with company policies. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Visitors should acquaint themselves with the local laws before traveling. (TOEFL TPO 37)
- Academic: The researcher was well acquainted with the relevant literature. (Annual Review of Psychology 2021)
- Common: Let me acquaint you with the rules of the game.
- 介词搭配: Before making any investment decisions, you should thoroughly acquaint yourself with the potential risks and market conditions.
- 介词搭配: The two business partners first became acquainted through mutual colleagues at an industry conference in Singapore.

- 介词搭配: I am not personally acquainted with the author, but I have read all of his published works.
- 介词搭配: The lawyer took great care to acquaint the jury with the facts of the case before the trial began.
- 介词搭配: The first chapter of the textbook acquaints the reader with the fundamental concepts of economics.
- 介词搭配: The detective is well acquainted with the criminal's methods after investigating several similar cases.
- 介词搭配: We first became acquainted through mutual friends at a dinner party hosted by a colleague.
</details>

- 律师非常仔细地在审判开始前让陪审团了解案件事实。
- 教科书的第一章让读者了解经济学的基本概念。

### 23. acquire <a id="23-acquire"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *acquerre* ("to acquire, obtain"), from Latin *acquīrere* ("to get, obtain"), from *ad-* ("to") + *quaerere* ("to seek, obtain").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *acquerre*（"获得，得到"），源自拉丁语 *acquīrere*（"得到，获得"），源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *quaerere*（"寻找，获得"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to gain or obtain something through effort or skill
- CN: 获得；习得；购得

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- More formal than "get" or "obtain" — 比 "get" 或 "obtain" 更正式
- Implies gradual or sustained effort — 暗示逐渐或持续的努力
- Common in business (acquire a company), education (acquire knowledge), and linguistics (acquire a language) — 常见于商业（收购公司）、教育（获取知识）和语言学（习得语言）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- acquire knowledge
- acquire a skill
- acquire a language
- acquire a company
- acquire a reputation
- acquire wealth
- acquire property
- gradually acquire
- acquire experience


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 获取知识
- 习得技能
- 习得语言
- 收购公司
- 获得声誉
- 获得财富
- 购置房产
- 逐渐获得
- 获得经验
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- acquire a taste for
- acquire sth from sb
- acquire through purchase
- acquire by inheritance
- acquire over time
- acquire in bulk


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 培养对…的爱好
- 从某人处获得

- 批量获取
</details>

### 24. act <a id="24-act"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *āctus* ("a doing, driving"), past participle of *agere* ("to do, drive, lead"). Also influenced by Old French *acter*.

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *āctus*（"行动，驱动"），*agere*（"做，驱动，领导"）的过去分词。也受古法语 *acter* 的影响。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to do something; to behave in a certain way; to perform a role
- CN: 行动；表现；扮演；起作用

**Part of Speech:** vi. & vt. (intransitive & transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Intransitive: "act on/upon", "act as", "act like" — 不及物："act on/upon"、"act as"、"act like"
- Transitive: "act a role/part" (in theater/film) — 及物："act a role/part"（在戏剧/电影中）- "Act" + adj: act strange (informal), act responsibly (formal) — "Act" + 形容词：act strange（非正式），act responsibly（正式）
- "Act on" = take action based on information — "Act on" = 根据信息采取行动

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- act immediately
- act accordingly
- act responsibly


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 立即行动
- 相应行动
- 负责任地行事
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- act on/upon
- act as
- act like
- act out
- act up
- act in accordance with
- act as a catalyst
- act on/upon sth
- act as sth
- act for sb
- act against one's will
- act with caution


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 根据…行事
- 充当；担任
- 表现得像
- 将…表演出来；发泄
- 出故障；捣乱
- 按照…行事
- 起催化作用
- 根据…行动
- 充当…
- 代理某人
- 违背意愿行事
- 谨慎行事
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The government must act quickly to address the housing crisis. (CET-4 2022-12)

- TOEFL/IELTS: The medicine acts directly on the nervous system to relieve pain. (TOEFL TPO 40)

- 介词搭配: The police acted on an anonymous tip and discovered a large quantity of illegal drugs in the warehouse.
- 介词搭配: A local law firm was appointed to act for the defendant during the complex legal proceedings.

- 介词搭配: The witness testified that the defendant had acted against his own will under duress.
- 介词搭配: Investors should act with caution during periods of economic uncertainty.
- 介词搭配: The committee's recommendations are in accordance with international standards and best practices.
- 介词搭配: The politician was criticized for acting like a dictator when he bypassed the legislature to pass the controversial law.
- 介词搭配: Children often act out the stories they have read or heard through imaginative play.
- 介词搭配: The politician was severely criticized for acting like a dictator when he bypassed the legislature to pass the controversial law.
- 介词搭配: Young children often act out the stories they have heard through imaginative role-play and creative expression.
- 介词搭配: My computer started acting up again, repeatedly crashing whenever I tried to open the demanding graphics software.
- 介词搭配: The committee decided to act on the recommendations made by the environmental advisory board.
</details>

- 我的电脑又开始出故障了，每当我试图打开要求高的图形软件时就反复崩溃。

### 25. activate <a id="25-activate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *āctīvus* ("active") + *-ate* (verbal suffix), from *agere* ("to do, drive"). First recorded in English in the 1620s.

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *āctīvus*（"活跃的"）+ *-ate*（动词后缀），源自 *agere*（"做，驱动"）。英语中首次记录于17世纪20年代。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make something active or operational; to trigger
- CN: 激活；启动；使活动

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Common in technology (activate an account), science (activate a reaction), and military (activate a device) — 常见于科技（激活账户）、科学（激活反应）和军事（启动设备）
- Opposite: deactivate — 反义词：deactivate
- Passive is common: "The alarm is activated by motion" — 被动语态常见："The alarm is activated by motion"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- activate an account
- activate a device
- activate a mechanism
- activate a response
- activate the alarm
- activate a system
- activate a feature
- activate a switch
- automatically activate
- activate an option

- 激活系统
- 激活功能
- 启动开关
- 自动激活
- 激活选项
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- activate by sth
- activate through a process
- activate in response to
- activate at a temperature
- activate with a button

- 通过过程激活

- 用按钮激活
</details>

### 26. adapt <a id="26-adapt"></a>
**Etymology:** From French *adapter*, from Latin *adaptāre* ("to fit, adjust"), from *ad-* ("to") + *aptus* ("fitted, suited").

**词源：** 源自法语 *adapter*，源自拉丁语 *adaptāre*（"使适合，调整"），源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *aptus*（"适合的"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to adjust to new conditions; to modify for a new purpose
- CN: 适应；改编；调整

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Adapt to" (intransitive): adapt to change — "Adapt to"（不及物）：适应变化
- "Adapt for" (transitive): adapt a novel for film — "Adapt for"（及物）：改编小说为电影
- "Adapt from": adapted from a true story — "Adapt from"：根据真实故事改编
- Commonly used in biology (adaptation) and literature/film — 常用于生物学（适应）和文学/电影

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- adapt a method
- adapt accordingly
- quickly adapt
- adapt a strategy

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

</details>

- adapt oneself to

- adapt to circumstances

- adapt to sth
- adapt for sth
- adapt from sth
- adapt sth to sth
- adapt according to

- 使某物适应…
- 根据…调整
</details>

### 27. add <a id="27-add"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *addere* ("to add, give to"), from *ad-* ("to") + *dare* ("to give"). Entered English via Old French *adder*.

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *addere*（"添加，给予"），源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *dare*（"给"）。通过古法语 *adder* 进入英语。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to put something together with something else; to increase
- CN: 添加；增加；补充说

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. (transitive & intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Add A to B" (transitive) — "Add A to B"（及物）- "Add to" (intransitive): contributes to — "Add to"（不及物）：增加/增添
- "Add up" = make sense (colloquial) or calculate total — "Add up" = 合乎情理（口语）或加起来
- Very common, basic verb — 非常常见的基本动词

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- add value
- add together

- 加在一起
</details>

- add weight to

- add sth to sth
- add in sth

</details>

- 介词搭配: Please add the milk to the flour gradually while stirring the mixture continuously.

### 28. addict <a id="28-addict"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *addictus* ("delivered, devoted, surrendered"), past participle of *addīcere* ("to give over, deliver"), from *ad-* ("to") + *dīcere* ("to say, declare").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *addictus*（"交付的，奉献的，投降的"），*addīcere*（"交出，交付"）的过去分词，源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *dīcere*（"说，宣布"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to cause someone to become physiologically or psychologically dependent on something
- CN: 使上瘾；使沉迷

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Almost always used in passive: "be/become addicted to" — "Add A to B"（及物）
- Noun forms: addict (person), addiction (condition) — "Add to"（不及物）：增加/增添
- "Addict oneself to" is rare; prefer "be addicted to" — "Add up" = 合乎情理（口语）或加起来

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- a drug addict
- addiction treatment
- add together
- get addicted
- a gambling addict
- treat addiction


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 吸毒成瘾者
- 成瘾治疗
- 加在一起
- 上瘾
- 赌瘾者
- 治疗成瘾
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be addicted to drugs/alcohol
- be addicted to gaming
- become addicted to
- add A to B
- add weight to
- add to the confusion
- add in
- be addicted to gambling
- be addicted to social media
- be addicted to sth
- become addicted to
- addict oneself to
- addicted from a young age


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 对毒品/酒精上瘾
- 沉迷游戏
- 对…上瘾
- 把A加到B中
- 增加…的分量
- 增加混乱
- 加入
- 沉迷赌博
- 沉迷社交媒体
- 对…上瘾
- 变得对…上瘾
- 使自己沉迷于…
- 从小上瘾
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Many teenagers become addicted to online games and neglect their studies. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: He was so addicted to social media that he couldn't focus on his work. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The documentary explored how people become addicted to prescription painkillers. (TOEFL TPO 52)
- Academic: Addiction is a complex brain disorder characterized by compulsive substance use. (Nature Reviews Neuroscience 2021)
- Common: I'm addicted to this TV series — I can't stop watching!
- 介词搭配: Many teenagers today are addicted to social media platforms and spend excessive amounts of time scrolling through content.
- 介词搭配: He started smoking cigarettes in middle school and was addicted from a young age, making it extremely difficult to quit later.
- 介词搭配: Many young people who experiment with drugs can quickly become addicted to them if proper intervention is not provided.
- 介词搭配: It is dangerous to addict oneself to any substance that alters one's mental state.
- 介词搭配: The teenager was so addicted to gaming that he often stayed up all night playing online games.
- 介词搭配: Many people find themselves addicted to their smartphones, checking notifications constantly throughout the day.
- 介词搭配: He entered a rehabilitation center after realizing he was addicted to alcohol and needed professional help.
- 介词搭配: The retired banker lost his entire life savings because he was addicted to gambling at the casino.
- 介词搭配: The recipe instructs you to add the eggs to the butter and sugar mixture.
- 介词搭配: The newly discovered fossil adds weight to the theory that birds evolved from dinosaurs.
- 介词搭配: The contradictory statements from government officials only added to the confusion among the public.
- 介词搭配: After the vegetables are cooked, add in the chopped herbs and stir well.
- 介词搭配: Many people who start smoking socially can quickly become addicted to nicotine.
</details>

- 他沉迷社交媒体，无法集中精力工作。
- 这部纪录片探讨了人们如何对处方止痛药上瘾。
- 成瘾是一种以强迫性物质使用为特征的复杂脑部疾病。
- 我对这部电视剧上瘾了——我停不下来！

- 沉迷于任何改变精神状态的东西都是危险的。

- 这位退休银行家因沉迷赌场赌博而失去了毕生积蓄。
- 食谱指示你将鸡蛋加入黄油和糖的混合物中。

### 29. address <a id="29-address"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *adrecier* ("to direct, aim"), from Latin *addīrēctiāre*, from *ad-* ("to") + *dīrēctus* ("straight, direct").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *adrecier*（"引导，瞄准"），源自拉丁语 *addīrēctiāre*，源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *dīrēctus*（"直的，直接的"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to speak to; to deal with or attend to; to write directions on an envelope
- CN: 向…讲话；处理；写地址；称呼

**Part of Speech:** vt. (transitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- "Address a problem/issue" = to deal with it — "Address a problem/issue" = 处理问题
- "Address sb" = to speak to someone formally — "Address sb" = 正式对某人讲话
- "Address sb as + title": address the judge as "Your Honor" — "Address sb as + 头衔"：address the judge as "Your Honor"
- Common in formal/business contexts — 常见于正式/商业语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- address a problem/issue
- address a meeting/crowd
- address concerns
- address the root cause
- address a question
- directly address
- address a topic


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 处理问题
- 向会议/人群发表讲话
- 处理关切
- 处理根本原因
- 回答问题
- 直接处理/直接对…讲话
- 讨论话题
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- address a letter to
- address sb as
- address the needs of
- address sth to sb
- address sb as sth
- address a letter to
- address in public
- address by name


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 把信寄给
- 称呼某人为
- 满足…的需求
- 向某人表达…
- 称呼某人为…
- 把信寄给…
- 公开发表讲话
- 直呼其名
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The president will address the nation tonight at 8 p.m. (CET-4 2022-06)
- 考研: The government has failed to address the root causes of poverty. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The report addresses several key issues facing the education system. (TOEFL TPO 49)
- Academic: This paper addresses the gap in the literature regarding adolescent mental health. (The Lancet Psychiatry 2022)
- Common: Please address the envelope clearly so the mail gets delivered correctly.
- 介词搭配: The new housing policy is designed to address the needs of low-income families struggling with rising rents.
- 介词搭配: In academic settings, students should address their professors by their appropriate titles rather than first names.
- 介词搭配: Please address the package to the head office in New York.
- 介词搭配: Students should address their teachers as "Professor" followed by their last name as a sign of respect.
- 介词搭配: The CEO addressed the concerns of the shareholders in a public statement released this morning.
- 介词搭配: In formal correspondence, one should address the recipient by their appropriate title rather than by their first name.
- 介词搭配: The controversial author will address the public for the first time at a book signing event next week.
- 介词搭配: The minister addressed his remarks to the young students, encouraging them to pursue their dreams.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 总统将于今晚8点向全国发表讲话。
- 政府未能解决贫困的根本原因。
- 报告讨论了教育系统面临的几个关键问题。
- 本文填补了青少年心理健康方面的文献空白。
- 请在信封上写清楚地址，以便邮件正确送达。
- 新住房政策旨在满足因租金上涨而陷入困境的低收入家庭的需求。
- 在学术场合，学生应通过合适的头衔称呼教授，而不是直呼其名。
- 请把包裹寄往纽约的总部。
- 学生应称呼老师为"教授"加姓氏，以示尊重。
- CEO在今天上午发布的公开声明中回应了股东的关切。
- 在正式信函中，应当用适当的头衔称呼收件人，而不是直呼其名。
- 这位有争议的作者将在下周的签书会上首次公开发表讲话。
- 部长向年轻学生们发表了讲话，鼓励他们追求梦想。
</details>

### 30. adhere <a id="30-adhere"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *adhærēre* ("to stick to"), from *ad-* ("to") + *hærēre* ("to stick, cling").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *adhærēre*（"黏附"），源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *hærēre*（"粘住，附着"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to stick firmly to; to follow or obey (rules, principles)
- CN: 黏附；遵守；坚持

**Part of Speech:** vi. (intransitive)

**Usage Notes:**

- Always followed by "to": adhere to a surface, adhere to rules — 总是后接 "to"：adhere to a surface, adhere to rules
- More formal than "stick to" — 比 "stick to" 更正式
- "Adherence" (noun) is common in formal writing — "Adherence"（名词）在正式写作中常见
- Not used transitively (NOT "adhere the paper") — 不能及物使用（不是 "adhere the paper"）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- firmly adhere


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 牢固黏附
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- adhere to the rules
- adhere to a principle
- adhere to a schedule
- adhere to guidelines
- adhere to a surface
- strictly adhere to
- adhere to a standard
- adhere to a policy
- adhere to the terms
- adhere to sth
- adhere strictly to
- adhere closely to
- adhere with glue


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 遵守规则
- 坚持原则
- 遵守时间表
- 遵循指导方针
- 黏附在表面上
- 严格遵守
- 遵守标准
- 遵守政策
- 遵守条款
- 黏附在…上；遵守…
- 严格遵守…
- 紧密遵守…
- 用胶水黏附
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: All participants must adhere to the safety regulations. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The company has always adhered to the highest ethical standards. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The glue ensures that the tiles adhere firmly to the wall. (TOEFL TPO 41)
- Academic: The experiment was conducted in strict adherence to the established protocol. (Nature Protocols 2021)
- Common: If you don't adhere to the diet plan, you won't see results.
- 介词搭配: The wallpaper must be pressed firmly against the wall to adhere to the surface without peeling.
- 介词搭配: All construction companies must strictly adhere to the safety regulations established by the government.
- 介词搭配: The glue must be applied evenly for the label to adhere closely to the surface of the package.
- 介词搭配: All participants in the competition must strictly adhere to the rules set by the organizing committee.
- 介词搭配: Throughout his career, the judge has always adhered to the principle that everyone is equal before the law.
- 介词搭配: The construction crew worked overtime to adhere to the tight schedule and complete the project on time.
- 介词搭配: Researchers must strictly adhere to the ethical guidelines when conducting experiments involving human subjects.
- 介词搭配: The manufacturer must adhere to the international quality standards in order to export its products abroad.
- 介词搭配: All employees are required to adhere to the company's anti-discrimination policy in the workplace.
- 介词搭配: The sticker will not adhere to a wet or dusty surface, so make sure it is clean and dry first.
- 介词搭配: All contractors must adhere strictly to the building codes and safety requirements defined by local authorities.
- 介词搭配: The film adaptation adheres closely to the original novel, preserving even the most subtle details.
- 介词搭配: The decorative trim can be adhered with glue to the edge of the wooden shelf.
</details>

- 如果你不遵守饮食计划，就不会看到效果。

- 所有建筑公司必须严格遵守政府制定的安全规定。
- 胶水必须均匀涂抹，标签才能紧密地黏附在包装表面。
- 所有参赛者必须严格遵守组委会制定的规则。
- 在整个职业生涯中，这位法官始终坚持法律面前人人平等的原则。
- 建筑队加班工作以遵守紧凑的日程安排，按时完成项目。
- 研究人员在进行涉及人类受试者的实验时必须严格遵守伦理准则。

- 所有员工都必须在工作场所遵守公司的反歧视政策。

- 所有承包商必须严格遵守地方当局定义的建筑规范和安全要求。

- 装饰条可以用胶水黏附在木架边缘。
