### 31. adjust <a id="31-adjust"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *ad-* "to" + *iuxta* "near," later via Old French *ajuster* "to approximate."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *ad-* "向" + *iuxta* "附近"，后通过古法语 *ajuster* "使近似"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to change slightly to make something fit, work better, or be more suitable
- CN: 调整；调节；适应

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Used transitively with an object: "adjust the settings" — 及物使用："adjust the settings"
- Used intransitively with "to": "adjust to the new environment" — 不及物与 "to" 连用："adjust to the new environment"
- Often used in technical and everyday contexts — 常用于技术和日常语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- adjust accordingly
- finely adjust
- adjust the settings
- adjust one's attitude
- adjust a plan
- adjust the price
- easily adjust
- adjust a schedule


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 相应调整
- 微调
- 调整设置
- 调整态度
- 调整计划
- 调整价格
- 容易调整
- 调整日程
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- adjust to
- adjust to new circumstances
- adjust to sth
- adjust for sth
- adjust according to
- adjust in response to
- adjust by doing


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 适应
- 适应新情况
- 适应…
- 为…而调整
- 根据…调整
- 响应…而调整
- 通过做…来调整
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: You need to adjust the microscope to get a clearer image. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: Immigrants must adjust to a new culture and language. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The body can gradually adjust to higher altitudes. (TOEFL TPO 31)
- Academic: Organisms must adjust their metabolic rates in response to climate change. (Nature 2019)
- Common: Please adjust your seat to an upright position before takeoff.
- 介词搭配: The economic data has been adjusted for seasonal variations to provide a more accurate picture of the trend.
- 介词搭配: The dosage of this medication should be adjusted according to the patient's weight and age to avoid adverse effects.
- 介词搭配: It took the immigrants several months to adjust to the new cultural environment and social norms.
- 介词搭配: The company adjusted its production schedule in response to the sudden increase in market demand.
- 介词搭配: The driver can adjust the seat height by pulling the lever located beneath the seat.
- 介词搭配: Immigrants often face significant challenges as they struggle to adjust to a new language, culture, and social environment.
- 介词搭配: Refugees often struggle to adjust to completely new circumstances when they settle in a foreign country.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 你需要调整显微镜以获得更清晰的图像。
- 移民必须适应新的文化和语言。
- 身体可以逐渐适应高海拔。
- 生物体必须调整其代谢率以应对气候变化。
- 起飞前请将座椅调整到直立位置。

- 该药物的剂量应根据患者的体重和年龄进行调整，以避免不良反应。

- 难民在外国定居时常常难以适应全新的环境。
</details>

### 32. administer <a id="32-administer"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *administrare*, from *ad-* "to" + *ministrare* "to serve."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *administrare*，源自 *ad-* "向" + *ministrare* "服务"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to manage or supervise the execution of something; to give or dispense (medicine, justice)
- CN: 管理；执行；给予（药物、司法等）

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Typically transitive, taking an object such as "a company," "medicine," "an exam," or "justice" — 通常及物，宾语可以是 "a company"、"medicine"、"an exam" 或 "justice"
- More formal than "manage" or "run" — 比 "manage" 或 "run" 更正式

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- administer a test/exam
- administer medicine/drugs
- administer justice
- administer a program
- administer first aid
- administer a company
- administer the law
- administer a fund
- administer treatment
- administer an oath


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 组织考试
- 给药
- 执行司法
- 管理项目
- 实施急救
- 管理公司
- 执行法律
- 管理基金
- 给予治疗
- 主持宣誓
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- administer to sb
- administer sth to sb
- administer as a treatment
- administer by injection
- administer under supervision


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 给予某人治疗/照顾
- 把某物给予某人
- 作为治疗施用
- 通过注射给药
- 在监督下管理
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The nurse will administer the vaccine in the upper arm. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: It is the court's duty to administer justice fairly. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The university hired a new official to administer the scholarship program. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The drug was administered intravenously to all participants. (The Lancet 2022)
- Common: Teachers administer final exams at the end of each semester.
- 介词搭配: Volunteer doctors traveled to the remote village to administer to the medical needs of the local residents.
- 介词搭配: The life-saving drug must be administered by injection under strict medical supervision.
- 介词搭配: The nurse carefully administered the vaccine to each child in the clinic.
- 介词搭配: This herbal remedy has been traditionally administered as a treatment for digestive disorders.
- 介词搭配: The experimental drug must be administered under the supervision of a licensed physician at all times.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 护士将在上臂接种疫苗。
- 公平执法是法院的职责。
- 大学聘请了一位新官员来管理奖学金项目。
- 该药物通过静脉注射给所有参与者。
- 教师在每学期末组织期末考试。
- 志愿医生前往偏远村庄为当地居民提供医疗服务。
- 这种救命药物必须在严格的医疗监督下通过注射给药。
- 护士仔细地为诊所里的每个孩子接种了疫苗。
- 这种草药疗法传统上被用作消化系统疾病的治疗手段。
- 这种实验性药物必须始终在有执照的医生的监督下施用。
</details>

### 33. administrate <a id="33-administrate"></a>
**Etymology:** Back-formation from "administration," modeled after Latin *administratus*; emerged in English in the 19th century.

**词源：** 是"administration"的逆构词，模仿拉丁语 *administratus*；于19世纪出现在英语中。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to manage or direct the affairs of (a business, institution, etc.)
- CN: 管理；掌管

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Considered less formal and sometimes redundant compared to "administer" — 与 "administer" 相比不太正式，有时被认为是冗余的
- Commonly used in business and corporate contexts — 常用于商业和公司语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- administrate a department
- administrate a database
- administrate the system
- administrate funds
- administrate operations
- administrate a project
- administrate a network
- administrate resources
- administrate a program
- administrate effectively


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 管理部门
- 管理数据库
- 管理系统
- 管理资金
- 管理运营
- 管理项目
- 管理网络
- 管理资源
- 管理程序
- 有效管理
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- administrate for an organization
- administrate across departments
- administrate through a system
- administrate with efficiency


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 为组织管理
- 跨部门管理
- 通过系统管理
- 高效管理
</details>

<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- 考研: The committee was formed to administrate the research grants. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The IT specialist will administrate the network servers. (TOEFL TPO 51)
- Academic: A central body was needed to administrate the growing university system. (Higher Education Quarterly 2020)
- Common: She was hired to administrate the new project from start to finish.
- 介词搭配: His primary responsibility is to administrate across various departments to ensure smooth interdepartmental communication.
- 介词搭配: The newly appointed director aims to administrate the company's resources with maximum efficiency and transparency.
- 介词搭配: She was hired to administrate for a non-profit organization dedicated to environmental conservation.
- 介词搭配: The network administrator can administrate user access through a centralized security system.
</details>

- 新任命的主任旨在以最大效率和透明度管理公司资源。
- 她被雇来为一家致力于环境保护的非营利组织做管理工作。

### 34. admire <a id="34-admire"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *admirari*, from *ad-* "at" + *mirari* "to wonder."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *admirari*，源自 *ad-* "在" + *mirari* "惊讶"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to regard with respect, approval, or warm approval; to look at with pleasure
- CN: 钦佩；赞赏；欣赏

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Cannot be followed by a *that*-clause; use "admire someone for something" instead — 不能接 that 从句；要用 "admire someone for something"
- Stronger than "like" but less intense than "adore" or "worship" — 比 "like" 更强，但不如 "adore" 或 "worship" 强烈

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- admire the view
- greatly/deeply admire
- admire one's work
- admire the courage
- admire the beauty
- truly admire
- admire a performance
- publicly admire

- 钦佩勇气
- 欣赏美景
- 真正钦佩
- 欣赏表演
- 公开赞赏
</details>

- admire someone for something
- admire from afar
- admire sb for sth
- admire from a distance

### 35. admit <a id="35-admit"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *admittere*, from *ad-* "to" + *mittere* "to send."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *admittere*，源自 *ad-* "向" + *mittere* "发送"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to confess or acknowledge something; to allow someone to enter a place or organization
- CN: 承认；允许进入；接纳

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Transitive with a direct object: "admit the mistake" — 及物："admit the mistake"
- Can be followed by a gerund: "admit doing something" (NOT "admit to do") — 可接动名词："admit doing something"（不是 "admit to do"）- "Admit to" (intransitive) is also acceptable: "admit to the crime" — "Admit to"（不及物）也可接受："admit to the crime"
- "Admit into/to" for entry: "admitted to the university" — "Admit into/to" 表示进入："admitted to the university"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- admit defeat
- admit a mistake
- admit guilt
- admit liability
- admit wrongdoing
- openly admit
- admit a patient


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 承认失败
- 承认错误
- 认罪
- 承认责任
- 承认不当行为
- 公开承认
- 收治病人
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- admit someone to the hospital
- admit into a school
- admit to a crime
- admit to sth
- admit sb to/into sth
- admit as a member
- admit with regret
- admit into evidence


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 收治某人入院
- 录取入学
- 认罪
- 承认…
- 允许某人进入/加入…
- 接纳为成员
- 遗憾地承认
- 被采纳为证据
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: He finally admitted that he had made an error. (CET-4 2022-12)
- 考研: Few people will readily admit to having prejudices. (考研英语 2016)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Patients were admitted to the clinic for observation. (TOEFL TPO 38)
- Academic: The court admitted the evidence after careful review. (Harvard Law Review 2021)
- Common: I must admit I was wrong about the outcome.
- 介词搭配: She was admitted into the prestigious law school after achieving exceptional scores on the entrance examination.
- 介词搭配: The exclusive country club admitted the famous businessman as a member after a thorough background check.
- 介词搭配: The court admitted the DNA evidence into evidence after verifying its authenticity.
- 介词搭配: The hospital admitted the patient with regret as all beds were fully occupied.
- 介词搭配: The paramedics rushed to admit the accident victim to the nearest hospital for emergency surgery.
- 介词搭配: After hours of interrogation, the suspect finally admitted to the crime under pressure.
- 介词搭配: The politician admitted to making false statements during the election campaign.
- 介词搭配: The prestigious university admits only the most qualified students into its medical program each year.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 很少有人会轻易承认自己有偏见。
- 患者被收入诊所进行观察。
- 法院在仔细审查后采纳了该证据。
- 我必须承认我对结果的判断是错的。
- 她在入学考试中取得了优异成绩后被这所著名的法学院录取。
- 这家高端乡村俱乐部在经过彻底背景调查后接纳了这位著名商人为会员。

- 经过数小时的审讯，嫌疑人在压力下终于认罪。
- 这位政治人物承认在竞选期间做出了虚假陈述。
- 这所著名的大学每年只录取最优秀的学生进入其医学项目。
</details>

### 36. adopt <a id="36-adopt"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *adoptare*, from *ad-* "to" + *optare* "to choose."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *adoptare*，源自 *ad-* "向" + *optare* "选择"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to legally take another's child as one's own; to choose and begin to use something
- CN: 收养；采用；采纳

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Not to be confused with "adapt" (to adjust) or "adept" (skillful) — 不要与 "adapt"（适应）或 "adept"（熟练的）混淆
- Used for both children and practices/ideas/technologies — 用于孩子以及实践/想法/技术

**General Collocations (English):**

### 37. adore <a id="37-adore"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *adorare*, from *ad-* "to" + *orare* "to speak, pray."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *adorare*，源自 *ad-* "向" + *orare* "说话，祈祷"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to love and respect deeply; to like very much
- CN: 崇拜；爱慕；非常喜欢

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Stronger than "like" or "admire"; implies deep affection — 比 "like" 或 "admire" 更强；暗示深厚的感情
- Often used informally to mean "like a lot": "I adore this song" — 常用于非正式场合，意为"非常喜欢"："I adore this song"
- Do not use with *that*-clause; use "adore doing" or "adore someone" — 不要接 that 从句；用 "adore doing" 或 "adore someone"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- absolutely adore
- adore someone/something
- adore doing something
- adore the way...
- adore children
- truly adore
- adore animals
- simply adore

- 喜爱…的方式
- 喜爱孩子
- 真心喜爱
- 喜爱动物
- 就是喜爱
</details>

- 喜爱胜过一切
</details>

### 38. advance <a id="38-advance"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *avancer*, from Latin *abante* "before."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *avancer*，源自拉丁语 *abante* "在前面"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to move forward; to make progress; to promote or propose
- CN: 前进；推进；提升；提出

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Both transitive ("advance a cause") and intransitive ("technology advances") — 既可及物（"advance a cause"）也可不及物（"technology advances"）
- "In advance of" means before something happens — "In advance of" 意为在…之前

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- advance a career
- advance a proposal
- advance one's knowledge
- advance an argument
- advance a theory
- advance rapidly
- advance one's interests


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 职业发展
- 提出建议
- 增进知识
- 提出论点
- 提出理论
- 迅速进步
- 增进利益
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- advance in technology
- in advance
- advance the cause of
- advance in sth
- advance on/toward sth
- advance to the next stage
- advance beyond expectations
- advance through the ranks


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 技术进步
- 提前
- 推进…的事业
- 在…方面进步
- 向…前进
- 进入下一阶段
- 超出预期地进步
- 逐级晋升
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Technology has advanced rapidly over the past decade. (CET-4 2022-06)
- 考研: The government advanced a new plan to reduce pollution. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The researcher advanced a novel theory about memory formation. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: CRISPR technology has advanced gene-editing capabilities significantly. (Nature 2020)
- Common: Please let us know in advance if you cannot attend.
- 介词搭配: Winning this preliminary match will allow the team to advance to the next stage of the national championship.
- 介词搭配: She joined the company as an intern and gradually advanced through the ranks to become vice president within a decade.
- 介词搭配: Medical technology has advanced significantly in recent years, improving patient outcomes worldwide.
- 介词搭配: The army advanced on the enemy's position under the cover of darkness.
- 介词搭配: The student advanced beyond the expectations of her teachers and graduated at the top of her class.
- 介词搭配: You should book your flight tickets well in advance to get the best prices during the holiday season.
- 介词搭配: The organization works tirelessly to advance the cause of human rights in developing countries.
- 介词搭配: The young scientist has advanced significantly in her research on renewable energy storage systems.

### 39. advertise <a id="39-advertise"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *advertere* "to turn toward," via Old French *avertir*; shifted meaning to "give notice" in the 15th century.

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *advertere* "转向"，经过古法语 *avertir*；在15世纪含义转变为"通知"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to promote products, services, or events publicly to attract customers
- CN: 做广告；宣传；公布

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Transitive: "advertise a product" — 及物："advertise a product"
- Intransitive: "advertise for a new employee" — 不及物："advertise for a new employee"
- British spelling: "advertise" (same); noun: "advertisement" — 英式拼写相同；名词："advertisement"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- advertise a product/service
- advertise online
- heavily advertise
- advertise a job
- advertise widely
- advertise an event


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 为产品/服务做广告
- 在线做广告
- 大量宣传
- 发布招聘广告
- 广泛宣传
- 宣传活动
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- advertise for staff
- advertise on TV
- advertise through media
- advertise in newspapers
- advertise for sb
- advertise sth as sth
- advertise to sb
- advertise on TV/in newspapers


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 招聘员工
- 在电视上做广告
- 通过媒体做广告
- 在报纸上做广告
- 招聘…
- 宣传某物为…
- 向…做广告
- 在电视/报纸上做广告
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Companies advertise heavily during the holiday season. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The company advertised the job vacancy on its website. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Social media platforms allow businesses to advertise to targeted audiences. (TOEFL TPO 54)
- Academic: The study examined how junk food is advertised to children. (American Journal of Public Health 2021)
- Common: We need to advertise the event to attract more participants.
- 介词搭配: The company is advertising its new smartphone as the most powerful device ever created in its price range.
- 介词搭配: The tech startup is actively advertising for experienced software engineers with a background in artificial intelligence.
- 介词搭配: The company advertised its new line of eco-friendly products on major television networks during prime time.
- 介词搭配: The health organization launched a campaign to advertise the dangers of smoking to young people through social media platforms.
- 介词搭配: The company aggressively advertises its latest smartphone as the most powerful and innovative device in its price range.
- 介词搭配: Many local businesses still prefer to advertise in newspapers to reach older residents who read print media daily.
- 介词搭配: The nonprofit organization launched a campaign to advertise through media outlets across the country.
- 介词搭配: The company uses targeted social media ads to advertise its products to young professionals aged 25 to 35.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 公司在假日季节大量投放广告。
- 公司在网站上发布了职位空缺广告。
- 社交媒体平台允许企业向目标受众投放广告。
- 该研究考察了垃圾食品是如何向儿童做广告的。
- 我们需要宣传这个活动以吸引更多参与者。
- 该公司将其新智能手机宣传为其价格范围内有史以来最强大的设备。
- 这家科技初创公司正在积极招聘有人工智能背景的经验丰富的软件工程师。
- 该公司在主要电视网络的黄金时段为其新的环保产品线做广告。
- 该健康组织发起了一场活动，通过社交媒体平台向年轻人宣传吸烟的危害。
- 该公司极力将其最新款智能手机宣传为其价格范围内最强大、最具创新性的设备。
- 许多本地企业仍喜欢在报纸上做广告以覆盖每天阅读印刷媒体的老年居民。
- 这家非营利组织发起了一项活动，通过全国各地的媒体渠道进行宣传。
- 该公司使用定向社交媒体广告向25至35岁的年轻专业人士宣传其产品。

### 40. advise <a id="40-advise"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *advisare*, from *ad-* "to" + *visere* "to view."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *advisare*，源自 *ad-* "向" + *visere* "查看"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to offer suggestions or recommendations about what someone should do
- CN: 建议；劝告；通知

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Verb: advise (spelled with -s-) vs. noun: advice (spelled with -c-) — 动词 advise 与名词 advice 的区别
- Common pattern: "advise someone to do something" (NOT "advise someone do") — 常用句型："advise someone to do something"
- Also: "advise against," "advise on/about" — 也用于 "advise against," "advise on/about"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- strongly advise
- advise caution
- advise the government
- advise clients
- legally advise
- advise accordingly


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 强烈建议
- 建议谨慎
- 向政府提供建议
- 为客户提供咨询
- 提供法律建议
- 相应建议
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- advise someone to do something
- advise against
- advise on/about
- advise a course of action
- advise sb of sth
- advise sb on/about sth
- advise against sth
- advise in favor of
- advise with caution


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 建议某人做某事
- 建议不要
- 就…提供建议
- 建议行动方案
- 通知某人某事
- 就…给某人建议
- 建议不要…
- 建议支持…
- 谨慎建议
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The doctor advised him to get more exercise. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: I would advise against making hasty decisions. (考研英语 2017)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The counselor advised the students on choosing a major. (TOEFL TPO 39)
- Academic: We advise that all participants sign the consent form. (British Medical Journal 2022)
- Common: Please advise us of any changes to your schedule.
- 介词搭配: The lawyer advised the client of his legal rights before the court proceedings began.
- 介词搭配: The financial analyst advised in favor of investing in renewable energy stocks for long-term growth.
- 介词搭配: The consultant advised the company on how to improve its supply chain efficiency.
- 介词搭配: The doctor advised against strenuous exercise until the patient fully recovered.
- 介词搭配: I would advise with caution when dealing with such complex legal matters.
- 介词搭配: The financial advisor strongly advised her clients to diversify their investment portfolios to minimize risk.
- 介词搭配: The financial advisor strongly advised her clients to diversify their investment portfolios in order to minimize risk.
- 介词搭配: The financial consultant advised a course of action that involved restructuring the company's debt.
- 介词搭配: The expert was invited to advise on the best strategies for reducing energy consumption in the building.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 我建议不要仓促做决定。
- 顾问就选择专业给学生提供建议。

- 请告知我们你日程的任何变化。
- 律师在法庭程序开始前告知了客户他的法律权利。
- 这位金融分析师建议支持投资可再生能源股票以获取长期增长。

- 财务顾问强烈建议她的客户分散投资组合以最小化风险。
- 财务顾问强烈建议她的客户分散投资组合以最小化风险。
- 财务顾问建议了一项涉及重组公司债务的行动方案。

### 41. advocate <a id="41-advocate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *advocatus*, from *ad-* "to" + *vocare* "to call."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *advocatus*，源自 *ad-* "向" + *vocare* "召唤"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to publicly recommend or support a particular cause, policy, or action
- CN: 提倡；主张；拥护

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Intransitive with "for": "advocate for change" — 不及物与 "for" 连用："advocate for change"
- Transitive with a noun or gerund: "advocate reform," "advocate doing something" — 及物后接名词或动名词
- Can also take a *that*-clause: "advocate that the policy be changed" — 也可接 that 从句

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- advocate change/reform
- strongly advocate
- advocate doing something
- advocate a policy
- openly advocate
- actively advocate


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 倡导变革/改革
- 强烈主张
- 主张做某事
- 主张一项政策
- 公开倡导
- 积极倡导
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- advocate for
- advocate on behalf of
- advocate the use of
- advocate for the rights of
- advocate for sth
- advocate as a solution
- advocate in public
- advocate with conviction


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 提倡；支持
- 代表…倡导
- 提倡使用
- 倡导…的权利
- 倡导…
- 提倡作为解决方案
- 公开倡导
- 坚定地倡导
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Many environmentalists advocate the use of renewable energy. (CET-4 2022-12)
- 考研: Some economists advocate lower taxes to stimulate growth. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The organization advocates for the rights of indigenous peoples. (TOEFL TPO 43)
- Academic: The authors advocate a patient-centered approach to healthcare. (The New England Journal of Medicine 2021)
- Common: She advocates for better mental health services in schools.
- 介词搭配: The well-known human rights lawyer regularly advocates on behalf of refugees seeking asylum in the country.
- 介词搭配: Many educators advocate project-based learning as a solution to the growing problem of student disengagement in high schools.
- 介词搭配: The non-profit organization actively advocates for the rights of marginalized communities around the world.
- 介词搭配: The senator decided to advocate in public for stricter gun control measures following the tragedy.
- 介词搭配: The lawyer advocated with conviction for the release of the wrongfully imprisoned man.
- 介词搭配: The non-governmental organization actively advocates for the rights of migrant workers in the region.
- 介词搭配: The non-governmental organization actively advocates for the fundamental rights and welfare of migrant workers in the region.
- 介词搭配: Many prominent environmental scientists strongly advocate the use of renewable energy sources to combat climate change.
</details>

- 这位著名的人权律师定期代表在该国寻求庇护的难民进行辩护。

- 该非政府组织积极倡导该地区移民工人的基本权利和福利。

### 42. affect <a id="42-affect"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *afficere* "to influence," from *ad-* "to" + *facere* "to do."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *afficere* "影响"，源自 *ad-* "向" + *facere* "做"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to influence or have an effect on something; to produce a change in
- CN: 影响；感动；侵袭

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Commonly confused: "affect" (verb) vs. "effect" (noun, but also a verb meaning "to bring about") — 常被混淆："affect"（动词）vs. "effect"（名词，也可作动词意为"引起"）- "Affect" can also mean "to put on a false show of" (e.g., "affect an accent") — "Affect" 也可意为 "假装"（如 "affect an accent"）
- In psychology: "affect" (noun /ˈæfekt/) means "emotion" — 在心理学中："affect"（名词 /ˈæfekt/）意为 "情感"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- affect the outcome
- affect people's lives
- adversely/negatively affect
- directly affect
- affect one's health
- affect the decision
- affect performance
- significantly affect
- affect behavior


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 影响结果
- 影响人们的生活
- 不利/负面影响
- 直接影响
- 影响健康
- 影响决定
- 影响表现
- 显著影响
- 影响行为
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- affect the quality of
- affect in a way
- affect by sth
- affect across the board
- affect to varying degrees
- affect through indirect means


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 影响…的质量
- 以某种方式影响
- 受…影响
- 全面影响
- 不同程度地影响
- 通过间接方式影响
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Air pollution can seriously affect people's health. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: The economic crisis affected millions of households. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Ocean currents significantly affect global climate patterns. (TOEFL TPO 35)
- Academic: Rising temperatures are affecting biodiversity worldwide. (Science 2021)
- Common: The lack of sleep really affects my concentration.
- 介词搭配: The recent economic recession has affected businesses across the board, from small local shops to large multinational corporations.
- 介词搭配: Climate change affects different geographical regions to varying degrees, with island nations facing the most immediate threats.
- 介词搭配: The new regulations will significantly affect the quality of air in industrial areas.
- 介词搭配: Crop yields are severely affected by prolonged drought and extreme weather events.
- 介词搭配: The manager's decision affected the team's morale through indirect means that were not immediately apparent.
- 介词搭配: The newly implemented tax policy will affect small businesses in a way that may force many of them to close down permanently.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 空气污染会严重影响人们的健康。
- 经济危机影响了数百万家庭。
- 洋流显著影响全球气候模式。
- 气温上升正在影响全球生物多样性。
- 睡眠不足确实影响我的注意力。
- 近期的经济衰退已全面影响所有企业，从小型本地商店到大型跨国公司。
- 气候变化对不同地理区域的影响程度不同，岛国面临最直接威胁。
- 新规定将显著影响工业区的空气质量。
- 农作物产量受到长期干旱和极端天气事件的严重影响。
- 经理的决定通过不易察觉的间接方式影响了团队士气。
- 新实施的税收政策将影响小企业的方式可能会迫使其中许多永久关闭。
</details>

### 43. affiliate <a id="43-affiliate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *affiliare*, from *ad-* "to" + *filius* "son."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *affiliare*，源自 *ad-* "向" + *filius* "儿子"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to officially join or connect a group, organization, or individual to a larger body
- CN: 使加入；使隶属；使紧密联系

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used in passive or reflexive: "be affiliated with," "affiliate oneself with" — 常用于被动或反身："be affiliated with", "affiliate oneself with"
- Intransitive: "affiliate with" — 不及物："affiliate with"
- Commonly used in academic, corporate, and political contexts — 常用于学术、公司和政治语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- affiliate a department
- affiliate members
- affiliate an organization
- be directly affiliated
- affiliate a school
- closely affiliated
- affiliate a program


<details>

- 使组织加入
- 直接隶属
- 使学校附属
- 紧密关联
- 使项目并入
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be affiliated with
- affiliate oneself with
- affiliate to/with a network
- be affiliated with/to sth
- affiliate oneself with
- affiliate as a member
- affiliate under an organization


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 加入；与…联合

- 加入…
- 作为成员加入

</details>

<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- Academic: All authors must confirm they are not affiliated with any competing interests. (Nature 2022)

- 介词搭配: The local hospital is affiliated under the Ministry of Health and follows all national medical guidelines.
- 介词搭配: The private school is affiliated with a prestigious international educational organization.

- 该分支办公室在成为国家贸易协会的成员后得到了正式认可。
- 该研究实验室隶属于国家科学院，并接受政府资助。
- 几位知名政界人士选择加入新的进步运动。
</details>

### 44. affirm <a id="44-affirm"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *affirmare*, from *ad-* "to" + *firmare* "to strengthen."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *affirmare*，源自 *ad-* "向" + *firmare* "加强"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to state firmly or publicly that something is true; to confirm
- CN: 断言；确认；肯定

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Stronger than "say" or "state"; implies conviction — 比 "say" 或 "state" 更强；暗示确信
- Often used in legal, formal, or religious contexts — 常用于法律、正式或宗教语境
- Can take a *that*-clause: "affirm that..." — 可接 that 从句："affirm that..."

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- affirm one's commitment
- affirm a decision
- affirm the importance
- affirm that...
- affirm one's faith
- affirm one's identity
- affirm a ruling
- publicly affirm
- affirm the value
- affirm the need


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 确认某人的承诺
- 确认决定
- 确认重要性
- 确认…
- 坚定信仰
- 确认身份
- 维持裁决
- 公开确认
- 肯定价值
- 确认必要性
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- affirm before the court
- affirm by vote
- affirm in a statement
- affirm with confidence


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 在法庭上确认
- 通过投票确认
- 在声明中确认
- 自信地确认
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The witness affirmed that she had seen the suspect. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: The president affirmed his commitment to education reform. (考研英语 2017)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The study affirms the link between exercise and mental health. (TOEFL TPO 42)
- Academic: The court affirmed the lower court's ruling. (Supreme Court Review 2021)
- Common: I want to affirm our support for this project.
- 介词搭配: The witness affirmed before the court that she had clearly seen the defendant leaving the building at midnight.
- 介词搭配: The company affirmed in a public statement its commitment to achieving carbon neutrality by 2030.
- 介词搭配: The new policy was affirmed by vote at the annual shareholders' meeting.
- 介词搭配: The CEO affirmed with confidence that the company would meet its quarterly targets.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 总统确认了他对教育改革的承诺。
- 该研究证实了运动与心理健康之间的联系。
- 法院维持了下级法院的裁决。
- 我想确认我们对这个项目的支持。
- 证人在法庭上确认她清楚地看到被告在午夜离开大楼。
- 该公司在一份公开声明中确认了其在2030年前实现碳中和的承诺。
- 新政策在年度股东大会上通过投票获得确认。
- CEO充满信心地确认公司能够实现季度目标。
</details>

### 45. affix <a id="45-affix"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *affixus*, past participle of *affigere*, from *ad-* "to" + *figere* "to fasten."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *affixus*，*affigere* 的过去分词，源自 *ad-* "向" + *figere* "固定"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to attach or fasten something to something else; to add (a signature, stamp, etc.)
- CN: 贴上；附上；签署

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Formal term; often used in administrative, postal, or legal contexts — 正式术语；常用于行政、邮政或法律语境
- "Affix a stamp" = to put a stamp on an envelope — "Affix a stamp" = 贴邮票
- "Affix one's signature" = to sign a document — "Affix one's signature" = 签名

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- affix a stamp
- affix a signature
- affix a label
- affix a seal
- affix a sticker
- affix a photo
- securely affix
- affix a value
- affix one's name


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 贴邮票
- 签名
- 贴标签
- 盖章
- 贴贴纸
- 贴照片
- 牢固地贴上
- 确定价值
- 签署名字
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- affix to the wall
- affix sth to sth
- affix with glue/tape
- affix onto a surface
- affix by means of

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 用胶水/胶带固定
- 固定到表面上
- 通过…方式固定
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- 考研: The label must be affixed to the package securely. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Participants affixed their fingerprints to the consent form. (TOEFL TPO 37)
- Academic: The specimens were affixed to glass slides for microscopic examination. (Nature Protocols 2020)
- Common: Don't forget to affix a stamp before mailing the letter.
- 介词搭配: The notice was affixed onto the bulletin board so that all employees could see it.
- 介词搭配: The broken handle was securely affixed to the ceramic mug with strong industrial-grade epoxy glue.
- 介词搭配: The company logo was affixed to the product packaging by means of a heat-sealing process.
- 介词搭配: Please affix the shipping label to the top of the cardboard box before handing the package to the courier.
- 介词搭配: Please affix the new company sign to the wall outside the main entrance.
</details>

- 公司标志通过热封工艺固定在产品包装上。

### 46. afford <a id="46-afford"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old English *geforthian* "to further, carry out," merged with Old French *aforer* "to offer."

**词源：** 源自古英语 *geforthian* "促进，实现"，与古法语 *aforer* "提供"合并。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to have enough money or resources to buy or do something; to be able to do something without risk
- CN: 负担得起；承受得起

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Almost always used with "can," "could," or "be able to": "can afford" — 几乎总是与情态动词连用："can afford"
- Followed by an infinitive: "afford to buy" — 后接不定式："afford to buy"
- Can also mean "to provide": "afford an opportunity" — 也可意为 "提供"："afford an opportunity"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- can/cannot afford
- afford the cost
- well afford
- afford an opportunity
- afford the time
- afford the expense
- barely afford
- afford protection
- afford a glimpse


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 负担得起/负担不起
- 负担得起费用
- 完全负担得起
- 提供机会
- 抽得出时间
- 负担得起费用
- 勉强负担得起
- 提供保护
- 提供一瞥
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- afford to do something
- afford to sb
- afford by the government
- afford through scholarships
- afford at a reasonable price
- afford in cash


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 负担得起做某事
- 为某人提供…
- 由政府提供
- 通过奖学金负担
- 以合理价格负担
- 用现金负担
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Many students cannot afford to live on campus. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The country cannot afford to ignore the issue of pollution. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The scholarship allowed her to afford tuition at a top university. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The experiment afforded valuable insights into cellular behavior. (Cell 2021)
- Common: We can't afford to waste any more time.
- 介词搭配: The large windows afford a breathtaking panoramic view of the entire valley to the residents of this penthouse.
- 介词搭配: Many talented students from low-income backgrounds can afford higher education through government-funded merit-based scholarships.
- 介词搭配: The university scholarship program makes quality education affordable to students from disadvantaged backgrounds at a reasonable price.
- 介词搭配: He paid for the used car in cash because he could afford the full amount without needing a loan.
- 介词搭配: Free school meals are afforded by the government to ensure that no child goes hungry during the school day.
- 介词搭配: With continuously rising tuition costs, many students from low-income families simply cannot afford to attend university without substantial financial aid.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 许多学生负担不起住校的费用。
- 该国承受不起忽视污染问题的代价。
- 奖学金让她能够负担得起一流大学的学费。
- 该实验为了解细胞行为提供了宝贵的见解。
- 我们不能再浪费时间了。
- 大窗户为这套顶层公寓的住户提供了整个山谷的壮丽全景。
- 许多来自低收入家庭的有才华的学生通过政府资助的奖学金能够负担得起高等教育。
- 大学奖学金项目使来自弱势背景的学生能够以合理的价格负担优质教育。
- 他用现金支付了二手车，因为他能负担全款而不需要贷款。
- 免费校餐由政府提供，以确保没有孩子在校期间挨饿。
- 随着学费不断上涨，许多低收入家庭的学生没有大量经济援助根本无法负担上大学的费用。
</details>

### 47. age <a id="47-age"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *aetas* via Old French *aage;* the verb form emerged in Middle English.

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *aetas* 经由古法语 *aage*；动词形式出现在中古英语中。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to grow old; to cause to look old; to mature or ripen
- CN: 变老；使老化；陈化

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Intransitive: "He has aged a lot." — 不及物："He has aged a lot."
- Transitive: "Worry ages you." — 及物："Worry ages you."
- Used in winemaking and cheese-making: "aged for two years" — 用于酿酒和奶酪制作

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- age well/badly
- age gracefully
- aged cheese/wine
- age rapidly
- age prematurely
- age a population
- age naturally
- age-related


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 衰老得好/不好
- 优雅地老去
- 陈年奶酪/葡萄酒
- 迅速老化
- 过早衰老
- 使人口老龄化
- 自然老化
- 与年龄相关的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- show signs of aging
- be aged in barrels
- age into sth
- age with grace
- age in barrels
- age over time
- age beyond recognition


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 显示出衰老迹象
- 在桶中陈酿
- 随着时间的推移变成…
- 优雅地老去
- 在桶中陈酿
- 随时间老化
- 老得认不出来
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The wine is aged in oak barrels for five years. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: The population is aging rapidly in many developed countries. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: As people age, their risk of heart disease increases. (TOEFL TPO 33)
- Academic: The cells were aged artificially in the laboratory. (Nature Aging 2022)
- Common: My grandmother has aged beautifully — she still travels everywhere.
- 介词搭配: The finest single-malt whiskey is aged in oak barrels for a minimum of fifteen years to develop its complex flavor profile.
- 介词搭配: Despite facing numerous hardships throughout her life, she aged with remarkable grace and maintained her optimistic outlook.
- 介词搭配: As people age over time, their skin loses elasticity and develops wrinkles.
- 介词搭配: The young actress aged into a mature and sophisticated performer over the course of her career.
- 介词搭配: The old photograph had faded and yellowed, showing clear signs of aging after decades of exposure to sunlight.
- 介词搭配: After years of hard labor in the sun, his face had aged beyond recognition compared to his youth.
- 介词搭配: Over the course of her long career, the actress gradually aged into a mature and highly respected performer.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这种葡萄酒在橡木桶中陈酿五年。
- 许多发达国家的人口正在迅速老龄化。
- 随着年龄增长，人们患心脏病的风险增加。
- 这些细胞在实验室中被人工老化。
- 我的祖母优雅地老去——她仍然到处旅行。
- 最好的单一麦芽威士忌在橡木桶中至少陈酿十五年，以形成其复杂的风味特征。
- 尽管一生中面临无数困难，她以非凡的优雅老去，并保持着乐观的态度。
- 随着年龄增长，人们的皮肤会失去弹性并出现皱纹。
- 这位年轻女演员在她的职业生涯中逐渐成长为一个成熟而富有鉴赏力的表演者。
- 这张老照片褪色变黄，经过数十年的阳光照射显示出明显的老化迹象。
- 在阳光下辛苦劳作多年后，他的脸与年轻时相比已经老得认不出来了。
- 在她漫长的职业生涯中，这位女演员逐渐成长为一位成熟且备受尊敬的表演者。
</details>

### 48. aggravate <a id="48-aggravate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *aggravare*, from *ad-* "to" + *gravare* "to weigh down" (*gravis* "heavy").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *aggravare*，源自 *ad-* "向" + *gravare* "加重"（*gravis* "重的"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make a problem, injury, or situation worse; to annoy
- CN: 加重；使恶化；激怒

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Often confused: "aggravate" vs. "irritate." Traditionally "aggravate" meant "to make worse," but colloquial use as a synonym for "annoy" is now widely accepted — 常被混淆："aggravate" 与 "irritate"
- Can be used medically: "aggravate an injury" — 可用于医学："aggravate an injury"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- aggravate a problem
- aggravate an injury
- aggravate the situation
- aggravate symptoms
- aggravate tensions
- aggravate a crisis
- greatly aggravate
- aggravate a condition
- aggravate inequality


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 加剧问题
- 加重伤势
- 使情况恶化
- 加重症状
- 加剧紧张
- 加剧危机
- 大大加剧
- 加重病情
- 加剧不平等
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be aggravated by
- aggravate by stress
- aggravate through neglect
- aggravate beyond repair
- aggravate over time


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 被…激怒/恶化
- 因压力而加剧
- 因忽视而恶化
- 恶化到无法修复
- 随时间恶化
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Loud noises can aggravate a headache. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The conflict was aggravated by a lack of communication. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Stress can aggravate many health conditions. (TOEFL TPO 40)
- Academic: Deforestation aggravates the effects of climate change. (Nature Climate Change 2021)
- Common: Don't aggravate the situation by arguing further.
- 介词搭配: If left untreated, the patient's condition will aggravate over time and may lead to serious complications.
- 介词搭配: The minor dispute between the neighbors aggravated beyond repair after years of unresolved complaints and misunderstandings.
- 介词搭配: The patient's condition was aggravated by stress from his demanding job.
- 介词搭配: The structural damage to the building was aggravated through years of neglect and lack of maintenance.
- 介词搭配: The teacher's frustration was aggravated by the students' constant talking during class.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 巨大的噪音会加重头痛。
- 缺乏沟通加剧了冲突。
- 压力会加重许多健康状况。
- 森林砍伐加剧了气候变化的影响。
- 不要再争论使情况恶化。
- 如果不治疗，患者的病情会随时间恶化，并可能导致严重的并发症。
- 邻里间的小纠纷在多年的未解决投诉和误解后恶化到无法修复的地步。
- 病人的病情因高压工作带来的压力而恶化。
- 建筑物结构损坏因多年忽视和缺乏维护而恶化。
- 老师的不耐烦因学生在课堂上不停地说话而加剧。
</details>

### 49. aggregate <a id="49-aggregate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *aggregatus*, from *ad-* "to" + *gregare* "to flock" (*grex* "flock").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *aggregatus*，源自 *ad-* "向" + *gregare* "聚集"（*grex* "群"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to collect or combine into a whole; to total
- CN: 聚集；合计；总计

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Both transitive ("aggregate data") and intransitive ("cells aggregate") — 既可及物（"aggregate data"）也可不及物（"cells aggregate"）
- Common in academic, statistical, and technical writing — 常见于学术、统计和技术写作

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- aggregate data
- aggregate results
- aggregate information
- aggregate demand/supply
- aggregate scores
- aggregate statistics
- aggregate content
- aggregate sources
- aggregate findings


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 汇总数据
- 汇总结果
- 汇集信息
- 总需求/总供给
- 总分
- 汇总统计数据
- 汇集内容
- 汇集来源
- 汇总发现
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- aggregate across
- aggregate into sth
- aggregate across groups
- aggregate by category
- aggregate from multiple sources
- aggregate over time


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 跨…汇总
- 聚集形成…
- 跨组汇总
- 按类别汇总
- 从多个来源汇集
- 随时间累积
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The website aggregates news from multiple sources. (CET-4 2022-12)
- 考研: The survey aggregated responses from over 5,000 participants. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The study aggregated data across ten different countries. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: Platelets aggregate at the site of a wound to form a clot. (Nature Reviews 2021)
- Common: The company aggregates user feedback to improve its products.
- 介词搭配: The research team aggregated data from multiple sources, including government surveys and academic journals, to conduct a comprehensive analysis.
- 介词搭配: The accounting software can aggregate all expenses by category, making it much easier to prepare annual tax reports.
- 介词搭配: The survey results were aggregated across groups to identify overall trends in consumer behavior.
- 介词搭配: The study found that platelets aggregate into clumps at the site of a blood vessel injury.
- 介词搭配: The system aggregates user data over time to generate personalized recommendations.
- 介词搭配: The software can aggregate sales data across all regional offices to generate a comprehensive monthly report.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该网站汇集来自多个来源的新闻。
- 该调查汇总了超过5000名参与者的回复。
- 该研究汇总了十个不同国家的数据。
- 血小板在伤口处聚集形成凝块。
- 该公司汇集用户反馈以改进产品。
- 研究团队汇集了包括政府调查和学术期刊在内的多个来源的数据，以进行全面分析。
- 该会计软件可以按类别汇总所有支出，使准备年度税务报告变得容易得多。
- 调查结果跨组汇总以识别消费者行为的总体趋势。
- 研究发现血小板在血管损伤处聚集形成团块。
- 该系统随时间累积用户数据以生成个性化推荐。
- 该软件可以汇总所有区域办事处的销售数据以生成全面的月度报告。
</details>

### 50. agitate <a id="50-agitate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *agitare* "to set in motion," frequentative of *agere* "to drive, do."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *agitare* "使运动"，*agere* "驱动，做"的反复动词。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make someone feel troubled or nervous; to stir up public concern; to shake or stir a liquid
- CN: 使不安；鼓动；搅动

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Transitive: "agitate the solution" (chemistry); "agitate the crowd" — 及物："agitate the solution"（化学）；"agitate the crowd"
- Intransitive with "for/against": "agitate for reform" — 不及物与 "for/against" 连用："agitate for reform"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- become agitated
- agitate a solution
- agitate the public
- agitate a liquid
- easily agitated
- agitate the masses
- agitate vigorously


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 变得焦虑不安
- 搅动溶液
- 鼓动公众
- 搅动液体
- 容易焦虑
- 鼓动群众
- 用力搅动
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- agitate for change
- agitate against something
- agitate for reform
- agitate for/against sth
- agitate about sth
- agitate among the masses
- agitate through social media
- agitate into action


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 鼓动变革
- 鼓动反对某事
- 鼓动改革
- 鼓动支持/反对…
- 对…感到不安；鼓动…
- 在群众中鼓动
- 通过社交媒体鼓动
- 鼓动采取行动
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The protesters agitated for environmental protection laws. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: He became visibly agitated during the interview. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The chemical solution was agitated for ten minutes. (TOEFL TPO 36)
- Academic: Social media can agitate public opinion rapidly. (Journal of Communication 2021)
- Common: Please do not agitate the dog — it might bite.
- 介词搭配: The student union organized a rally to agitate against the university's decision to increase tuition fees by 30%.
- 介词搭配: Environmental activists successfully used online platforms to agitate for policy change through social media campaigns that reached millions.
- 介词搭配: The opposition party began to agitate among the masses for a general election.
- 介词搭配: The documentary on animal cruelty agitated many viewers into action, prompting them to sign petitions and donate to animal welfare organizations.
- 介词搭配: Local residents have been agitating about the proposed construction of a chemical factory near their homes for months.
- 介词搭配: Local residents have been agitating about the proposed construction of a chemical waste plant near their residential area for over a year.
- 介词搭配: The labor union continues to agitate for better working conditions and fair wages for factory workers.
- 介词搭配: The student movement began to agitate for reform of the outdated education system.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 抗议者鼓动制定环境保护法律。
- 他在面试中明显变得焦躁不安。
- 化学溶液被搅动了十分钟。
- 社交媒体可以迅速煽动公众舆论。
- 请不要招惹那条狗——它会咬人。
- 学生会组织集会鼓动反对大学提高30%学费的决定。
- 环保活动人士成功利用在线平台通过触及数百万人的社交媒体运动来鼓动政策变革。
- 反对党开始在群众中鼓动举行大选。
- 这部关于虐待动物的纪录片促使许多观众采取行动，签署请愿书并向动物福利组织捐款。
- 几个月来，当地居民一直在对自己家附近拟建的化工厂感到不安并进行抗议。
- 一年多来，当地居民一直在对自己居住区附近拟建的化学废料厂感到不安并提出抗议。
- 工会继续鼓动为工厂工人争取更好的工作条件和公平工资。
- 学生运动开始鼓动改革过时的教育体制。
</details>

### 51. agree <a id="51-agree"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *ad-* "to" + *gratus* "pleasing," via Old French *agreer*.

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *ad-*（向）+ *gratus*（令人愉快的），经由古法语 *agreer*。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to have the same opinion; to consent; to be consistent with
- CN: 同意；赞成；一致

**Part of Speech:** vi./vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Most common intransitive pattern: "agree with someone" or "agree to something" — 最常见的不及物模式："agree with someone" 或 "agree to something"- "Agree on/upon" a topic or decision — "Agree on/upon" 某个话题或决定
- "Agree that..." + clause — "Agree that..." + 从句
- Do NOT say "agree someone's opinion" — use "agree with someone's opinion"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- fully/strongly agree
- agree that
- agree to do sth
- mutually agree
- agree to differ/disagree


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 完全同意/强烈同意
- 同意…
- 同意做某事
- 双方同意
- 同意各自保留不同意见
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- agree with sb
- agree to sth
- agree on/upon sth
- agree about sth
- agree in principle


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 同意某人
- 同意某事
- 就某事达成一致
- 就某事意见一致
- 原则上同意
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Most scientists agree that climate change is real. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: The two sides finally agreed on a cease-fire. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The results agree with the initial hypothesis. (TOEFL TPO 52)
- Academic: The data agree well with the theoretical predictions. (Physical Review Letters 2022)
- Common: I completely agree with your point of view.
- 介词搭配: I fully agree with the professor's analysis of the current economic situation.
- 介词搭配: The committee agreed to the proposed budget after extensive discussion.
- 介词搭配: The two countries finally agreed on a comprehensive trade agreement after years of negotiation.
- 介词搭配: They could not agree about the best way to proceed with the project.
- 介词搭配: The board approved the merger in principle, pending further review.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 大多数科学家一致认为气候变化是真实的。
- 双方最终就停火达成了一致。
- 结果与最初的假设相符。
- 数据与理论预测很好地吻合。
- 我完全同意你的观点。
- 我完全同意教授对当前经济形势的分析。
- 委员会经过广泛讨论后同意了拟议预算。
- 经过多年谈判，两国最终就一项全面贸易协定达成了一致。
- 他们无法就推进该项目的最佳方式达成一致。
- 董事会原则上批准了合并，有待进一步审查。
</details>

### 52. aid <a id="52-aid"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *adiuvare*, from *ad-* "to" + *iuvare* "to help."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *adiuvare*，由 *ad-*（向）+ *iuvare*（帮助）构成。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to provide help, support, or assistance
- CN: 帮助；援助；促进

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Both transitive ("aid someone") and intransitive ("aid in the investigation") — 既可及物（"aid someone"）也可不及物（"aid in the investigation"）
- More formal than "help" — 比 "help" 更正式
- Often used in humanitarian, medical, and legal contexts — 常用于人道主义、医疗和法律语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- aid recovery
- aid a process
- aid someone in need
- aid the development of
- aid and abet
- financial aid
- greatly aid


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 帮助恢复
- 促进过程
- 帮助有需要的人
- 促进……的发展
- 同谋/协助犯罪
- 经济援助
- 极大地帮助
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- aid in (doing) sth
- aid with sth
- aid sb in sth
- aid in sth
- aid through sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 帮助（做）某事
- 在……方面帮助
- 在某事上帮助某人
- 在……方面起帮助作用
- 通过……帮助
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The organization aids refugees in finding housing. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: Regular exercise aids in the prevention of heart disease. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Visual aids can significantly aid comprehension. (TOEFL TPO 34)
- Academic: The enzyme aids in the digestion of proteins. (Nature Biochemistry 2021)
- Common: A good dictionary aids language learning.
- 介词搭配: The charity aids in providing clean drinking water to remote mountain villages.
- 介词搭配: This software aids with data organization and retrieval.
- 介词搭配: The Red Cross aided the local community in rebuilding homes after the earthquake.
- 介词搭配: A balanced diet and regular exercise aid in the prevention of many chronic diseases.
- 介词搭配: Economic reforms aided recovery through market liberalization.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该组织帮助难民寻找住所。
- 定期锻炼有助于预防心脏病。
- 视觉辅助工具可以显著帮助理解。
- 这种酶有助于蛋白质的消化。
- 一本好字典有助于语言学习。
- 该慈善机构帮助为偏远山村提供清洁饮用水。
- 该软件有助于数据组织和检索。
- 红十字会帮助当地社区在地震后重建家园。
- 均衡饮食和规律锻炼有助于预防许多慢性疾病。
- 经济改革通过市场自由化促进了复苏。
</details>

### 53. ail <a id="53-ail"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old English *eglan* "to trouble, afflict," related to Old High German *eigil* "painful."

**词源：** 源自古英语 *eglan*（使烦恼，折磨），与古高地德语 *eigil*（痛苦的）相关。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to cause pain, trouble, or illness; to be unwell
- CN: 使不适；使苦恼；生病

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Less common in modern English; often used in questions: "What ails you?" — 现代英语中不太常用；常用于问句："What ails you?"
- Mainly used figuratively in formal or literary contexts — 主要用于正式或文学语境中的比喻义
- More common as the adjective "ailing" (an ailing economy) — 更常见的是形容词 "ailing"（不景气的经济）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- what ails sb/sth
- ailing economy/industry
- ailing health
- ailing patient
- continue to ail
- ailing company
- ailing relationship
- what ails the system


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 什么困扰着某人/某事
- 不景气的经济/行业
- 不佳的健康状况
- 患病的病人
- 继续受困扰
- 境况不佳的公司
- 出现问题的关系
- 系统的问题所在
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- ail from sth
- be ailing with sth
- ail with sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 因某事而痛苦
- 因……而患病
- 受……困扰
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The doctor asked what was ailing the patient. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: The country's ailing economy needs urgent reform. (考研英语 2017)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The ailing healthcare system struggles to meet demand. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The ailing coral reefs show signs of bleaching. (Nature Ecology 2020)
- Common: She has been ailing for weeks with no clear diagnosis.
- 介词搭配: The company has been ailing from years of mismanagement.
- 介词搭配: Many hospitals are ailing with a shortage of essential medical supplies.
- 介词搭配: The entire public transportation system ails with chronic underfunding and poor maintenance.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 医生询问病人哪里不舒服。
- 该国不景气的经济需要紧急改革。
- 状况不佳的医疗体系难以满足需求。
- 状况不佳的珊瑚礁显示出白化的迹象。
- 她已经病了几周，但没有明确的诊断。
- 这家公司多年来因管理不善而处境不佳。
- 许多医院因基本医疗用品短缺而运转困难。
- 整个公共交通系统深受长期资金不足和维护不善的困扰。
</details>

### 54. aim <a id="54-aim"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *aestimare* "to estimate," via Old French *asmer* and *aimer*.

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *aestimare*（估计），经由古法语 *asmer* 和 *aimer*。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to point a weapon or object toward a target; to intend or strive to achieve something
- CN: 瞄准；旨在；打算

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Both transitive ("aim a gun") and intransitive ("aim at the target") — 既可及物（"aim a gun"）也可不及物（"aim at the target"）- "Aim to do" (common): "I aim to finish this today" — "Aim to do"（常见）："I aim to finish this today"
- "Aim at doing" (British) vs "aim to do" (American) — "Aim at doing"（英式）vs "aim to do"（美式）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- aim to do sth
- aim high
- aim a weapon
- aim carefully


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 旨在做某事
- 志存高远
- 瞄准武器
- 仔细瞄准
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- aim at sth
- aim for sth
- be aimed at doing sth
- aim at achieving
- aim for perfection


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 瞄准某物；旨在
- 以……为目标
- 旨在做某事
- 旨在实现
- 追求完美
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The program aims to reduce poverty in rural areas. (CET-4 2022-06)
- 考研: We should aim for sustainable development. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The telescope was aimed at the distant galaxy. (TOEFL TPO 50)
- Academic: This study aims to identify the key factors affecting plant growth. (Plant Biology 2022)
- Common: You should aim high and pursue your dreams.
- 介词搭配: The archer aimed at the center of the target with remarkable precision.
- 介词搭配: Young entrepreneurs should aim for long-term sustainable growth rather than quick profits.
- 介词搭配: The new policy is aimed at reducing carbon emissions by 50% by 2030.
- 介词搭配: The training program aims at achieving international certification standards.
- 介词搭配: This luxury brand aims for perfection in every detail of its handcrafted products.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该项目旨在减少农村地区的贫困。
- 我们应该以可持续发展为目标。
- 望远镜对准了遥远的星系。
- 本研究旨在确定影响植物生长的关键因素。
- 你应该志存高远，追求自己的梦想。
- 弓箭手以惊人的精确度瞄准了靶心。
- 年轻企业家应以长期可持续发展为目标，而非追求快速利润。
- 这项新政策旨在到2030年将碳排放减少50%。
- 该培训项目以达成国际认证标准为目标。

### 55. air <a id="55-air"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *aer* and Greek *aēr*; the verb sense "to expose to air" emerged in the 16th century.

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *aer* 和希腊语 *aēr*；动词义“使通风”出现于16世纪。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to broadcast on radio or television; to express an opinion publicly; to expose to fresh air
- CN: 播送；公开发表；晾干

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Transitive: "air a show," "air grievances," "air clothing" — 及物："air a show"（播出一个节目），"air grievances"（表达不满），"air clothing"（晾衣服）
- Intransitive: "The show airs on Friday." — 不及物："The show airs on Friday."（该节目周五播出。）- "Air one's views" = to express opinions openly — "Air one's views" = 公开发表观点

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- air a program/show
- air one's views/grievances
- air a room
- air laundry
- air a complaint
- air publicly
- be aired live


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 播出节目
- 公开发表观点/不满
- 给房间通风
- 晾衣服
- 公开表达不满
- 公开表达
- 现场直播
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- air on TV
- air out
- air sth in public
- air on a network


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 在电视上播出
- 使通风
- 公开表达某事
- 在电视网播出
</details>

<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The interview will air on national television tonight. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The meeting was an opportunity to air complaints. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The documentary first aired in 2019. (TOEFL TPO 39)
- Academic: The podcast airs weekly and covers scientific breakthroughs. (Nature Podcast 2022)
- Common: Open the windows to air out the kitchen after cooking.
- 介词搭配: The new documentary will air on national television next Monday evening.
- 介词搭配: Please open all the windows to air out the stuffy conference room before the meeting starts.
- 介词搭配: The activist aired her concerns in public during the town hall meeting.
- 介词搭配: The popular talk show airs on a national network every weekday evening.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">

### 56. alarm <a id="56-alarm"></a>
**Etymology:** From Italian *all'arme* "to arms!"; the verb meaning "to frighten" dates from the late 16th century.

**词源：** 源自意大利语 *all’arme*（拿起武器！）；动词义“使惊恐”可追溯至16世纪末。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to cause someone to feel sudden fear, worry, or danger; to fit with an alarm device
- CN: 使惊慌；警告；给……装警报器

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Stronger than "worry"; implies a sense of urgency or danger — 比 "worry" 更强烈；暗示紧迫感或危险
- Often used in passive: "I was alarmed by the news" — 常用于被动语态："I was alarmed by the news"
- "Alarm" as a noun is more common; as a verb it is formal or emphatic — "Alarm" 作名词更常见；作动词时较正式或带有强调意味

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- alarm sb
- alarm the public
- alarm bells ring
- alarm system
- sound the alarm
- raise the alarm
- false alarm
- alarmed expression


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使某人惊慌
- 引起公众恐慌
- 警钟敲响
- 警报系统
- 发出警报
- 发出警报
- 虚惊一场
- 惊慌的表情
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be alarmed at sth
- be alarmed by sth
- be alarmed about sth

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 被……惊吓
- 对……感到担忧

### 57. alert <a id="57-alert"></a>
**Etymology:** From Italian *all'erta* "to the watchtower"; entered English as an adjective in the 17th century, later used as a verb.

**词源：** 源自意大利语 *all’erta*（到瞭望塔）；17世纪作为形容词进入英语，后用作动词。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to warn someone about a danger, problem, or important situation
- CN: 警告；使警觉；通知

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Typically: "alert someone to something" — 典型用法："alert someone to something"
- Can take a *that*-clause: "alerted them that..." — 可接 *that* 从句："alerted them that..."
- Adjective form more common: "stay alert" — 形容词形式更常见："stay alert"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- alert the authorities
- alert the public
- alert emergency services
- be on the alert
- alert system
- automatically alert


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 通知当局
- 警示公众
- 通知紧急服务部门
- 保持警惕
- 警报系统
- 自动提醒
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- alert sb to sth
- alert sb about sth
- alert in advance
- alert via email/text
- alert sb of sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 提醒某人注意某事
- 就某事提醒某人
- 提前提醒
- 通过电子邮件/短信提醒
- 通知某人某事
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The weather service alerted residents about the approaching storm. (CET-4 2022-12)
- 考研: The study alerts us to the dangers of plastic pollution. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Security guards were alerted to the suspicious package. (TOEFL TPO 41)
- Academic: The early warning system alerted the population to the tsunami. (Nature Geoscience 2021)
- Common: Please alert me if there are any changes to the schedule.
- 介词搭配: The meteorological department alerted coastal residents to the approaching typhoon.
- 介词搭配: The hospital alerted the public about the urgent need for blood donations after the accident.
- 介词搭配: All passengers were alerted in advance of the flight cancellation due to the typhoon.
- 介词搭配: The security system alerts users via email whenever unusual activity is detected.
- 介词搭配: The manager alerted the team of the upcoming changes to the project schedule.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 经理通知了团队项目日程即将发生的变化。

### 58. alienate <a id="58-alienate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *alienatus*, from *alienare* "to make another's," from *alius* "other."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *alienatus*，来自 *alienare*（使成为他人的），源自 *alius*（其他的）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to cause someone to feel isolated, estranged, or unfriendly; to transfer ownership
- CN: 使疏远；使不友好；让渡（所有权）

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used in psychological or sociological contexts — 常用于心理学或社会学语境
- "Alienate someone from something" — "Alienate someone from something"
- Passive common: "felt alienated from society" — 被动语态常见："felt alienated from society"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- feel alienated
- alienate friends/family
- alienate voters/supporters
- socially alienated
- alienate the public
- alienate potential customers
- become alienated
- mutually alienated

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 感到被疏远
- 疏远朋友/家人
- 失去选民/支持者
- 在社会上被疏远
- 使公众疏远
- 失去潜在客户
- 变得疏远
- 相互疏远的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- alienate sb from sth
- alienate sb from sb
- be alienated from
- alienate by sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 使某人与……疏远
- 使某人与某人疏远
- 与……疏远
- 因……而疏远
</details>

<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- TOEFL/IELTS: Immigrants sometimes feel alienated in a new country. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: Social media may inadvertently alienate users seeking genuine connection. (Journal of Social Psychology 2022)
- Common: Don't alienate your team members by ignoring their ideas.
- 介词搭配: The manager's dismissive attitude alienated the entire design team from the creative decision-making process.
- 介词搭配: His constant criticism and negative remarks alienated him from his closest friends.
- 介词搭配: Many young voters feel alienated from the traditional political system.
- 介词搭配: The strict policies alienated a large number of customers by restricting their choices.
</details>

### 59. align <a id="59-align"></a>
**Etymology:** From French *aligner*, from Latin *ad-* "to" + *linea* "line."

**词源：** 源自法语 *aligner*，源自拉丁语 *ad-*（向）+ *linea*（线）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to place in a straight line or in correct relative positions; to give support to a group or cause
- CN: 排列整齐；使成一线；使一致；与……结盟

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Both transitive ("align the text") and intransitive ("align with") — 既可及物（"align the text"）也可不及物（"align with"）- "Align with" means to support or agree with — "Align with" 表示支持或同意
- In business/strategy: "align goals/strategies" — 在商业/策略中："align goals/strategies"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- align text/objects
- align goals/strategies
- align interests
- align values
- align perfectly
- align properly
- remain aligned


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 对齐文本/对象
- 协调目标/策略
- 协调利益
- 协调价值观
- 完全一致
- 正确对齐
- 保持一致
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- align with sth
- align oneself with sb/sth
- align sth with sth
- align against sb/sth
- align sth to sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 与……对齐/一致
- 使自己与……结盟/一致
- 使某物与某物对齐
- 联合反对……
- 使某物与某物一致
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Make sure the text is aligned to the left margin. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The company's policies are aligned with environmental standards. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The planets aligned in a rare celestial event. (TOEFL TPO 38)
- Academic: The sequences were aligned using computational tools. (Nature Biotechnology 2021)
- Common: Our values align perfectly, so we work well together.
- 介词搭配: The company's long-term business strategy aligns with global sustainability goals.
- 介词搭配: Several tech startups aligned themselves with the industry giant to strengthen their market position.
- 介词搭配: The marketing department needs to align its campaign with the latest consumer trends.
- 介词搭配: The smaller nations aligned against the expansionist policies of their powerful neighbor.
- 介词搭配: The new curriculum was carefully aligned to the national education standards.
</details>

### 60. allege <a id="60-allege"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *allegare* "to send a message, bring forth," from *ad-* "to" + *legare* "to depute."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *allegare*（发送信息，提出），源自 *ad-*（向）+ *legare*（派遣）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to claim or assert something without proof; to state as an allegation
- CN: 断言；指控；声称（尤指未经证实）

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used in legal and journalistic contexts — 常用于法律和新闻语境
- Implies the claim is unproven or disputed — 暗示该声称未经证实或有争议
- Patterns: "allege that...", "be alleged to do/have done" — 句型："allege that..."，"be alleged to do/have done"
- Adverb: "allegedly" (commonly used in news reports) — 副词："allegedly"（常用于新闻报道）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- allege that
- be alleged to
- allege wrongdoing
- allege fraud
- allegedly
- allege misconduct
- allege conspiracy
- falsely allege
- it is alleged that


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 声称/指控……
- 据称……
- 指控不当行为
- 指控欺诈
- 据称
- 指控不当行为
- 指控阴谋
- 虚假指控
- 据称……
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- (allege typically uses that-clauses and object complements rather than prepositional patterns)- allege sth against sb
- allege sth about sb/sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 指控某人某事
- 声称有关某人/某事的情况
</details>

<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The article alleged that the company had misused funds. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: He is alleged to have accepted bribes. (考研英语 2017)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The plaintiff alleged copyright infringement. (TOEFL TPO 53)
- Academic: The report alleges systematic errors in the original study. (Nature 2022)
- Common: It is alleged that the accident was caused by negligence.
- 介词搭配: The prosecution alleged fraud against the former CEO.
- 介词搭配: The report alleges disturbing facts about the company's safety practices.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">

### 61. alleviate <a id="61-alleviate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *alleviatus*, past participle of *alleviare* ("to lighten, alleviate"), from *ad-* ("to") + *levis* ("light, not heavy").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *alleviatus*，*alleviare*（减轻，缓解）的过去分词，源自 *ad-*（向）+ *levis*（轻的）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To make (suffering, a deficiency, or a problem) less severe; to mitigate or relieve.
- CN: 减轻，缓解（痛苦、缺陷或问题）。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used with abstract objects like "pain," "suffering," "concern," "poverty," or "symptom." — 常与抽象宾语连用，如"pain"、"suffering"、"concern"、"poverty"或"symptom"
- Stronger and more formal than "relieve"; typically implies partial rather than complete removal. — 比"relieve"更强烈、更正式；通常指部分而非完全消除
- In medical contexts, preferred over "relieve" for describing symptom management. — 在医疗语境中，描述症状管理时比"relieve"更常用

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- alleviate poverty
- alleviate pain / suffering
- alleviate symptoms
- alleviate concerns / fears
- alleviate the burden
- alleviate stress
- alleviate the problem
- alleviate congestion
- greatly alleviate


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 减轻贫困
- 缓解疼痛/痛苦
- 缓解症状
- 减轻担忧/恐惧
- 减轻负担
- 缓解压力
- 缓解问题
- 缓解拥堵
- 大大缓解
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- alleviate sb of sth
- alleviate by doing sth
- alleviate through sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 减轻某人的（负担等）
- 通过做某事来缓解
- 通过……来缓解
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The government has taken measures to alleviate the housing shortage in urban areas. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: Meditation can help alleviate anxiety and improve concentration. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Planting trees along the coastline can alleviate the impact of storm surges. (TOEFL TPO 51)
- Academic: A team of researchers found that a new drug compound could significantly alleviate chemotherapy-induced nausea. (Nature 2019)
- Common: Drinking warm tea helped alleviate her sore throat.
- 介词搭配: The medication alleviated him of the chronic pain in his lower back.
- 介词搭配: The government alleviated the housing shortage by accelerating the construction of affordable apartments.
- 介词搭配: The company alleviated employee concerns through transparent communication about the restructuring plan.
</details>

- 药物缓解了他下背部的慢性疼痛。

### 62. allocate <a id="62-allocate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Medieval Latin *allocatus*, past participle of *allocare* ("to place, assign"), from Latin *ad-* ("to") + *locare* ("to place").

**词源：** 源自中世纪拉丁语 *allocatus*，*allocare*（放置，分配）的过去分词，源自拉丁语 *ad-*（向）+ *locare*（放置）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To distribute (resources or duties) for a particular purpose; to set apart or designate.
- CN: 分配，划拨（资源、任务等）。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Typically used in formal or business contexts regarding resources, funds, time, or tasks. — 通常用于关于资源、资金、时间或任务的正式或商业语境
- Followed by a direct object + prepositional phrase (e.g., "allocate funds to education"). — 后接直接宾语 + 介词短语（例如："allocate funds to education"）
- In computing, refers to reserving memory or storage space. — 在计算机领域指预留内存或存储空间

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- allocate resources / funds / budget
- allocate tasks / duties / responsibilities
- allocate shares
- allocate memory
- allocate space
- allocate efficiently
- allocate according to need
- allocate proportionally


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 分配资源/资金/预算
- 分配任务/职责/责任
- 分配股份
- 分配内存
- 分配空间
- 高效分配
- 根据需要分配
- 按比例分配
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- allocate sth to sb
- allocate time for sth
- allocate for sth
- allocate among/between
- allocate to sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 将某物分配给某人
- 为某事分配时间
- 为……而分配
- 在……之间分配
- 分配给……
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The company decided to allocate more resources to research and development. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: It is essential to allocate sufficient time for each section of the exam. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The government must allocate a larger portion of the budget to renewable energy. (IELTS Cambridge 17)
- Academic: The experiment required the lab to allocate dedicated server space for data processing. (Science 2020)
- Common: The manager allocated each team member a specific role for the project.
- 介词搭配: The government allocated significant funding to local schools for infrastructure improvements.
- 介词搭配: Students should allocate sufficient time for exam preparation and extracurricular activities.
- 介词搭配: A substantial portion of the annual budget is allocated for scientific research and technological development.
- 介词搭配: The emergency funds were allocated among the five affected provinces according to the scale of the disaster.
- 介词搭配: Additional resources were allocated to the departments that needed them most urgently.
</details>

### 63. allot <a id="63-allot"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *aloter*, from *a-* ("to") + *lot* ("portion, share"), ultimately from Frankish *lot*.

**词源：** 源自古法语 *aloter*，源自 *a-*（向）+ *lot*（份额），最终源自法兰克语 *lot*。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To give or apportion (something) to someone as a share or task; to assign officially.
- CN: 分配，分派（份额、任务等）。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Closely related to "allocate," but "allot" often suggests dividing a fixed amount among multiple recipients. — 与"allocate"密切相关，但"allot"通常指将固定数量分配给多个接收者
- Commonly used for time, space, portions, and specific entitlements. — 常用于时间、空间、份额和特定权利

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- allot time / money / space
- allot a portion / share
- allot seats / tickets
- allot a budget
- allot resources
- allot equally
- allot by lottery
- allot a quota


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 分配时间/金钱/空间
- 分配一份/份额
- 分配座位/门票
- 分配预算
- 分配资源
- 平均分配
- 抽签分配
- 分配配额
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- allot sth to sb
- be allotted to sb
- allot for sth
- allot to sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 将某物分配给某人
- 被分配给某人
- 为……分配
- 分配给……
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Each student was allotted 15 minutes for the oral presentation. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: The limited resources must be allotted fairly among all departments. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The organizer allotted two tickets per person for the opening ceremony. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: In the clinical trial, patients were randomly allotted to either the treatment or the placebo group. (The Lancet 2021)
- Common: I allotted myself two hours to finish the report.
- 介词搭配: The committee allotted a substantial budget for the construction of the new library.
- 介词搭配: A specific research topic was allotted to each graduate student by their advisor.
- 介词搭配: The organizer allotted the best performance slots to the most popular acts.
- 介词搭配: A significant portion of the government grant was allotted to community health programs.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 每位学生被分配了15分钟进行口头报告。
- 有限的资源必须在所有部门之间公平分配。
- 组织者为每人分配了两张开幕式门票。
- 在临床试验中，患者被随机分配到治疗组或安慰剂组。
- 我给自己分配了两个小时完成报告。
- 委员会为新建图书馆划拨了可观的预算。
- 每个研究生的导师都给他们分配了特定的研究课题。
- 组织者将最佳表演时段分配给了最受欢迎的节目。
- 政府资助的很大一部分被分配给了社区健康项目。
</details>
