### 64. allow <a id="64-allow"></a>
**Etymology:** From Late Latin *allaudare* ("to praise, approve"), from Latin *ad-* ("to") + *laudare* ("to praise"), later shifting in Old French and Middle English to mean "permit."

**词源：** 源自晚期拉丁语 *allaudare*（赞扬，认可），源自拉丁语 *ad-*（向）+ *laudare*（赞扬），后在古法语和中古英语中转变为“允许”之意。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To permit (someone) to have or do something; to enable something to happen; to acknowledge or admit as true.
- CN: 允许；准许；使能够；承认。

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- One of the most common English verbs; three main uses: (1) "allow somebody to do something," (2) "allow something," (3) "allow that + clause." — 英语中最常见的动词之一；三种主要用法：（1）"allow somebody to do something"（2）"allow something"（3）"allow that + 从句"- "Allow for" means "to take into consideration" (e.g., "allow for delays"). — "Allow for" 意为"考虑到"（例如："allow for delays"）
- In passive voice: "Smoking is not allowed." — 被动语态："Smoking is not allowed."

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- allow sb to do sth
- allow sth (e.g., allow access, allow entry)
- allow that (concede)
- not allow / be not allowed
- allow access
- legally allowed


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 允许某人做某事
- 允许某事（如允许进入）
- 承认
- 不允许
- 允许进入
- 法律允许的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- allow for sth
- allow time for
- allow room for
- allow sb into sth
- allow of sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 考虑到；顾及
- 为……留出时间
- 为……留出余地
- 允许某人进入某处
- 容许有可能
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The new policy allows employees to work from home twice a week. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The online platform allows users to access thousands of academic papers for free. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The visa allows the holder to stay in the country for up to six months. (IELTS Cambridge 15)
- Academic: The algorithm allows for real-time data processing without significant latency. (Nature Machine Intelligence 2020)
- Common: Please allow me to introduce myself.
- 介词搭配: The project budget allows for unexpected delays and material cost increases.
- 介词搭配: You should allow ample time for traffic jams when commuting during rush hour.
- 介词搭配: The new company policy allows room for flexible working hours and remote work options.
- 介词搭配: The security guard would not allow anyone into the building without a valid ID card.
- 介词搭配: The strict regulations allow of no exceptions under any circumstances.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 新政策允许员工每周在家工作两天。
- 该在线平台允许用户免费访问数千篇学术论文。
- 该签证允许持有人在国家停留最长六个月。
- 该算法允许实时数据处理而不会出现显著延迟。
- 请允许我自我介绍。
- 项目预算考虑到了不可预见的延误和材料成本上涨。
- 在高峰时段通勤时，你应该为交通堵塞留出充足的时间。
- 新公司政策为弹性工作时间和远程办公选项留出了余地。
- 保安不允许任何没有有效身份证件的人进入大楼。
- 严格的规定在任何情况下都不容许例外。
</details>

### 65. alloy <a id="65-alloy"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *aloi* (noun), from Latin *alligare* ("to bind together"), from *ad-* ("to") + *ligare* ("to bind").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *aloi*（名词），源自拉丁语 *alligare*（捆绑在一起），源自 *ad-*（向）+ *ligare*（捆绑）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: (vt.) To mix (metals) to form an alloy; to debase or impair by admixture. (n.) A metal made by combining two or more metallic elements.
- CN: (vt.) 将……铸成合金；使成合金；搀杂。 (n.) 合金。

**Part of Speech:** vt. (also n.)

**Usage Notes:**

- Primarily used as a noun in everyday contexts; the verb form is more technical. — 在日常语境中主要用作名词；动词形式更偏技术性
- As a verb, often in passive: "steel is alloyed with chromium." — 作动词时，常用于被动语态："steel is alloyed with chromium"
- Figuratively, can mean to spoil or reduce the value of something (e.g., "joy alloyed with sorrow"). — 比喻义可表示破坏或降低某物的价值（例如："joy alloyed with sorrow"）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- alloyed steel / metal
- alloy of (two metals)
- lightweight alloy
- alloy composition
- alloy wheel
- high-strength alloy
- alloying element
- alloy production


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 合金钢/金属
- （两种金属的）合金
- 轻质合金
- 合金成分
- 合金轮毂
- 高强度合金
- 合金元素
- 合金生产
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- alloy with sth
- be alloyed with
- alloy sth with sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 与某物熔成合金
- 与……熔合
- 将某物与某物熔成合金
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Bronze is an alloy of copper and tin that has been used since ancient times. (CET-4 2017-06)
- 考研: The new alloy developed by the research team is both stronger and lighter than traditional steel. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Aircraft engines are typically made from high-temperature alloys that can withstand extreme stress. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: By alloying iron with carbon, we obtain steel with vastly improved mechanical properties. (Nature Materials 2020)
- Common: The happiness of the occasion was alloyed with a touch of sadness.
- 介词搭配: Copper alloys readily with tin to form bronze, one of the earliest metals used by ancient civilizations.
- 介词搭配: The manufacturer alloys copper with tin to produce bronze, an ancient material still widely used today.
- 介词搭配: Stainless steel is alloyed with chromium and nickel to enhance its corrosion resistance.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 青铜是铜和锡的合金，自古以来就被使用。
- 研究团队开发的新型合金比传统钢材更强更轻。
- 飞机发动机通常由能够承受极端应力的高温合金制成。
- 通过将铁与碳熔合，我们获得了机械性能大幅提高的钢。
- 这个场合的快乐中掺杂着一丝悲伤。
- 铜容易与锡熔合形成青铜，这是古代文明最早使用的金属之一。
- 制造商将铜与锡熔合生产青铜，这是一种至今仍广泛使用的古老材料。
- 不锈钢与铬和镍熔合以增强其耐腐蚀性。
</details>

### 66. allude <a id="66-allude"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *alludere* ("to play with, jest, refer to"), from *ad-* ("to") + *ludere* ("to play").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *alludere*（戏弄，开玩笑，提及），源自 *ad-*（向）+ *ludere*（玩耍）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To make an indirect reference to something; to hint at or suggest without stating explicitly.
- CN: 间接提到；暗指；影射。

**Part of Speech:** vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Always followed by the preposition "to" (e.g., "allude to a problem"). — 总是后接介词"to"（例如："allude to a problem"）
- Not to be confused with "elude" (to evade) or "delude" (to mislead) — a common spelling/meaning error.
- Softer and more oblique than "refer to"; implies the reference is subtle or indirect. — 比"refer to"更委婉、更间接；暗示引用是微妙或间接的

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- allude in passing
- frequently alluded to
- never allude to
- allude in a speech


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 顺便提及
- 经常被提及
- 从不提及
- 在演讲中提及
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- allude to sth/sb
- allude indirectly to
- allude to the possibility
- allude to a fact
- allude to one's past


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 暗指某事/某人
- 间接提到
- 暗示可能性
- 暗指某个事实
- 提及某人的过去
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The speaker alluded to the economic crisis without giving any specific details. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: In his speech, the professor alluded to several theories that would be discussed in the next lecture. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The author alludes to Shakespeare's Hamlet to emphasize the theme of indecision. (TOEFL TPO 38)
- Academic: The paper alludes to earlier work by Einstein but does not develop that line of inquiry. (Physical Review Letters 2019)
- Common: She didn't mention his name, but everyone knew she was alluding to him.
- 介词搭配: The finance minister alluded to the possibility of tax reforms in the upcoming budget.
- 介词搭配: The author alluded indirectly to historical events without explicitly naming them.
- 介词搭配: The investigative report alluded to a disturbing fact about the company's environmental violations.
- 介词搭配: During the interview, the famous actor alluded to his difficult childhood without going into specific details.
- 介词搭配: In her speech, the professor alluded to a famous philosopher whose work influenced her research.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 演讲者提到了经济危机，但没有给出任何具体细节。
- 在演讲中，教授提到了将在下一节课讨论的几个理论。
- 作者引用了莎士比亚的《哈姆雷特》来强调犹豫不决的主题。
- 这篇论文提到了爱因斯坦的早期工作，但没有展开那条研究路线。
- 她没有提他的名字，但每个人都知道她指的是他。
- 财政部长在即将到来的预算中暗示了税制改革的可能性。
- 作者间接提到了历史事件，但没有明确点名。
- 这篇调查报道暗示了关于该公司环境违规行为的一个令人不安的事实。
- 在采访中，这位著名演员没有详谈就提起了他艰苦的童年。
- 在她的演讲中，教授提及了一位其著作影响了她的研究的著名哲学家。
</details>

### 67. allure <a id="67-allure"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *aleurer* ("to attract, lure"), from *a-* ("to") + *loirre* ("lure, bait"), from Frankish *lothr* ("bait").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *aleurer*（吸引，引诱），源自 *a-*（向）+ *loirre*（诱饵），源自法兰克语 *lothr*（诱饵）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: (vt.) To powerfully attract or tempt someone; to charm or fascinate. (n.) The quality of being attractive or fascinating.
- CN: (vt.) 引诱；诱惑；吸引。(n.) 诱惑力；魅力。

**Part of Speech:** vt. (also n.)

**Usage Notes:**

- Stronger and more compelling than "attract"; implies a seductive or irresistible quality. — 比"attract"更强烈、更引人注目；暗示有诱惑力或不可抗拒
- Often used with abstract nouns: "allure of fame/money/power/adventure." — 常与抽象名词连用："allure of fame/money/power/adventure"
- The noun form is more common in modern usage than the verb. — 在现代用法中名词形式比动词更常见

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- allure of sth
- sexual allure
- the allure of fame / money / the unknown
- alluring smile / offer
- alluring prospect
- alluring fragrance
- resist the allure
- alluring voice


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 某事物的诱惑力
- 性诱惑
- 名声/金钱/未知的诱惑
- 迷人的微笑/诱人的提议
- 诱人的前景
- 迷人的香味
- 抵抗诱惑
- 迷人的声音
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be allured by
- allure sb into sth
- allure sb from sth
- allure sb to sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 被……诱惑
- 引诱某人做某事
- 将某人从某处引诱开
- 吸引某人到某处
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The beautiful beaches and warm climate allure millions of tourists every year. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: Many young people are allured by the prospect of starting their own business. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The allure of studying abroad lies in both academic opportunity and cultural immersion. (IELTS Cambridge 16)
- Academic: The study examines how social media algorithms allure users into prolonged engagement. (Journal of Communication 2020)
- Common: The allure of the city at night was impossible to resist.
- 介词搭配: Many talented professionals are allured by the high salaries and fast-paced environment of the tech industry.
- 介词搭配: The misleading advertisement allured consumers into purchasing products that did not meet their expectations.
- 介词搭配: The promise of adventure allured many young people from their quiet hometowns.
- 介词搭配: The exclusive resort allured wealthy tourists to its pristine private beaches.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 许多年轻人被创业的前景所吸引。
- 出国留学的魅力在于学术机会和文化沉浸。
- 该研究考察了社交媒体算法如何吸引用户长时间参与。
- 夜晚城市的魅力令人无法抗拒。
- 许多有才能的专业人士被科技行业的高薪和快节奏环境所诱惑。
- 误导性广告引诱消费者购买了不符合预期的产品。
- 冒险的承诺将许多年轻人从宁静的家乡吸引走了。
- 这家独家度假村将富裕游客吸引到了其 pristine 的私人海滩。
</details>

### 68. ally <a id="68-ally"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *alier* ("to combine, unite"), from Latin *alligare* ("to bind to"), from *ad-* ("to") + *ligare* ("to bind").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *alier*（结合，联合），源自拉丁语 *alligare*（绑定到），源自 *ad-*（向）+ *ligare*（捆绑）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: (vt.) To unite or form a connection between parties, typically for mutual benefit. (vi.) To become an ally. (n.) A person or group that cooperates with another.
- CN: (vt.) 使结盟；使联合。(vi.) 结盟。(n.) 盟友；同盟者。

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. & n.

**Usage Notes:**

- Verb often used reflexively: "ally oneself with/to somebody." — 动词常用于反身结构："ally oneself with/to somebody"
- The noun form is far more common in everyday usage. — 名词形式在日常使用中常见得多
- In international relations, "allied forces," "allied nations." — 在国际关系中："allied forces"（盟军），"allied nations"（同盟国）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- allied forces / nations
- close ally / staunch ally
- political / military ally
- remain allied
- traditional ally
- strategically allied


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 盟军/同盟国
- 亲密盟友/坚定盟友
- 政治/军事盟友
- 保持同盟
- 传统盟友
- 战略结盟的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- ally with sb
- ally oneself with/to sb
- ally against a common enemy
- ally sb to sb/sth
- be allied with/to


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 与某人结盟
- 使自己与某人结盟
- 联合对抗共同敌人
- 使某人与某人/某事联合
- 与……结盟
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The two companies allied with each other to develop new technology. (CET-4 2017-12)
- 考研: Small nations often ally themselves with larger powers for economic and military protection. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: During the war, the two countries allied to defeat the invading army. (TOEFL TPO 42)
- Academic: The species allied itself with certain bacteria to enhance nutrient absorption. (Nature Ecology & Evolution 2020)
- Common: She found a trusted ally in her fight for equal rights.
- 介词搭配: During World War II, Britain allied with France and other nations to defeat the Axis powers.
- 介词搭配: The company allied itself with several innovative tech startups to expand its market reach.
- 介词搭配: The opposition parties allied against the controversial new legislation.
- 介词搭配: The strategic partnership allied the small manufacturer with a major global distributor.
- 介词搭配: The non-profit organization is closely allied with several international health agencies.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 两家公司相互联盟以开发新技术。
- 小国常与大国结盟以获得经济和军事保护。
- 战争期间，两国结盟击败了入侵军队。
- 该物种与某些细菌结盟以增强营养吸收。
- 她在争取平等权利的过程中找到了一个值得信赖的盟友。
- 二战期间，英国与法国及其他国家结盟以击败轴心国。
- 该公司与几家创新型科技初创公司结盟以扩大其市场覆盖范围。
- 反对党联合起来反对这项有争议的新立法。
- 这一战略伙伴关系使这家小型制造商与一家全球大型经销商联合了起来。
- 该非营利组织与几个国际卫生机构关系密切。
</details>

### 69. alter <a id="69-alter"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *alterare* ("to change, make different"), from *alter* ("the other (of two)").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *alterare*（改变，使不同），源自 *alter*（两者中的另一个）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To change or make different in some characteristic, typically without transforming the fundamental nature.
- CN: 改变；更改；改动。

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Softer than "change"; often implies modification rather than complete transformation. — 比"change"更温和；通常暗示修改而非彻底转变
- Can be transitive ("alter a plan") or intransitive ("the situation has altered"). — 既可及物（"alter a plan"）也可不及物（"the situation has altered"）
- In tailoring, "alter" means to adjust the fit of clothing. — 在裁缝领域，"alter"指调整衣服的合身度

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- alter the course of
- alter one's appearance
- alter a plan / decision / policy
- alter clothes / a garment
- alter the outcome
- alter significantly
- alter behavior
- permanently alter
- alter one's lifestyle


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 改变……的进程
- 改变某人的外表
- 改变计划/决定/政策
- 改衣服
- 改变结果
- 显著改变
- 改变行为
- 永久改变
- 改变某人的生活方式
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- alter from sth to sth
- alter in sth
- alter for the better/worse
- alter by sth
- alter into sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从……变为……
- 在某方面改变
- 变好/变坏
- 通过……改变
- 变成……
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The discovery of the error altered the entire course of the experiment. (CET-4 2020-09)
- 考研: Advances in technology have fundamentally altered the way we communicate. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Construction of the dam may alter the natural flow of the river. (TOEFL TPO 33)
- Academic: Climate change is altering migration patterns of many bird species. (Science 2021)
- Common: I need to alter this dress — the sleeves are too long.
- 介词搭配: The abandoned warehouse was altered from a storage facility into a trendy art gallery.
- 介词搭配: The genetic sequence did not alter in any significant way across multiple generations.
- 介词搭配: The economic situation has altered for the better since the new policies were implemented.
- 介词搭配: The original architectural plan was altered by adding several new structural supports to ensure safety.
- 介词搭配: The old factory hall was altered into a modern open-plan office space.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 错误发现改变了整个实验的进程。
- 技术进步从根本上改变了我们的沟通方式。
- 大坝的建设可能会改变河流的自然水流。
- 气候变化正在改变许多鸟类的迁徙模式。
- 我需要改这条裙子——袖子太长了。
- 废弃的仓库从存储设施改造成了一家时尚的画廊。
- 遗传序列在数代之间没有发生任何显著的改变。
- 自新政策实施以来，经济形势已好转。
- 原始建筑方案通过增加几处新的结构支撑来确保安全而进行了修改。
- 旧厂房大厅被改造成了一个现代化的开放式办公空间。
</details>

### 70. alternate <a id="70-alternate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *alternatus*, past participle of *alternare* ("to do one thing and then another, alternate"), from *alternus* ("alternating, every other"), from *alter* ("the other").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *alternatus*，*alternare*（交替进行）的过去分词，源自 *alternus*（交替的，每隔一个的），源自 *alter*（另一个）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: (vi.) To occur or succeed in turns; to take turns. (vt.) To cause to follow one another in succession. (adj.) Happening in turn; every other.
- CN: (vi.) 交替；轮流。(vt.) 使交替；使轮流。(adj.) 交替的；间隔的。

**Part of Speech:** vi. & vt. & adj. & n.

**Usage Notes:**

- Pronunciation differs: verb /ˈɔːltərneɪt/ vs. adjective /ɔːlˈtɜːrnət/. — 发音不同：动词 /ˈɔːltərneɪt/ vs 形容词 /ɔːlˈtɜːrnət/
- Verb pattern: "alternate between A and B" or "alternate A with B." — 动词句型："alternate between A and B" 或 "alternate A with B"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- alternate days / weeks / years
- alternate current (AC)
- alternate routes
- alternate regularly
- alternate shifts
- alternate positions


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 每隔一天/周/年
- 交流电
- 替代路线
- 定期交替
- 轮班
- 交换位置
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- alternate between X and Y
- alternate with sth
- alternate between work and rest
- alternate in doing sth
- alternate from sth to sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 在X和Y之间交替
- 与……交替
- 劳逸结合
- 轮流做某事
- 从……交替到……
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The weather alternated between rain and sunshine throughout the day. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: The study suggests that people who alternate between sitting and standing at work report less back pain. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The two speakers alternated their presentations to maintain audience interest. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The compound alternates between two structural conformations depending on temperature. (Journal of the American Chemical Society 2021)
- Common: We alternate weekend shifts so that everyone gets time off.
- 介词搭配: The weather in this region frequently alternates between dry sunny days and heavy rainfall.
- 介词搭配: Periods of intense concentration should alternate with short breaks to maintain productivity.
- 介词搭配: It is important for students to alternate between intense study and adequate rest to avoid burnout.
- 介词搭配: The two professors alternate in delivering the weekly lecture series.
- 介词搭配: The local climate alternates from hot and humid summers to cold and dry winters.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 全天天气在雨和晴之间交替。
- 研究表明，工作中交替坐立的人报告的背痛更少。
- 两位演讲者交替进行演示以保持观众兴趣。
- 该化合物根据温度在两种结构构象之间交替。
- 我们轮流值周末班，这样每个人都有休息时间。
- 这个地区的天气经常在干燥晴朗和强降雨之间交替。
- 高度专注的时段应与短暂休息交替进行以保持效率。
- 学生应在紧张学习和充分休息之间交替以避免过度疲劳。
- 两位教授轮流主讲每周的系列讲座。
- 当地气候从炎热潮湿的夏季交替到寒冷干燥的冬季。
</details>

### 71. amass <a id="71-amass"></a>
**Etymology:** From French *amasser*, from *a-* ("to") + *masser* ("to gather in a mass"), from Latin *massa* ("mass, lump").

**词源：** 源自法语 *amasser*，源自 *a-*（向）+ *masser*（聚集），源自拉丁语 *massa*（块，团）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To gather together or accumulate (a large amount or quantity of something) over time.
- CN: 积累；积聚（大量财物、知识等）。

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Implies collecting a substantial amount; stronger than "gather" or "collect." — 暗示收集大量；比"gather"或"collect"语气更强
- Often associated with wealth, fortune, knowledge, or data. — 常与财富、财产、知识或数据相关
- Somewhat formal; less common in casual speech. — 较为正式；在 casual 口语中不太常见

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- amass a fortune / wealth
- amass knowledge / information
- amass a collection
- amass power / influence
- amass evidence
- amass data
- amass resources
- quickly amass


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 积累财富
- 积累知识/信息
- 积累收藏
- 积累权力/影响力
- 收集证据
- 积累数据
- 积累资源
- 迅速积累
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- amass from sth
- amass through sth
- amass over time
- amass by doing sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从……积累
- 通过……积累
- 随着时间积累
- 通过做某事积累
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Over the years, he amassed a vast collection of ancient coins. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: Through decades of research, the scientist amassed an enormous amount of data on climate patterns. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The museum has amassed one of the finest collections of Renaissance art in Europe. (TOEFL TPO 37)
- Academic: By mining social media data, the team amassed over 10 million user interactions for analysis. (Nature Communications 2020)
- Common: She amassed enough frequent flyer miles for a free trip to Japan.
- 介词搭配: He amassed a substantial fortune from his early investments in e-commerce startups.
- 介词搭配: The evidence was amassed over time through meticulous forensic investigation.
- 介词搭配: The research team amassed a vast collection of data by conducting surveys across multiple regions over five years.
- 介词搭配: The political leader amassed enormous wealth through corrupt business dealings during his time in office.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 多年来，他积累了大量的古钱币收藏。
- 通过数十年的研究，这位科学家积累了关于气候模式的巨量数据。
- 该博物馆积累了欧洲最精美的文艺复兴艺术收藏之一。
- 通过挖掘社交媒体数据，该团队收集了超过1000万用户互动以供分析。
- 她积累了足够的飞行里程，可以免费去日本旅行。
- 他通过早期投资电子商务初创公司积累了一笔可观的财富。
- 证据是通过细致的法医调查随着时间推移而积累起来的。
- 研究团队通过在多个地区进行为期五年的调查，积累了大量数据。
- 这位政治领导人在任职期间通过腐败的商业交易积累了巨额财富。
</details>

### 72. amaze <a id="72-amaze"></a>
**Etymology:** From Middle English *amasen*, from Old English *āmasian* ("to confuse, astonish"), from *a-* (intensive prefix) + *masian* ("to confound, perplex").

**词源：** 源自中古英语 *amasen*，源自古英语 *āmasian*（使困惑，使惊讶），源自 *a-*（强调前缀）+ *masian*（使迷惑，使困惑）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To cause someone to feel great surprise or wonder; to astonish.
- CN: 使惊奇；使惊诧；使惊叹。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Stronger than "surprise"; implies wonderment and admiration. — 比"surprise"更强烈；暗示惊叹和赞赏
- Often used in passive: "I am amazed at/by..." — 常用于被动语态："I am amazed at/by..."
- "Amazing" as an adjective is extremely common in informal speech. — "Amazing"作形容词在非正式口语中极其常见

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- amaze sb
- it amazes me that...
- truly amaze
- amazing achievement / talent / beauty
- never cease to amaze
- be amazed to find
- amaze the world
- simply amazing


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使某人惊奇
- 令我惊讶的是……
- 真正地令人惊奇
- 惊人的成就/天赋/美丽
- 总是令人惊讶
- 惊奇地发现
- 震惊世界
- 简直令人惊叹
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be amazed at sth
- be amazed by sth
- be amazed with sth
- be amazed at how


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 对……感到惊奇
- 被……所惊叹
- 对……感到惊讶
- 对……方式感到惊奇
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The magician's performance amazed everyone in the audience. (CET-4 2017-12)
- 考研: It amazes me how much progress she has made in such a short time. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Tourists never cease to be amazed by the architectural grandeur of the ancient city. (IELTS Cambridge 14)
- Academic: The researchers were amazed to discover that the bacteria could survive in boiling water. (Nature Microbiology 2021)
- Common: Her ability to speak six languages fluently never ceases to amaze me.
- 介词搭配: Tourists are amazed at the breathtaking views from the mountaintop.
- 介词搭配: Archaeologists were amazed by the remarkably well-preserved ancient artifacts discovered in the cave.
- 介词搭配: I was truly amazed with the level of craftsmanship in the antique furniture.
- 介词搭配: The botanists were amazed at how the rare plant species managed to survive in such harsh conditions.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 魔术师的表演让所有观众惊叹不已。
- 令我惊讶的是她在这么短的时间内取得了如此大的进步。
- 游客们无不为古城的宏伟建筑感到惊叹。
- 研究人员惊讶地发现这种细菌能在沸水中存活。
- 她能流利地说六种语言的能力总是让我惊叹不已。
- 游客们对山顶上令人叹为观止的景色感到惊奇。
- 考古学家被洞穴中发现的保存异常完好的古代器物所震惊。
- 我对古董家具的工艺水平感到由衷惊叹。
- 植物学家们对稀有植物物种在如此恶劣条件下生存的方式感到惊奇。
</details>

### 73. amend <a id="73-amend"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *amender*, from Latin *emendare* ("to free from fault, correct"), from *e-* ("out of") + *mendum* ("fault, defect").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *amender*，源自拉丁语 *emendare*（使无罪，纠正），源自 *e-*（出）+ *mendum*（错误，缺陷）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To make minor changes or improvements to a document, law, policy, or text; to correct or revise.
- CN: 修订；修正；改正（法律、文件、文本等）。

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Formal register; common in legal, legislative, and editorial contexts. — 正式语体；常见于法律、立法和编辑语境
- Not to be confused with "emend" (to correct a text, especially in textual criticism). — 不要与"emend"（校订文本，尤指文本批评）混淆
- In law, "to amend a constitution/contract/bill." — 在法律中："to amend a constitution/contract/bill"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- amend the law / constitution / rule
- amend a document / contract / agreement
- amend one's ways / behavior (formal)
- propose an amendment
- amended version
- amend a statement
- amend accordingly
- be subject to amendment


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 修改法律/宪法/规则
- 修改文件/合同/协议
- 改正某人的方式/行为
- 提出修正案
- 修订版
- 修改声明
- 相应修改
- 需修订
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- amend to sth
- amend by consensus
- amend through sth
- amend in accordance with
- amend from sth to sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 修改为……
- 通过共识修订
- 通过……修改
- 按照……修改
- 从……修改为……
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The committee voted to amend the rules regarding student conduct. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: The government amended the tax law to provide relief for small businesses. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The Constitution can only be amended through a formal process requiring broad consensus. (TOEFL TPO 49)
- Academic: The authors amended the manuscript to address the reviewers' comments before resubmission. (BMJ 2020)
- Common: He promised to amend his behavior after the warning from his boss.
- 介词搭配: The outdated clause in the agreement was amended to reflect modern business practices.
- 介词搭配: The company's internal policies should only be amended by consensus among senior management.
- 介词搭配: The education regulations were amended through a formal parliamentary process that took over a year.
- 介词搭配: The contract was amended in accordance with the latest changes in tax legislation.
- 介词搭配: The travel policy was amended from a strict no-remote-work rule to a flexible hybrid model.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 委员会投票修改了关于学生行为的规则。
- 政府修订了税法以向小企业提供减免。
- 宪法只能通过需要广泛共识的正式程序进行修订。
- 作者修改了手稿以回应审稿人的意见，然后重新提交。
- 在受到老板警告后，他承诺改正自己的行为。
- 协议中的过时条款被修改为反映现代商业实践。
- 公司内部政策只有通过高层管理人员的共识才能修改。
- 教育规定通过历时一年多的正式议会程序进行了修订。
- 合同按照税法的最新变化进行了修改。
- 差旅政策从严格的禁止远程办公规定修改为了灵活的混合办公模式。
</details>

### 74. amplify <a id="74-amplify"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *amplificare* ("to enlarge"), from *amplus* ("large, spacious") + *facere* ("to make, do").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *amplificare*（放大），源自 *amplus*（大的，宽敞的）+ *facere*（制作，做）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To increase the volume, strength, or magnitude of something; to add detail to a statement or narrative.
- CN: 放大；增强（声音、信号等）；详述；进一步阐述。

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Literal meaning: increase sound/volume (electronic amplification). — 字面义：增大声音/音量（电子放大）
- Figurative meaning: expand on an idea or statement (e.g., "amplify a point"). — 比喻义：详细阐述观点或陈述（例如："amplify a point"）
- In biology/genetics: "amplify DNA" (PCR amplification). — 在生物学/遗传学中："amplify DNA"（PCR扩增）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- amplify sound / signal / voice
- amplify a point / argument / statement
- amplify the impact / effect
- amplify concerns / fears
- amplify DNA
- amplify the message
- greatly amplify
- amplify the meaning
- amplify the noise


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 放大声音/信号/嗓音
- 进一步阐述观点/论点/陈述
- 放大影响/效果
- 加剧担忧/恐惧
- 扩增DNA
- 放大信息
- 大大放大
- 进一步阐明含义
- 放大噪音
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- amplify through sth
- amplify by sth
- amplify on/upon sth
- amplify with sth
- amplify into sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 通过……放大
- 通过……扩大
- 详细阐述某事
- 用……放大
- 扩大成为……
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: A microphone is used to amplify the speaker's voice so that everyone can hear. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: Social media platforms can both amplify and distort public opinion. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The device amplifies the electrical signals received from the sensors. (TOEFL TPO 40)
- Academic: Using PCR, the researchers amplified the target DNA sequence for further analysis. (Nature Protocols 2020)
- Common: He was asked to amplify on his earlier remarks about the company's future.
- 介词搭配: The radio signal is amplified through a network of relay stations.
- 介词搭配: The device amplifies the incoming audio signal by increasing its voltage.
- 介词搭配: During the lecture, the professor amplified on his earlier comments about climate change policy.
- 介词搭配: The faint whisper was amplified with a highly sensitive directional microphone.
- 介词搭配: What began as a minor disagreement quickly amplified into a major conflict between the two departments.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 麦克风用于放大演讲者的声音，以便每个人都能听到。
- 社交媒体平台既可以放大也可以扭曲公众舆论。
- 该设备放大从传感器接收的电信号。
- 使用PCR，研究人员扩增了目标DNA序列以供进一步分析。
- 他被要求进一步阐述他早先关于公司未来的评论。
- 无线电信号通过中继站网络被放大。
- 该设备通过增加电压来放大输入的音频信号。
- 在讲座中，教授详细阐述了他早先关于气候变化政策的评论。
- 微弱的低语用高灵敏度定向麦克风被放大了。
- 起初的小分歧迅速扩大成为两个部门之间的重大冲突。
</details>

### 75. amuse <a id="75-amuse"></a>
**Etymology:** From Middle French *amuser* ("to cause to muse, to divert"), from *a-* ("to") + *muser* ("to muse, stare idly"), perhaps from *mus* ("snout").

**词源：** 源自中古法语 *amuser*（使沉思，使消遣），源自 *a-*（向）+ *muser*（沉思，闲看），可能源自 *mus*（口鼻部）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To entertain or cause someone to laugh or smile; to provide enjoyment or keep occupied pleasantly.
- CN: 逗乐；逗笑；给……提供娱乐。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Less intense than "entertain"; implies gentle, lighthearted enjoyment. — 不如"entertain"强烈；暗示温和、轻松愉快的享受
- "Amused" can mean "slightly entertained" or even "mildly cynical" in context. — "Amused"可表示"略微被逗乐"，甚至在某些语境中表示"略带讽刺"
- Pattern: "amuse somebody with/by something." — 句型："amuse somebody with/by something"
- Reflexive use is common: "amuse oneself." — 反身用法常见："amuse oneself"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- amuse sb
- amuse oneself
- keep sb amused
- find it amusing that
- amuse the crowd
- greatly amused
- amuse guests
- mildly amused


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 逗某人开心
- 自娱自乐
- 让某人保持愉快
- 觉得……很有趣
- 逗乐人群
- 非常开心
- 招待客人
- 觉得有点好笑
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be amused at sth
- be amused by sth
- amuse sb with sth
- be amused with sth
- amuse sb by doing sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 被……逗乐
- 被……逗乐
- 用某事逗某人开心
- 对……感到有趣
- 通过做某事逗某人开心
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The clown amused the children by performing magic tricks. (CET-4 2017-06)
- 考研: Even during difficult times, she managed to amuse her friends with witty remarks. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: While waiting for the bus, the tourists amused themselves by taking photos of the street performers. (IELTS Cambridge 15)
- Academic: The study found that participants who were amused during the learning task retained information better than those who were not. (Journal of Educational Psychology 2020)
- Common: I brought a book to amuse myself during the long flight.
- 介词搭配: The audience was amused at the comedian's clever wordplay and witty punchlines.
- 介词搭配: The children were amused by the clown's funny antics at the birthday party.
- 介词搭配: The babysitter amused the children with a collection of colorful picture books and toys.
- 介词搭配: The young patients were amused with the hospital's new playroom and interactive games.
- 介词搭配: The host amused the guests by telling hilarious stories from his world travels.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 即使在困难时期，她也用机智的言语逗朋友开心。
- 等公交车时，游客们通过拍摄街头艺人的照片来娱乐自己。
- 研究发现，在学习任务中感到愉快的参与者比不愉快的参与者更好地记住了信息。
- 我带了本书在长途飞行中自娱自乐。
- 观众被喜剧演员巧妙的文字游戏和机智的笑话逗乐了。
- 孩子们被小丑在生日派对上滑稽的动作逗乐了。
- 保姆用一批五彩缤纷的图画书和玩具逗孩子们开心。
- 小患者们对医院的新游戏室和互动游戏感到有趣。
- 主人通过讲述他环球旅行中的趣事逗乐了客人。
</details>

### 76. analyze (also analyse) <a id="76-analyze"></a>
**Etymology:** From French *analyser*, from *analyse* ("analysis"), from Latin *analysis*, from Greek *analusis* ("a dissolving, breaking up"), from *ana-* ("up, throughout") + *lusis* ("a loosing").

**词源：** 源自法语 *analyser*，源自 *analyse*（分析），源自拉丁语 *analysis*，源自希腊语 *analusis*（分解，瓦解），源自 *ana-*（向上，遍及）+ *lusis*（松开）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To examine something in detail in order to understand its structure, nature, or meaning; to perform a systematic investigation.
- CN: 分析；解析；详细研究。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Spelling: "analyze" (American English), "analyse" (British English). — 拼写："analyze"（美式英语），"analyse"（英式英语）
- Widely used across academic disciplines: data analysis, chemical analysis, literary analysis, etc. — 广泛用于各学科：数据分析、化学分析、文学分析等
- Stronger than "examine"; implies methodological/systematic breakdown. — 比"examine"更强烈；暗示有条理/系统性地分解

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- analyze data / results / information
- analyze the situation / problem
- analyze a text / poem / work
- carefully / thoroughly analyze
- analyze the cause
- analyze statistically
- analyze and evaluate


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 分析数据/结果/信息
- 分析情况/问题
- 分析文本/诗歌/作品
- 仔细/彻底分析
- 分析原因
- 统计分析
- 分析与评估
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- analyze in depth / detail
- analyze from multiple angles
- analyze for sth
- analyze by sth
- analyze through sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 深入/详细分析
- 从多角度分析
- 为……而分析
- 通过……分析
- 通过……来分析
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The report analyzes the impact of social media on teenagers' mental health. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The researcher spent months analyzing the survey responses to identify patterns. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: In the essay, students are required to analyze the causes of the Industrial Revolution. (TOEFL TPO 34)
- Academic: We employed a machine learning algorithm to analyze gene expression data from over 5,000 patients. (Cell 2021)
- Common: The therapist helped her analyze the root causes of her anxiety.
- 介词搭配: The researcher analyzed the survey responses in depth to identify meaningful patterns.
- 介词搭配: The complex issue should be analyzed from multiple angles before reaching a conclusion.
- 介词搭配: Soil samples were analyzed for traces of pesticide residues.
- 介词搭配: The experimental data was analyzed by statistical methods to determine its significance.
- 介词搭配: The team analyzed the patient's symptoms through a comprehensive series of diagnostic tests.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该报告分析了社交媒体对青少年心理健康的影响。
- 研究人员花了数月时间分析调查回复以识别模式。
- 在文章中，学生需要分析工业革命的原因。
- 我们采用机器学习算法分析了来自5000多名患者的基因表达数据。
- 治疗师帮助她分析了焦虑的根本原因。
- 研究人员深入分析了调查回复以识别有意义的模式。
- 在得出结论之前，应该从多角度分析这个复杂的问题。
- 对土壤样本进行了农药残留痕量分析。
- 实验数据通过统计方法进行分析以确定其显著性。
- 团队通过一系列全面的诊断测试分析了患者的症状。
</details>

### 77. anchor <a id="77-anchor"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old English *ancor*, from Latin *ancora*, from Greek *ankura* ("anchor"), from *ankos* ("a bend, curve").

**词源：** 源自古英语 *ancor*，源自拉丁语 *ancora*，源自希腊语 *ankura*（锚），源自 *ankos*（弯曲，曲线）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: (vt.) To secure firmly in position; to serve as a stabilizing force. (vi.) To drop anchor; to become fixed. (n.) A heavy device used to moor a ship.
- CN: (vt.) 抛锚；使固定；使稳固；主持（节目）。(vi.) 抛锚；固定。(n.) 锚；主持人。

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. & n.

**Usage Notes:**

- Multiple meanings: nautical (literal), metaphorical (emotional anchor), broadcasting (news anchor), and computing (anchor element in HTML). — 多种含义：航海（字面义）、比喻义（情感支柱）、广播（新闻主播）和计算机（HTML中的锚元素）
- In broadcasting, "to anchor a show" means to serve as the main host. — 在广播领域，"to anchor a show"指担任主要主持人
- Often used with "in" or "to": "anchored in reality," "anchored to the ground." — 常与"in"或"to"连用："anchored in reality"，"anchored to the ground"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- anchor a ship / boat
- anchor a news program / show
- anchor in place
- anchor the team
- anchor the economy
- securely anchored
- anchor point


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 抛锚停船
- 主持新闻节目
- 固定到位
- 成为团队的核心
- 稳定经济
- 牢牢固定
- 锚点；固定点
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be anchored to sth
- be anchored in sth
- anchor one's hope on sth
- anchor in sth
- anchor sth to sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 被固定在……上
- 扎根于……；基于……
- 把希望寄托在……上
- 在……抛锚
- 将某物固定在某物上
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The ship anchored in the harbor to wait out the storm. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: Traditional values often anchor a community during times of rapid change. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Coral reefs anchor themselves to the ocean floor and provide a habitat for marine life. (TOEFL TPO 50)
- Academic: The cables were anchored to the seabed using concrete blocks to ensure stability. (Engineering Structures 2021)
- Common: She anchors the evening news on the local television station.
- 介词搭配: The massive cargo ship was anchored to the dock by thick steel cables during the storm.
- 介词搭配: The novel is anchored in the author's personal experiences during the war.
- 介词搭配: The local community anchored its hope on the newly elected mayor to revive the struggling economy.
- 介词搭配: The fishing fleet anchored in the protected bay to wait out the approaching storm.
- 介词搭配: The tent was securely anchored to the ground with heavy metal stakes to withstand the strong winds.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 船在港口抛锚以等待暴风雨过去。
- 在快速变化的时代，传统价值观往往能稳定一个社区。
- 珊瑚礁固定在海底，为海洋生物提供栖息地。
- 电缆用混凝土块固定在海底以确保稳定性。
- 她在当地电视台主持晚间新闻。
- 暴风雨期间，这艘巨型货轮用粗钢缆牢牢固定在码头上。
- 这部小说基于作者在战争期间的个人经历。
- 当地社区将希望寄托在新当选的市长身上以振兴陷入困境的经济。
- 捕鱼船队在避风海湾抛锚以等待即将到来的暴风雨过去。
- 帐篷用沉重的金属桩牢牢固定在地上以抵御强风。
</details>

### 78. anger <a id="78-anger"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old Norse *angr* ("grief, sorrow") and *angra* ("to grieve, vex"), from Proto-Germanic *angazaz* ("narrow, tight, painful"). Related to "anguish" and "anxiety."

**词源：** 源自古诺尔斯语 *angr*（悲伤，忧愁）和 *angra*（使悲伤，使烦恼），源自原始日耳曼语 *angazaz*（狭窄的，紧的，痛苦的）。与“anguish”和“anxiety”相关。

**Definitions:**

- EN: (vt.) To make someone angry; to provoke or irritate. (n.) A strong feeling of displeasure or hostility.
- CN: (vt.) 使生气；激怒。(n.) 愤怒；怒火。

**Part of Speech:** vt. & n.

**Usage Notes:**

- As a verb, less common than the noun in everyday speech ("make someone angry" is more common than "anger someone"). — 作动词时在日常口语中不如名词常见（"make someone angry"比"anger someone"更常用）
- The verb is more formal or literary than the noun. — 动词比名词更正式或更具文学性
- Passive: "He was angered by the accusation." — 被动语态："He was angered by the accusation."

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- anger sb
- express / control / suppress one's anger
- anger management
- in a fit of anger
- anger easily
- public anger
- anger the public
- provoke one's anger


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 激怒某人
- 表达/控制/压抑某人的愤怒
- 愤怒管理
- 一怒之下
- 容易生气
- 公众的愤怒
- 激怒公众
- 激起某人的愤怒
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be angered by sth
- be angered at sth
- be angered over sth
- anger sb with sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 被……激怒
- 因……而生气
- 因……而愤怒
- 因某事激怒某人
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The unfair decision angered many students on campus. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: The government's failure to address the crisis angered the general public. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The destruction of the historical building angered local residents and preservationists alike. (TOEFL TPO 43)
- Academic: The findings suggest that prolonged exposure to online harassment increases cortisol levels and anger responses. (Journal of Behavioral Medicine 2020)
- Common: His rude comments angered everyone in the room.
- 介词搭配: She was angered at the blatant disregard for basic safety regulations in the workplace.
- 介词搭配: Environmental activists were angered by the company's decision to dump industrial waste into the river.
- 介词搭配: Local farmers were angered over the government's unreasonable delay in providing disaster relief.
- 介词搭配: The manager angered his entire staff with the unfair distribution of annual bonuses.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这个不公平的决定激怒了校园里的许多学生。
- 政府未能解决危机激怒了公众。
- 历史建筑的拆除激怒了当地居民和环保人士。
- 研究结果表明，长期暴露于网络骚扰会增加皮质醇水平和愤怒反应。
- 他粗鲁的评论激怒了房间里的每个人。
- 她对工作场所公然无视基本安全规定感到愤怒。
- 环保活动家被公司向河流倾倒工业废物的决定激怒了。
- 当地农民对政府不合理地拖延提供救灾感到愤怒。
- 经理因年终奖分配不公而激怒了全体职员。
</details>

### 79. animate <a id="79-animate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *animatus*, past participle of *animare* ("to give life to, fill with breath"), from *anima* ("soul, breath, life").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *animatus*，*animare*（赋予生命，注入气息）的过去分词，源自 *anima*（灵魂，呼吸，生命）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: (vt.) To bring to life; to give motion, energy, or spirit to something. (adj.) Alive; having life.
- CN: (vt.) 使有生命；使活跃；激励；制作动画。(adj.) 有生命的；活的。

**Part of Speech:** vt. (also adj.)

**Usage Notes:**

- Pronunciation differs: verb /ˈænɪmeɪt/ vs. adjective /ˈænɪmət/. — 发音不同：动词 /ˈænɪmeɪt/ vs 形容词 /ˈænɪmət/
- Common in film/animation contexts ("animated film," "computer-animated"). — 常见于电影/动画领域（"animated film"，"computer-animated"）
- Figurative meaning: "animate a discussion" (to enliven it). — 比喻义："animate a discussion"（使讨论活跃）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- animate a film / character
- animated discussion / debate / conversation
- computer-animated / hand-animated
- animate the conversation
- animated expression
- animate and engage
- real-time animated


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 制作动画电影/角色
- 热烈的讨论/辩论/谈话
- 电脑动画/手工动画
- 活跃谈话气氛
- 生动的表情
- 激励和吸引
- 实时动画的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be animated by sth
- be animated with sth
- animate sb with sth
- animate by a spirit of


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 受……驱动；被……赋予生命
- 因……而活跃
- 用某事激励某人
- 受……精神驱动
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The children were delighted by the animated cartoon characters on screen. (CET-4 2017-06)
- 考研: Her enthusiasm animated the entire team, inspiring everyone to work harder. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Pixar's films are known for their ability to animate inanimate objects with distinct personalities. (TOEFL TPO 41)
- Academic: The researcher used the software to animate the molecular docking process for the presentation. (Nature Methods 2020)
- Common: A lively debate animated the classroom for the entire hour.
- 介词搭配: The entire project was animated by a spirit of innovation and collaboration.
- 介词搭配: Her voice was animated with genuine excitement as she described her research findings.
- 介词搭配: The motivational speaker animated the tired audience with an inspiring story of perseverance and success.
- 介词搭配: The community project was animated by a spirit of cooperation and mutual support.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">

- 皮克斯电影以其赋予无生命物体鲜明个性的动画能力而闻名。
- 研究人员使用该软件为演示制作了分子对接过程的动画。
- 一场热烈的辩论使整个教室活跃了一个小时。
- 整个项目在创新与合作的精神驱动下充满活力。
- 她在描述自己的研究发现时，声音里洋溢着真切的兴奋。
- 这位励志演讲者用关于毅力和成功的鼓舞人心的故事激励了疲惫的听众。
- 这个社区项目在合作与互助的精神驱动下充满活力。

### 80. annex <a id="80-annex"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *annexus*, past participle of *annectere* ("to bind to, attach"), from *ad-* ("to") + *nectere* ("to bind, tie").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *annexus*，*annectere*（绑定，附加）的过去分词，源自 *ad-*（向）+ *nectere*（捆绑，系）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: (vt.) To append or add as an extra part; to take possession of territory by force or conquest. (n.) A building added to an existing one.
- CN: (vt.) 附加；添加；吞并（领土或国家）。(n.) 附属建筑；附件。

**Part of Speech:** vt. (also n.)

**Usage Notes:**

- Strong political connotation: "annex territory" implies seizure, often by military force. — 强烈的政治含义："annex territory"暗示夺取，通常通过军事力量
- In building/architecture: "annex" as a noun is an extension or wing of a building. — 在建筑学中："annex"作名词指建筑的扩建部分或侧翼
- The verb is formal; the noun is neutral. — 动词是正式的；名词是中性的

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- annex territory / land / a region
- annex a document (as attachment)
- building annex / school annex
- illegal annexation
- formally annexed
- attempted annexation
- annex resources


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 吞并领土/土地/地区
- 附加文件
- 附属建筑/学校附属楼
- 非法吞并
- 正式被吞并
- 试图吞并
- 吞并资源
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be annexed to sth
- annex by force
- annex sth to sth
- annex from sth
- annex into sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 被并入/附加到……
- 武力吞并
- 将某物并入/附加到某物
- 从……吞并
- 并入……
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The country was criticized for attempting to annex the neighboring region. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: Historical records show that the empire annexed several smaller kingdoms during its expansion. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The library annex was built to house the growing collection of rare manuscripts. (TOEFL TPO 36)
- Academic: The protocol was annexed to the main agreement and signed separately by the participating nations. (International Law Review 2021)
- Common: We held the meeting in the annex since the main building was under renovation.
- 介词搭配: The small coastal territory was annexed to the larger nation after the historic referendum.
- 介词搭配: The disputed region was annexed by force during the military conflict.
- 介词搭配: The conquering general annexed the rich agricultural lands to the expanding empire.
- 介词搭配: The empire annexed vast territories from its neighboring kingdoms during the 18th century.
- 介词搭配: The newly conquered provinces were gradually annexed into the empire's administrative and legal system.
</details>

<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 历史记录显示，该帝国在扩张过程中吞并了几个较小的王国。
- 图书馆附属楼是为了收藏日益增多的稀有手稿而建的。
- 议定书附在主协议之后，由参与国分别签署。
- 我们在附属楼开会，因为主楼正在翻修。
- 这个沿海小领土在历史性公投后被并入了较大的国家。
- 争议地区在军事冲突期间被武力吞并。
- 征服将军将肥沃的农业用地并入了不断扩张的帝国。
- 该帝国在18世纪从其邻国吞并了广阔的领土。
- 新被征服的省份逐渐被并入了帝国的行政和法律体系。
</details>

### 81. announce <a id="81-announce"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *anuncier*, from Latin *annuntiare* ("to report, give news of"), from *ad-* ("to") + *nuntiare* ("to announce, report"), from *nuntius* ("messenger").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *anuncier*，源自拉丁语 *annuntiare*（报告，告知消息），源自 *ad-*（向）+ *nuntiare*（宣布，报告），源自 *nuntius*（信使）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To make a public or formal statement about something; to declare officially.
- CN: 宣布；公布；通告。

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Typically formal/official: governments, companies, institutions announce. — 通常为正式/官方：政府、公司、机构宣布
- Pattern: "announce that," "announce something," "announce to somebody." — 句型："announce that"，"announce something"，"announce to somebody"
- Can be used more informally for personal news ("she announced her engagement"). — 也可用于较非正式的个人消息（"she announced her engagement"）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- announce a plan / decision / result
- announce the launch / arrival / opening
- publicly / officially announce
- it is with great pleasure that we announce
- announce one's intention / engagement / resignation
- announce a breakthrough
- announce candidacy
- formally announce
- announce the winner


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 宣布计划/决定/结果
- 宣布启动/到达/开幕
- 公开/正式宣布
- 我们非常荣幸地宣布
- 宣布某人的意图/订婚/辞职
- 宣布突破
- 宣布参选
- 正式宣布
- 宣布获胜者
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- announce to sb
- announce sth as sth
- announce in advance
- announce on sth
- announce at a press conference


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 向某人宣布
- 宣布某物为……
- 提前宣布
- 在……上宣布
- 在新闻发布会上宣布
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The university announced that tuition fees would remain unchanged for the next academic year. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: The company announced a partnership with a leading tech firm to develop AI solutions. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The airline announced the delayed departure of Flight 237 to Chicago. (IELTS Cambridge 13)
- Academic: NASA announced the discovery of a new exoplanet within the habitable zone of its star. (Nature Astronomy 2021)
- Common: They announced their wedding date at the family dinner.
- 介词搭配: The principal announced the new policy to all students during the morning assembly.
- 介词搭配: The new product line was announced as a major breakthrough in sustainable packaging technology.
- 介词搭配: The date of the final examination will be announced in advance to allow students adequate preparation time.
- 介词搭配: The company's quarterly financial results were officially announced on their corporate website.
- 介词搭配: The winner of the award will be announced at a press conference next Friday.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 大学宣布下学年的学费保持不变。
- 该公司宣布与一家领先科技公司合作开发人工智能解决方案。
- 航空公司宣布飞往芝加哥的237航班延误起飞。
- NASA宣布在其恒星的宜居带内发现了一颗新的系外行星。
- 他们在家庭晚宴上宣布了婚期。
- 校长在晨会上向全体学生宣布了新政策。
- 新产品线被宣布为可持续包装技术的一项重大突破。
- 期末考试日期将提前公布，以便学生有充足的准备时间。
- 公司的季度财务业绩在其官方网站上正式公布。
- 获奖者将在下周五的新闻发布会上公布。
</details>

### 82. annoy <a id="82-annoy"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *anoier* ("to tire, vex"), from Late Latin *inodiare* ("to make hateful"), from Latin *in odio esse* ("to be hated"), from *odium* ("hatred").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *anoier*（使疲倦，使烦恼），源自晚期拉丁语 *inodiare*（使可恨），源自拉丁语 *in odio esse*（被憎恨），源自 *odium*（憎恨）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To cause slight anger or irritation; to bother or disturb someone.
- CN: 使烦恼；使生气；打扰。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Milder than "anger"; implies repeated or persistent irritation rather than intense rage. — 比"anger"温和；暗示反复或持续的烦扰而非强烈的愤怒
- Pattern: "annoy somebody" or "be annoyed with somebody/about something." — 句型："annoy somebody"或"be annoyed with somebody/about something"
- "It annoys me that..." is a common construction. — "It annoys me that..."是常见结构

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- annoy sb
- find it annoying
- annoy the hell out of (informal)
- annoying habit / noise / person
- slightly annoyed
- visibly annoyed
- annoying delay
- easily annoyed


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使某人生气
- 觉得……烦人
- 烦死人了
- 烦人的习惯/噪音/人
- 有点恼火
- 明显恼火
- 烦人的延误
- 容易生气
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be annoyed with sb
- be annoyed at sth
- be annoyed by sth
- be annoyed about sth
- annoy sb with sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 对某人生气
- 因某事而恼火
- 被某事烦扰
- 对某事感到恼火
- 用某事烦扰某人
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The constant noise from the construction site annoyed the residents. (CET-4 2017-12)
- 考研: She was annoyed by the frequent interruptions during her online class. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Mosquitoes annoy tourists more than any other insect in tropical regions. (TOEFL TPO 32)
- Academic: The study found that background office chatter annoys employees more than loud machinery noise. (Journal of Environmental Psychology 2020)
- Common: It annoys me when people are late without letting me know.
- 介词搭配: She was annoyed with herself for forgetting such an important appointment.
- 介词搭配: The professor was annoyed at the students' constant texting during his lecture.
- 介词搭配: The manager was annoyed by the repeated errors in the monthly financial reports.
- 介词搭配: The passengers were annoyed about the lack of clear information regarding the flight delay.
- 介词搭配: The younger brother annoyed his older sister with endless silly questions during the road trip.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 建筑工地持续不断的噪音使居民感到烦恼。
- 她对网课上频繁的打扰感到恼火。
- 在热带地区，蚊子比其他任何昆虫都更让游客烦恼。
- 研究发现，办公室的背景聊天声比嘈杂的机器噪音更让员工烦恼。
- 当别人迟到却不告诉我时，我很恼火。
- 她因为忘记这么重要的约会而生自己的气。
- 教授对学生们在课上不停发短信感到恼火。
- 经理被月度财务报表中反复出现的错误所烦扰。
- 乘客们对航班延误缺乏明确信息感到恼火。
- 弟弟在公路旅行中用没完没了的傻问题烦扰他的姐姐。
</details>

### 83. answer <a id="83-answer"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old English *andswaru* ("a response, reply"), from Proto-Germanic *andaswarō* ("an answering, a swearing against"), from *anda-* ("against") + *swarjan* ("to swear"). The verb evolved from the noun.

**词源：** 源自古英语 *andswaru*（回应，答复），源自原始日耳曼语 *andaswarō*（回答，宣誓反对），源自 *anda-*（反对）+ *swarjan*（宣誓）。动词由名词演变而来。

**Definitions:**

- EN: (vt./vi.) To respond to a question, call, or request; to be responsible or accountable. (n.) A reply or solution.
- CN: (vt./vi.) 回答；回应；接听（电话）；应对。(n.) 答案；回复。

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi. & n.

**Usage Notes:**

- One of the most common English verbs. Can be transitive or intransitive. — 英语中最常见的动词之一。可及物或不及物
- "Answer the phone/door" — to respond to the ringing or knocking.- "Answer for something" — to be responsible or accountable.
- "Answer to somebody" — to be responsible to a higher authority.

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- answer a question / the phone / the door
- answer back (talk back rudely)
- answer the call (respond to a summons)
- answer in writing
- answer directly
- answer with confidence
- refuse to answer


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 回答问题/接电话/开门
- 顶嘴
- 响应号召
- 书面答复
- 直接回答
- 自信地回答
- 拒绝回答
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- answer to sb (be accountable)
- answer for sth (take responsibility)
- answer with sth
- answer in sth
- answer by doing sth
- answer to sth (respond to)


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 对某人负责
- 为某事负责
- 以……回答
- 以……方式回答
- 通过做某事回应
- 回应某事
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Students are required to answer at least three questions in the oral exam. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: The CEO refused to answer questions about the company's financial losses. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Please answer the following questions based on the lecture you just heard. (TOEFL TPO 39)
- Academic: The experiment was designed to answer a fundamental question about cellular metabolism. (Cell Metabolism 2021)
- Common: No one was home, so nobody answered the door.
- 介词搭配: The mid-level manager answers to the regional director for all operational matters.
- 介词搭配: The CEO must answer for the company's failure to meet its financial targets.
- 介词搭配: He answered the criticism with a detailed explanation of his actions.
- 介词搭配: Please answer in complete sentences during the oral examination.
- 介词搭配: The fire department answered the emergency call by dispatching three fire trucks immediately.
- 介词搭配: The government must answer to the growing public demand for stronger environmental protections.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 学生需要在口试中至少回答三个问题。
- CEO拒绝回答有关公司财务亏损的问题。
- 请根据你刚刚听到的讲座回答以下问题。
- 该实验旨在回答关于细胞代谢的一个基本问题。
- 没人在家，所以没有人应门。
- 这位中层经理就所有运营事务向区域总监负责。
- CEO必须为公司未能达到财务目标而负责。
- 他以对自己行为的详细解释回应了批评。
- 在口试中请用完整的句子回答。
- 消防部门通过立即派出三辆消防车来响应紧急呼叫。
- 政府必须回应公众日益强烈的加强环境保护的诉求。
</details>

### 84. anticipate <a id="84-anticipate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *anticipatus*, past participle of *anticipare* ("to take before, forestall"), from *ante* ("before") + *capere* ("to take").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *anticipatus*，*anticipare*（预先采取，先发制人）的过去分词，源自 *ante*（在…之前）+ *capere*（拿取）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To expect or foresee something and take action in preparation; to look forward to.
- CN: 预期；预料；期望；提前准备。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Stronger than "expect"; implies active preparation or forethought. — 比"expect"更强烈；暗示积极的准备或预先考虑
- Pattern: "anticipate something" or "anticipate that" or "anticipate doing something." — 句型："anticipate something"或"anticipate that"或"anticipate doing something"
- Common in business: "anticipate demand/needs/challenges." — 商业中常见："anticipate demand/needs/challenges"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- anticipate a problem / need / change
- anticipate demand / growth
- eagerly / fully anticipate
- anticipate the worst
- anticipate doing sth
- anticipate a question
- anticipate results
- correctly anticipate


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 预见到问题/需求/变化
- 预期需求/增长
- 热切/充分期待
- 做最坏的打算
- 预期做某事
- 预见到问题
- 预期结果
- 正确地预见
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- anticipate with excitement
- anticipate sth with sth
- anticipate by doing sth
- anticipate from sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 兴奋地期待
- 以……心情期待某事
- 通过做某事提前准备
- 从……预料
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The company anticipated a surge in demand and increased production accordingly. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: By analyzing market trends, investors can anticipate potential risks before they materialize. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Engineers anticipated the structural weaknesses and reinforced the bridge accordingly. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The models anticipated a 2°C temperature rise by 2050 under current emission scenarios. (Nature Climate Change 2020)
- Common: We anticipate having the report ready by Friday.
- 介词搭配: The children anticipated the summer vacation with great excitement.
- 介词搭配: The experienced investor anticipated the market crash by selling his shares in advance.
- 介词搭配: The final results were far better than what we anticipated from the preliminary data analysis.
- 介词搭配: The students anticipated the summer vacation with great excitement.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 工程师预见到了结构弱点并相应加固了桥梁。
- 模型预测在当前排放情景下，到2050年温度将上升2°C。
- 我们预计在周五前准备好报告。
- 孩子们怀着极大的兴奋期待暑假的到来。
- 这位经验丰富的投资者通过提前抛售股票预判到了市场崩盘并做好准备。
- 最终结果比我们从初步数据分析中预期的要好得多。
- 学生们怀着极大的兴奋期待着暑假的到来。

### 85. apologize (also apologise) <a id="85-apologize"></a>
**Etymology:** From Greek *apologia* ("a speech in defense"), from *apo-* ("away from, off") + *logos* ("speech, reason"). English usage shifted from "defend" to "express regret" in the 1590s.

**词源：** 源自希腊语 *apologia*（辩护词），源自 *apo-*（离开）+ *logos*（言语，理性）。英语用法在16世纪90年代从“辩护”转变为“表达遗憾”。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To express regret for a wrongdoing, fault, or offense; to make an apology.
- CN: 道歉；认错；谢罪。

**Part of Speech:** vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Always intransitive; pattern: "apologize to somebody for something." — 总是不及物；句型："apologize to somebody for something"
- Never used transitively (do NOT say "apologize somebody"; say "apologize to somebody"). — 绝不可及物（不要说"apologize somebody"；要说"apologize to somebody"）
- Spelling: "apologize" (American), "apologise" (British). — 拼写："apologize"（美式），"apologise"（英式）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- sincerely / publicly / formally apologize
- apologize profusely
- I apologize (for...)
- apologize unreservedly
- humbly apologize


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 真诚/公开/正式道歉
- 再三道歉
- 我（为……）道歉
- 毫无保留地道歉
- 虚心道歉
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- apologize to sb
- apologize for sth / doing sth
- apologize in advance
- apologize on behalf of sb
- apologize to sb for sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 向某人道歉
- 为某事/做某事道歉
- 提前道歉
- 代表……道歉
- 因某事向某人道歉
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The company apologized to customers for the delay in delivery. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: The politician publicly apologized for his inappropriate remarks during the debate. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: If you arrive late for an interview, you should apologize briefly and then proceed. (IELTS Cambridge 16)
- Academic: The journal issued a formal retraction and apologized for the publication of erroneous data. (Nature 2020)
- Common: I apologize for the confusion — I should have explained more clearly.
- 介词搭配: The company spokesperson apologized to the public after the scandal broke.
- 介词搭配: He sincerely apologized for arriving late to the important business meeting.
- 介词搭配: I apologize in advance if my questions seem too personal.
- 介词搭配: The lawyer apologized on behalf of his client for the disrespectful remarks.
- 介词搭配: The government officially apologized to the affected residents for the delays in disaster relief.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 公司因延迟交货向客户道歉。
- 这位政治家为他在辩论中的不当言论公开道歉。

- 我为造成的困惑道歉——我本应该解释得更清楚。
- 丑闻曝光后，公司发言人向公众道歉。
- 他为重要商务会议的迟到而诚恳道歉。
- 如果我的问题显得过于私人，我提前道歉。
- 律师代表其当事人为不当言论道歉。
- 政府因救灾延误向受灾居民正式道歉。
</details>

### 86. appal (also appall) <a id="86-appal"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *apalir* ("to grow pale"), from *a-* ("to") + *palir* ("to grow pale"), from Latin *pallere* ("to be pale").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *apalir*（变得苍白），源自 *a-*（向）+ *palir*（变得苍白），源自拉丁语 *pallere*（苍白的）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To greatly dismay or horrify someone; to shock deeply.
- CN: 使惊骇；使震惊；使恐惧。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Very strong — implies profound shock and horror, not mild surprise.
- Spelling: "appal" (British, also "appals," "appalling"), "appall" (American, "appalls," "appalling"). — 拼写："appal"（英式，"appals""appalling"），"appall"（美式，"appalls""appalling"）
- Often used in passive: "I was appalled at/by..." — 常用于被动语态："I was appalled at/by..."

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- appalling behavior / conditions / treatment
- it appalls me that
- appalling lack of sth
- appalling crime
- morally appalled
- rightly appalled
- appalling weather


<details>

- 理应感到震惊
- 糟糕透顶的天气
</details>

- be appalled by sth

- 人权活动家被拘留中心政治犯受到的待遇所震惊。

### 87. appeal <a id="87-appeal"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *apeler* ("to call upon, appeal"), from Latin *appellare* ("to address, call upon, entreat").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *apeler*（呼吁，上诉），源自拉丁语 *appellare*（称呼，呼吁，恳求）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: (vi.) To make a serious or urgent request; to be attractive or interesting. (vt.) To apply to a higher court for review. (n.) An urgent request; attractiveness.
- CN: (vi.) 呼吁；恳求；有吸引力。 (vt.) 上诉。(n.) 呼吁；吸引力；上诉。

**Part of Speech:** vi. & vt. & n.

**Usage Notes:**

- Three major meanings: (1) "appeal to somebody for something" (earnest request), (2) "appeal to somebody" (be attractive), (3) legal "appeal a decision." — 三种主要含义：（1）"appeal to somebody for something"（恳切请求），（2）"appeal to somebody"（有吸引力），（3）法律"appeal a decision"（上诉）
- The verb is usually intransitive for meanings (1) and (2): "appeal to." — 含义（1）和（2）时动词通常不及物："appeal to"
- For meaning (3/legal), the verb can be transitive: "appeal the verdict." — 含义（3/法律）时动词可及物："appeal the verdict"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- have / hold an appeal for
- make an appeal
- appeal directly
- universal appeal


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 对……有吸引力
- 发出呼吁
- 直接呼吁
- 普遍的吸引力
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- appeal to sb
- appeal for help / support / donations
- appeal against a decision
- appeal a decision
- appeal strongly to
- appeal on the grounds of
- appeal to sb for sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 对某人有吸引力；向某人呼吁
- 呼吁帮助/支持/捐款
- 对判决提出上诉
- 对判决提出上诉
- 强烈吸引/呼吁
- 以……为由上诉
- 为某事向某人呼吁
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The charity appealed to the public to donate money for disaster relief. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The idea of working remotely appeals to many young professionals. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The defendant decided to appeal the court's decision to a higher court. (IELTS Cambridge 14)
- Academic: The researchers appeal for greater international cooperation in addressing antimicrobial resistance. (The Lancet 2020)
- Common: Jazz music has always appealed to me more than pop.
- 介词搭配: The concept of sustainable living appeals to an increasing number of environmentally conscious consumers.
- 介词搭配: The non-profit organization appealed for urgent donations to support the earthquake relief efforts.
- 介词搭配: The losing candidate decided to appeal against the election results in court.
- 介词搭配: The defendant chose not to appeal the court's decision and accepted the sentence.
- 介词搭配: The promise of a better career opportunities appeals strongly to young professionals seeking growth.
- 介词搭配: The company appealed on the grounds that the verdict was not supported by sufficient evidence.
- 介词搭配: The environmental group appealed to the government for stricter regulations on industrial emissions.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 慈善机构呼吁公众为救灾捐款。
- 远程工作的想法吸引了许多年轻专业人士。
- 被告决定向上级法院对判决提出上诉。
- 研究人员呼吁加强国际合作以应对抗菌素耐药性。
- 爵士乐一直比流行音乐更吸引我。
- 可持续生活的理念吸引着越来越多具有环保意识的消费者。
- 非营利组织呼吁紧急捐款以支持地震救援工作。
- 落选候选人决定就选举结果向法院提出上诉。
- 被告选择不对法院判决提出上诉并接受了量刑。
- 更好的职业机会承诺强烈吸引着寻求成长的年轻专业人士。
- 该公司以判决缺乏充分证据为由提出上诉。
- 环保组织呼吁政府对工业排放实施更严格的规定。
</details>

### 88. appear <a id="88-appear"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *apareir*, from Latin *apparere* ("to appear, come into sight"), from *ad-* ("to") + *parere* ("to come forth, be visible").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *apareir*，源自拉丁语 *apparere*（出现，显现），源自 *ad-*（向）+ *parere*（出现，可见）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: (vi.) To come into sight or become visible; to seem or give the impression of being; to perform or present oneself publicly.
- CN: (vi.) 出现；显现；似乎；看来；登台表演；出版。

**Part of Speech:** vi. (linking v.)

**Usage Notes:**

- Can function as a linking verb (copula): "appear to be," "appear + adjective" ("appear calm"). — 可作系动词："appear to be"，"appear + 形容词"（"appear calm"）- "Appear" differs from "seem": "appear" can imply visual impression; "seem" implies subjective judgment. — "Appear"与"seem"不同："appear"可暗示视觉印象；"seem"暗示主观判断
- Pattern: "it appears that," "appear to do," "appear in/on/at." — 句型："it appears that"，"appear to do"，"appear in/on/at"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- appear to be
- it appears that
- appear suddenly
- appear likely to
- appear on the surface


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 似乎是
- 似乎……
- 突然出现
- 很可能……
- 表面上看来
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- appear in sth
- appear on sth
- appear at sth
- appear in court
- appear as sth
- appear before the court
- appear in public
- appear to sb


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 出现在……中
- 出现在……上
- 出现在……处
- 出庭
- 表现为……
- 出庭
- 公开露面
- 对某人而言显得……
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The results appear to support the hypothesis proposed by the researchers. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: The problem appears more complex than originally thought. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The singer appeared on stage to thunderous applause from the audience. (TOEFL TPO 35)
- Academic: A new variant of the virus appears to be more transmissible, according to preliminary data. (Nature Medicine 2021)
- Common: He appeared calm despite the difficult situation.
- 介词搭配: The renowned actor has appeared in over forty films throughout his distinguished career.
- 介词搭配: The breaking news story appeared on the front page of every major newspaper.
- 介词搭配: The famous singer appeared at the charity gala to show support for the cause.
- 介词搭配: The key witness was required to appear in court and testify under oath.
- 介词搭配: The early symptoms of the disease appeared as a mild fever and fatigue.
- 介词搭配: The celebrity rarely appears in public without a full security team.
- 介词搭配: The suspect is scheduled to appear before the court for the first hearing next week.
- 介词搭配: The solution to this complex problem appears quite simple to the experienced engineer.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 结果似乎支持研究人员提出的假设。
- 这个问题似乎比最初想象的更复杂。
- 歌手出现在舞台上，观众报以雷鸣般的掌声。
- 根据初步数据，一种新的病毒变体似乎更具传染性。
- 尽管处境艰难，他看起来仍然冷静。
- 这位著名演员在其辉煌的职业生涯中出演过四十多部电影。
- 这则突发新闻报道出现在各大报纸的头版。
- 这位著名歌手出现在慈善晚会上以示对这项事业的支持。
- 关键证人被要求出庭并宣誓作证。
- 该疾病的早期症状表现为轻微发热和疲劳。
- 这位名人很少不带完整安保团队出现在公共场合。
- 嫌疑人定于下周出庭参加首次听证会。
- 这个复杂问题的解决方案在经验丰富的工程师看来相当简单。
</details>

### 89. appease <a id="89-appease"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *apesier* ("to pacify, calm"), from *a-* ("to") + *pa(i)s* ("peace"), from Latin *pax*, *pacis* ("peace").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *apesier*（安抚，使平静），源自 *a-*（向）+ *pa(i)s*（和平），源自拉丁语 *pax*，*pacis*（和平）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To calm or satisfy someone by giving in to their demands; to pacify or conciliate, often by making concessions.
- CN: 安抚；缓和；绥靖；满足（需求）。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Often carries a negative connotation — implies yielding to unreasonable demands or avoiding conflict at a moral cost.
- Famous historical usage: "appeasement" policy toward Nazi Germany in the 1930s (Chamberlain's appeasement). — 著名历史用法：20世纪30年代对纳粹德国的"appeasement"（绥靖）政策（张伯伦的绥靖政策）
- Neutral usage: "appease one's hunger/thirst/curiosity." — 中性用法："appease one's hunger/thirst/curiosity"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- appease sb (especially critics / protesters / the public)
- appease one's hunger / thirst / curiosity
- policy of appeasement
- appease the gods
- attempt / try to appease
- appease critics
- appease anger
- difficult to appease
- appease one's conscience


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 安抚某人（尤指批评者/抗议者/公众）
- 满足某人的饥饿/口渴/好奇心
- 绥靖政策
- 取悦神灵
- 试图安抚
- 安抚批评者
- 平息愤怒
- 难以安抚
- 安抚某人的良心
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- appease sb with sth
- appease sb by doing sth
- appease through sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 用某物安抚某人
- 通过做某事安抚某人
- 通过……安抚
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The government tried to appease the protesters by agreeing to some of their demands. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: Historians often debate whether the policy of appeasement in the 1930s was a mistake. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: To appease local communities, the mining company promised to invest in infrastructure projects. (TOEFL TPO 52)
- Academic: The new CEO attempted to appease shareholders by announcing a share buyback program. (Harvard Business Review 2020)
- Common: A glass of water appeased his thirst after the long run.
- 介词搭配: The school attempted to appease angry parents with a promise to improve cafeteria food.
- 介词搭配: The manager tried to appease the dissatisfied customer by offering a full refund and a sincere apology.
- 介词搭配: The government tried to appease public discontent through a series of social welfare reforms.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 政府试图通过同意抗议者的一些要求来安抚他们。
- 历史学家经常讨论20世纪30年代的绥靖政策是否是一个错误。
- 为了安抚当地社区，矿业公司承诺投资基础设施项目。
- 新任CEO试图通过宣布股票回购计划来安抚股东。
- 长跑后，一杯水解了他的渴。
- 学校试图通过承诺改善食堂伙食来安抚愤怒的家长。
- 经理试图通过提供全额退款和真诚道歉来安抚不满的顾客。
- 政府试图通过一系列社会福利改革来平息公众的不满。
</details>

### 90. applaud <a id="90-applaud"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *applaudere* ("to strike upon, clap"), from *ad-* ("to") + *plaudere* ("to clap, strike"). Related to "plausible," "applause," "explode" (originally "to drive off the stage by clapping").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *applaudere*（拍打，鼓掌），源自 *ad-*（向）+ *plaudere*（鼓掌，拍打）。与“plausible”、“applause”、“explode”（原义为“用掌声赶下舞台”）相关。

**Definitions:**

- EN: (vt./vi.) To show approval by clapping hands; to express strong approval or praise for something.
- CN: (vt./vi.) 鼓掌；赞赏；称赞。

**Part of Speech:** vt. & vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Literal meaning: clap hands — "applaud a performance."
- Figurative meaning: express strong approval — "applaud an effort/decision."
- The noun form is "applause" (uncountable). — 名词形式是"applause"（不可数）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- applaud a performance / effort / decision
- applaud loudly / enthusiastically / warmly
- applaud the initiative / move
- universally applauded
- applaud the courage
- applaud wildly
- widely applauded
- applaud the success


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 为表演/努力/决定鼓掌
- 大声/热情/热烈鼓掌
- 称赞倡议/举措
- 受到普遍赞扬
- 赞扬勇气
- 疯狂鼓掌
- 广受赞扬
- 称赞成功
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- applaud sb for sth
- should be applauded for
- applaud sb for doing sth
- applaud with enthusiasm


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 因某事赞扬某人
- 应该因……受到赞扬
- 赞扬某人做某事
- 热情鼓掌
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The audience applauded the speaker warmly at the end of the lecture. (CET-4 2017-06)
- 考研: The decision to reduce plastic packaging was applauded by environmental groups. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The pianist's masterful performance was applauded by a standing ovation. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The scientific community applauded the researchers for publishing their data openly. (Nature 2021)
- Common: I applaud your commitment to completing this difficult project.
- 介词搭配: The audience applauded the performers for their outstanding musical talent and stage presence.
- 介词搭配: The local government should be applauded for its efforts to preserve historical architecture.
- 介词搭配: The scientific community applauded the researchers for sharing their data openly with the public.
- 介词搭配: The entire audience applauded with genuine enthusiasm at the end of the moving performance.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 减少塑料包装的决定受到了环保组织的赞扬。
- 钢琴家的精湛表演获得了全场起立鼓掌。
- 科学界赞扬研究人员公开分享数据。
- 我赞赏你完成这个困难项目的决心。
- 观众因表演者杰出的音乐才华和舞台表现力而鼓掌称赞。
- 当地政府因保护历史建筑的努力而应受到赞扬。
- 科学界赞扬研究人员向公众公开分享数据。

</details>

### 91. apply <a id="91-apply"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *applicare* ("to attach, connect, apply"), from *ad-* ("to") + *plicare* ("to fold, lay").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *applicare*（附着，连接，应用），源自 *ad-*（向）+ *plicare*（折叠，放置）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To make a formal request; 2) To put something into operation or practice; 3) To be relevant or appropriate.
- CN: 1) 申请；2) 应用，运用；3) 适用。

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- When meaning "to make a request," use *apply to* (organization) + *for* (position/thing): "apply to the university for admission." — 当表示"申请"时，使用 *apply to*（机构）+ *for*（职位/事物）："apply to the university for admission"
- When meaning "to put into practice," use *apply something to something*: "apply the theory to the experiment." — 当表示"应用"时，使用 *apply something to something*："apply the theory to the experiment"
- When meaning "to be relevant," use *apply to*: "The rules apply to everyone." — 当表示"适用"时，使用 *apply to*："The rules apply to everyone."

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- apply theory/pressure/knowledge
- apply oneself to sth
- apply the law
- apply in practice
- apply equally


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 应用理论/施加压力/运用知识
- 致力于某事
- 适用法律
- 在实践中应用
- 同样适用
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- apply for a job/visa/loan
- apply to a university/company
- apply for permission
- apply for membership
- apply to sb/sth
- apply sth to sth
- apply to do sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 申请工作/签证/贷款
- 向大学/公司申请
- 申请许可
- 申请会员资格
- 适用于某人/某物
- 将某物应用于某物
- 申请做某事
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: She decided to apply for the scholarship after learning about the requirements. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: The new technology can be applied to various industrial fields to improve efficiency. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Students must apply to the graduate program before the December deadline. (TOEFL TPO 31)
- Academic: These findings apply broadly across different demographic groups. (Nature 2019)
- Common: He applied himself to his studies and achieved excellent results.
- 介词搭配: She decided to apply for the marketing manager position at a multinational corporation.
- 介词搭配: Over ten thousand students applied to Harvard University this year from around the world.
- 介词搭配: The research team applied for permission to conduct clinical trials on the new drug.
- 介词搭配: The renowned scholar was invited to apply for membership in the prestigious national academy.
- 介词搭配: These strict safety regulations apply to all employees working in the manufacturing plant.
- 介词搭配: The new machine learning technique can be applied to a wide range of industrial problems.
- 介词搭配: He applied to study environmental engineering at the local university in 2023.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 在了解要求后，她决定申请奖学金。
- 这项新技术可应用于各个工业领域以提高效率。
- 学生必须在12月截止日期前申请研究生项目。
- 这些发现广泛适用于不同的人口群体。
- 他专心学习并取得了优异的成绩。
- 她决定申请一家跨国公司的市场营销经理职位。
- 今年有超过一万名学生从世界各地申请了哈佛大学。
- 研究团队申请许可进行新药的临床试验。
- 这位著名学者受邀申请加入享有盛誉的国家科学院。
- 这些严格的安全规定适用于所有在制造厂工作的员工。
- 这种新的机器学习技术可以应用于广泛的工业问题。
- 他于2023年申请在当地大学学习环境工程。
</details>
