### 92. appoint <a id="92-appoint"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *apointier* ("to arrange, settle"), from Latin *ad-* + *punctum* ("point"), literally "to bring to a point."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *apointier*（安排，解决），源自拉丁语 *ad-* + *punctum*（点），字面意思为“使达到一点”。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To assign a job or position to someone; 2) To set or decide upon a time, date, or place formally.
- CN: 1) 任命，委派；2) 指定，约定（时间、地点等）。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- For positions, use *appoint someone (as/to be) something*: "They appointed him (as) director." — 表示职位时，使用 *appoint someone (as/to be) something*："They appointed him (as) director."
- For time/place, use *appoint a time/date for something*: "We appointed a date for the meeting." — 表示时间/地点时，使用 *appoint a time/date for something*："We appointed a date for the meeting."
- More formal than *choose* or *name*; typical of official/professional contexts. — 比 *choose* 或 *name* 更正式；典型用于官方/专业语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- appoint a committee/board
- appoint a time/place
- appoint a successor
- appoint an agent
- officially appoint


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 任命委员会/董事会
- 约定时间/地点
- 任命继任者
- 指定代理人
- 正式任命
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- appoint sb as director/manager
- appoint sb to a post/position
- be appointed by the president/board
- appoint a date for sth
- appoint sb to do sth
- appoint sb as sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 任命某人为董事/经理
- 任命某人担任职务
- 由总统/董事会任命
- 确定……的日期
- 指派某人做某事
- 任命某人为……
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: A new principal was appointed to lead the school from next semester. (CET-4 2020-09)
- 考研: The CEO appointed a task force to investigate the decline in sales. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The committee was appointed to review the university's admission policies. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: Participants were randomly appointed to either the control or experimental group. (Science 2022)
- Common: We appointed a meeting for next Tuesday at 10 a.m.
- 介词搭配: The board appointed her as the new chief financial officer of the corporation.
- 介词搭配: She was appointed to the position of senior research fellow at the university's medical school.
- 介词搭配: The committee member was appointed by the president to serve a five-year term.
- 介词搭配: The committee has appointed a date for the annual general meeting in early June.
- 介词搭配: The CEO appointed a special task force to investigate the recent data security breach.
- 介词搭配: The board of directors decided to appoint her as the chief financial officer of the corporation.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 任命了一位新校长从下学期开始领导学校。
- CEO任命了一个工作组调查销售下滑的原因。
- 该委员会被任命审查大学的招生政策。
- 参与者被随机分配到对照组或实验组。
- 我们约定了下周二上午10点开会。
- 董事会任命她为公司的首席财务官。
- 她被任命为大学医学院的高级研究员职位。
- 该委员会成员由总统任命，任期五年。
- 委员会已经确定了六月初召开年度股东大会的日期。
- CEO指派了一个特别工作组调查最近的数据安全漏洞。
- 董事会决定任命她为公司的首席财务官。
</details>

### 93. appraise <a id="93-appraise"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *apreisier* ("to set a price on"), from Latin *ad-* + *pretium* ("price"). Related to *praise* and *price*.

**词源：** 源自古法语 *apreisier*（定价），源自拉丁语 *ad-* + *pretium*（价格）。与 *praise* 和 *price* 相关。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To assess the value or quality of something; 2) To evaluate performance or worth formally.
- CN: 1) 估价，鉴定；2) 评估，评价。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Used in both financial contexts (property/jewelry valuation) and performance contexts (employee appraisal). — 用于财务语境（财产/珠宝估价）和绩效语境（员工评估）
- More formal than *evaluate* or *assess*. — 比 *evaluate* 或 *assess* 更正式
- Do not confuse with *apprise* (to inform); these are commonly mixed up. — 不要与 *apprise*（通知）混淆；这两者常被混淆

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- appraise the value/worth of sth
- appraise a property/asset
- appraise an employee's performance
- get sth appraised
- critically appraise
- appraise the situation
- appraise the damage
- professionally appraised


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 评估某物的价值
- 评估财产/资产
- 评估员工的表现
- 请人评估某物
- 批判性评估
- 评估形势
- 评估损失
- 经专业评估
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- appraise sth at sth
- appraise for sth
- be appraised by sb
- appraise sth as sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 评估某物价值为……
- 为……而评估
- 由某人评估
- 将某物评估为……
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The antique vase was appraised at over ten thousand yuan. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: Managers should regularly appraise their team members' contributions. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Before purchasing the house, we had it professionally appraised. (TOEFL TPO 54)
- Academic: The study appraises the effectiveness of three different treatment methods. (The Lancet 2021)
- Common: It is difficult to appraise the true cost of the damage.
- 介词搭配: The estate was appraised for inheritance tax purposes at approximately five million dollars.
- 介词搭配: The antique vase was appraised by a certified expert from the renowned auction house.
- 介词搭配: The literary critic appraised her latest novel as a masterpiece of contemporary fiction.
- 介词搭配: The professional appraiser appraised the vintage sports car at over two hundred thousand dollars.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这个古董花瓶估价超过一万元。
- 经理应定期评估团队成员的贡献。
- 在购买房子之前，我们请专业人士对其进行了评估。
- 该研究评估了三种不同治疗方法的有效性。
- 很难评估损失的真实成本。
- 出于遗产税目的，该房产被估价约五百万美元。
- 这个古董花瓶由著名拍卖行的认证专家进行了鉴定。
- 文学评论家将她的最新小说评价为当代小说的杰作。
- 专业鉴定师将这辆 vintage 跑车估价超过二十万美元。
</details>

### 94. appreciate <a id="94-appreciate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *appretiare* ("to set a price to"), from *ad-* + *pretium* ("price"). Developed meanings of "value, esteem" and later "increase in value."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *appretiare*（定价），源自 *ad-* + *pretium*（价格）。后来发展出“重视，尊重”以及“增值”的含义。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To recognize the value or significance of something; 2) To be grateful for something; 3) To increase in value.
- CN: 1) 欣赏，赏识；2) 感激；3) 增值。

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- As a transitive verb meaning "to be grateful," the pattern is *appreciate + noun/gerund*: "I appreciate your help." — 作及物动词表示"感激"时，句型为 *appreciate + 名词/动名词*："I appreciate your help."
- As an intransitive verb meaning "to increase in value": "The property has appreciated significantly." — 作不及物动词表示"增值"时："The property has appreciated significantly."
- Avoid: "I very appreciate" ✗ → "I really/deeply appreciate" ✓ — 避免：I very appreciate ✗ → I really/deeply appreciate ✓

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- appreciate the importance/significance/value
- appreciate sb's help/support/efforts
- deeply / highly appreciate
- I would appreciate it if...
- sincerely appreciate
- fully appreciate
- appreciate art/music
- appreciate the difference


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 认识到重要性/意义/价值
- 感激某人的帮助/支持/努力
- 深表感激/高度赞赏
- 如果……我将不胜感激
- 衷心感谢
- 充分理解
- 欣赏艺术/音乐
- 认识到差异
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- appreciate in value
- appreciate sb for sth
- appreciate sth as sth
- appreciate sth for sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 升值
- 因某事感谢/赏识某人
- 将某物视为……来欣赏
- 因某事而欣赏某物
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: I really appreciate your taking the time to explain this to me. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: It is important to appreciate the cultural differences when doing business abroad. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Art historians appreciate this painting for its innovative use of color. (TOEFL TPO 28)
- Academic: We appreciate the complexity of these ecological interactions. (Nature Ecology & Evolution 2020)
- Common: The value of this investment has appreciated by 20% over five years.
- 介词搭配: Real estate in this area has appreciated significantly in value over the past five years.
- 介词搭配: We sincerely appreciate our volunteers for their dedication and hard work.
- 介词搭配: Art critics appreciate this sculpture as a remarkable fusion of classical and modern styles.
- 介词搭配: She appreciates classical music for its emotional depth and structural complexity.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 我非常感谢你花时间向我解释这个。
- 在国外做生意时，理解文化差异很重要。
- 艺术史学家欣赏这幅画对色彩的创新运用。
- 我们认识到这些生态相互作用的复杂性。
- 这项投资的价值在五年内增长了20%。
- 该地区的房地产在过去五年中大幅升值。
- 我们由衷感谢志愿者的奉献和辛勤工作。
- 艺术评论家将这座雕塑视为古典与现代风格的非凡融合来欣赏。
- 她欣赏古典音乐是因为其情感深度和结构复杂性。
</details>

### 95. approach <a id="95-approach"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *aprochier* ("to come near"), from Late Latin *appropriare*, from Latin *ad-* + *prope* ("near").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *aprochier*（靠近），源自晚期拉丁语 *appropriare*，源自拉丁语 *ad-* + *prope*（近的）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To come near or nearer to someone or something; 2) To speak to someone about something; 3) To begin dealing with a problem or task.
- CN: 1) 接近，靠近；2) 接洽，找……商量；3) 处理，对付。

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- When meaning "to deal with," use *approach + problem/task/issue*. — 当表示"处理"时，使用 *approach + problem/task/issue*
- When meaning "to speak to," use *approach someone about something*. — 当表示"接洽"时，使用 *approach someone about something*
- *Approach* as a verb does NOT take a preposition before the object: "approach the city" ✓, "approach to the city" ✗. — *Approach* 作动词时宾语前不加介词："approach the city" ✓，"approach to the city" ✗

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- approach a problem/task/issue
- approach a deadline/target
- approach cautiously / directly
- approach the speed of
- approach perfection
- approach the subject
- approach systematically


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 处理问题/任务/议题
- 接近截止日期/目标
- 谨慎地接近/直接接触
- 接近……的速度
- 接近完美
- 探讨话题
- 系统地处理
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- approach sb about sth
- approach with caution/care
- approach sb with a question/offer
- approach to sb/about sth
- approach from a perspective


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 就某事与某人接洽
- 谨慎地处理
- 向某人提出问题/提议
- 与某人接洽/关于某事
- 从某个角度处理
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: As we approach the final exam, students are becoming more focused. (CET-4 2022-06)
- 考研: We need to approach this environmental problem from a new perspective. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The researcher approached the data analysis using statistical modeling. (IELTS Cambridge 16)
- Academic: As temperatures approach critical levels, the ecosystem faces collapse. (Science 2021)
- Common: She approached the stranger to ask for directions.
- 介词搭配: A journalist approached the senator about the recent corruption allegations.
- 介词搭配: The delicate situation must be approached with great caution to avoid any misunderstandings.
- 介词搭配: A stranger approached the collector with an offer to buy his vintage sports car.
- 介词搭配: The lawyer made a formal approach to the opposing party about a possible settlement.
- 介词搭配: The environmental problem should be approached from a scientific perspective rather than an emotional one.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 随着期末考试的临近，学生们变得更加专注。
- 我们需要从新的角度处理这个环境问题。
- 研究人员使用统计建模来进行数据分析。
- 随着温度接近临界水平，生态系统面临崩溃。
- 她走近陌生人问路。
- 一名记者就最近的腐败指控与参议员进行了接洽。
- 必须以极大的谨慎来处理这一微妙的局面，以避免任何误解。
- 一个陌生人向收藏家提出要买他的复古跑车。
- 律师就可能的和解方案与对方进行了正式接洽。
- 应该从科学的角度而不是情感的角度来处理这个环境问题。
</details>

### 96. appropriate <a id="96-appropriate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Late Latin *appropriatus* ("made one's own"), from Latin *ad-* + *proprius* ("one's own").

**词源：** 源自晚期拉丁语 *appropriatus*（使成为自己的），源自拉丁语 *ad-* + *proprius*（自己的）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To take something for one's own use, often without permission; 2) To set aside funds for a specific purpose.
- CN: 1) 挪用，盗用；2) 拨出（款项）。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Do not confuse the verb (pronounced /əˈproʊpriˌeɪt/) with the adjective (pronounced /əˈproʊpriət/). — 不要将动词（/əˈproʊpriˌeɪt/）与形容词（/əˈproʊpriət/）混淆
- When meaning "to take without permission," it implies a degree of illegitimacy or theft. — 当表示"未经许可拿用"时，暗示一定程度的不合法或盗窃
- When meaning "to allocate funds," it is formal and used in government/business contexts. — 当表示"拨款"时，用于正式场合和政府/商业语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- appropriate funds/money/resources
- appropriate sb's ideas/work
- appropriate property/land
- culturally appropriate
- appropriate illegally
- appropriate public funds
- government-appropriated


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 拨出资金/金钱/资源
- 盗用某人的想法/作品
- 侵占财产/土地
- 文化挪用
- 非法挪用
- 挪用公款
- 政府拨款的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be appropriated for a purpose
- appropriate for personal use
- appropriate for sth
- appropriate from sth
- appropriate sth for oneself


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 被拨作特定用途
- 据为己用
- 为……而拨款
- 从……挪用
- 将某物据为己有
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The company was accused of appropriating customer data without consent. (CET-6 2020-09)
- 考研: The government appropriated a billion yuan for disaster relief efforts. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The artist was criticized for appropriating traditional cultural symbols. (TOEFL TPO 40)
- Academic: No funds were appropriated for this research in the current budget. (Nature 2019)
- Common: He appropriated his colleague's idea and presented it as his own.
- 介词搭配: A significant portion of the national budget was appropriated for education and healthcare infrastructure.
- 介词搭配: The accountant was found guilty of appropriating company funds for personal use.
- 介词搭配: The government appropriated an additional amount for disaster relief in the affected regions.
- 介词搭配: The colonial powers appropriated vast natural resources from the lands they conquered.
- 介词搭配: A large portion of the national budget was appropriated for education and healthcare purposes.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该公司被指控未经同意盗用客户数据。
- 政府拨出十亿元人民币用于救灾工作。
- 这位艺术家因挪用传统文化符号而受到批评。
- 当前预算中未拨出任何资金用于这项研究。
- 他盗用了同事的想法并当作自己的提出。
- 国家预算的很大一部分被拨用于教育和医疗基础设施建设。
- 该会计被判犯有将公司资金挪作个人使用的罪行。
- 政府额外拨出资金用于受灾地区的救灾工作。
- 殖民国家从其征服的土地上掠夺了大量的自然资源。
- 国家预算的很大一部分被拨用于教育和医疗目的。
</details>

### 97. approve <a id="97-approve"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *aprover* ("to approve"), from Latin *approbare* ("to assent to, regard as good"), from *ad-* + *probare* ("to test, prove").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *aprover*（批准），源自拉丁语 *approbare*（同意，认为好），源自 *ad-* + *probare*（测试，证明）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To officially agree to or accept something as satisfactory; 2) To have a favorable opinion.
- CN: 1) 批准，通过；2) 赞成，认可。

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- As a transitive verb (formal/official approval): "The committee approved the plan." — 作及物动词（正式/官方批准）："The committee approved the plan."
- As an intransitive verb (personal opinion): *approve of*: "I don't approve of smoking." — 作不及物动词（个人意见）：*approve of*："I don't approve of smoking."
- In business/government, *approve* without a preposition is standard: "approve the budget." — 在商业/政府语境中，*approve* 不带介词是标准用法："approve the budget."

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- approve a plan/proposal/budget
- approve a request/application
- approve a measure
- approve a bill
- approve unanimously
- formally approve


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 批准计划/提案/预算
- 批准请求/申请
- 批准措施
- 批准法案
- 一致批准
- 正式批准
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- approve of sb's behavior/decision
- be approved by the board/committee/government
- approve in principle
- approve of sb
- approve sth for sb


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 赞成某人的行为/决定
- 由董事会/委员会/政府批准
- 原则上批准
- 赞成/认可某人
- 为某人批准某事
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The school board approved the new curriculum for the coming academic year. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The drug has been approved by the FDA for clinical trials. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The university approved his application for a research grant. (TOEFL TPO 34)
- Academic: The ethics committee approved the experimental protocol before data collection began. (The New England Journal of Medicine 2020)
- Common: Her parents don't approve of her choice of career.
- 介词搭配: Her parents do not approve of her decision to drop out of college to start a business.
- 介词搭配: The new cancer drug was approved by the FDA after rigorous clinical trials.
- 介词搭配: The proposal was approved in principle, though the final budget still needs to be negotiated.
- 介词搭配: The board of directors thoroughly approves of the new CEO and his innovative management style.
- 介词搭配: The committee approved a special grant for the research project on renewable energy.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 学校董事会批准了新学年的课程。
- 该药物已获FDA批准进行临床试验。
- 大学批准了他的研究资助申请。
- 伦理委员会在数据收集开始前批准了实验方案。
- 她的父母不赞成她的职业选择。
- 她的父母不赞成她退学创业的决定。
- 这种新型抗癌药经过严格的临床试验后获得了FDA的批准。
- 该提案原则上已获批准，但最终预算仍需协商。
- 董事会非常认可新任CEO及其创新管理风格。
- 委员会为可再生能源研究项目批准了一项特别拨款。
</details>

### 98. approximate <a id="98-approximate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Late Latin *approximatus* ("drawn near to"), from Latin *ad-* + *proximus* ("nearest"), superlative of *prope* ("near").

**词源：** 源自晚期拉丁语 *approximatus*（接近），源自拉丁语 *ad-* + *proximus*（最近的），*prope*（近的）的最高级。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To estimate or calculate something roughly; 2) To come close to being something.
- CN: 1) 近似，粗略估计；2) 接近，近似于。

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- The verb is pronounced /əˈprɑksɪˌmeɪt/, while the adjective is /əˈprɑksɪmət/. — 动词发音为 /əˈprɑksɪˌmeɪt/，而形容词发音为 /əˈprɑksɪmət/
- In mathematical/scientific contexts, *approximate* is very common for estimation. — 在数学/科学语境中，*approximate* 非常常用于估算
- Use *approximate to* or *approximate something*: "The cost approximates $5,000." — 使用 *approximate to* 或 *approximate something*："The cost approximates $5,000."

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- approximate the cost/value/size
- approximate the number
- approximate the age
- roughly approximate
- closely approximate
- a close approximation of


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 估算成本/价值/大小
- 估算数量
- 估算年龄
- 大致接近
- 非常接近
- ……的近似值
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- approximate to the truth/reality
- be approximated by
- approximate to sth
- approximate at sth
- approximate by doing sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 接近真相/现实
- 可由……近似
- 接近于……
- 估计为……
- 通过做某事来近似
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: We can approximate the total cost based on last year's data. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: The population of the city is approximated at around three million. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Scientists approximate the age of the fossil at 50,000 years. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The observed values closely approximate those predicted by the model. (Physical Review Letters 2021)
- Common: The design approximates the original but lacks some details.
- 介词搭配: The estimated cost of the project approximates to the actual expenses within a 5% margin.
- 介词搭配: The complex mathematical function can be approximated by a simpler linear regression model.
- 介词搭配: The total economic damage from the natural disaster is approximated at over one billion dollars.
- 介词搭配: Scientists approximate the age of ancient artifacts by analyzing carbon isotope ratios in the samples.
- 介词搭配: The witness's account of the incident only approximates to the truth but omits several crucial details.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 我们可以根据去年的数据估算总成本。
- 该城市的人口估计约为三百万。
- 科学家估计这块化石的年龄为5万年。
- 观测值与模型预测值非常接近。
- 这个设计接近原件，但缺少一些细节。
- 该项目的估算成本与实际支出的误差在5%以内。
- 这个复杂的数学函数可以用一个更简单的线性回归模型来近似。
- 自然灾害造成的经济损失总额估计超过十亿美元。
- 科学家通过分析样本中的碳同位素比例来估算古代器物的年代。
- 证人对事件的描述只是接近真相，但遗漏了几个关键细节。
</details>

### 99. arbitrate <a id="99-arbitrate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *arbitrari* ("to be a witness, give judgment"), from *arbiter* ("witness, judge").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *arbitrari*（作证人，作出裁决），源自 *arbiter*（证人，法官）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To settle a dispute between two parties through an impartial third party; to act as an arbitrator.
- CN: 仲裁，公断，调解。

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Use *arbitrate between/among parties*: "A neutral judge arbitrated between the two sides." — 使用 *arbitrate between/among parties*："A neutral judge arbitrated between the two sides."
- Use *arbitrate a dispute/conflict*: "The UN arbitrated the border dispute." — 使用 *arbitrate a dispute/conflict*："The UN arbitrated the border dispute."
- More formal than *mediate*; implies a binding decision rather than mere facilitation. — 比 *mediate* 更正式；暗示具有约束力的决定而非仅仅是调解

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- arbitrate a dispute/conflict
- submit to arbitration
- binding arbitration
- arbitration clause
- arbitrate fairly/impartially
- agree to arbitrate
- international arbitration


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 仲裁争端/冲突
- 提交仲裁
- 有约束力的仲裁
- 仲裁条款
- 公正仲裁
- 同意仲裁
- 国际仲裁
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- arbitrate between parties/sides
- arbitrate in a case/matter
- arbitrate for sb
- arbitrate among sb
- arbitrate on sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 在各方之间进行仲裁
- 仲裁案件/事项
- 为某人进行仲裁
- 在……之间仲裁
- 就某事进行仲裁
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- 考研: The international court was asked to arbitrate the territorial conflict. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The contract states that any disagreements will be arbitrated by a third party. (IELTS Cambridge 14)
- Academic: The WTO arbitrated the trade disagreement between the two nations. (Journal of International Law 2020)
- Common: They agreed to let a professional mediator arbitrate their disagreement.
- 介词搭配: A neutral third party was invited to arbitrate between the management and the labor union.
- 介词搭配: The United Nations was asked to arbitrate in the long-standing border dispute between the two countries.
- 介词搭配: A well-known legal expert was hired to arbitrate for the workers in the complex salary dispute.
- 介词搭配: The special committee was formed to arbitrate among the various stakeholders with conflicting interests.
- 介词搭配: An independent international expert was invited to arbitrate on the complex trade disagreement.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 他们同意让一位专业调解人来仲裁他们的分歧。
- 一名中立的第三方受邀在管理层与工会之间进行仲裁。
- 联合国被请求就两国之间长期存在的边界争端进行仲裁。
- 一位知名的法律专家被雇来在复杂的薪资纠纷中为工人们进行仲裁。
- 专门委员会成立以在有利益冲突的各方利益相关者之间进行仲裁。
- 一位独立的国际专家受邀就复杂的贸易纠纷进行仲裁。
</details>

### 100. arch <a id="100-arch"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *arche*, from Latin *arcus* ("bow, arch"). The verb developed from the noun meaning "a curved structure."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *arche*，源自拉丁语 *arcus*（弓，拱）。动词由名词“拱形结构”发展而来。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To form or bend into a curved shape; 2) To provide with an arch; 3) To move in an arc.
- CN: 1) 使成弓形，拱起；2) 用拱覆盖；3) 呈弧形移动。

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Commonly used with body parts: *arch one's back* (cats, stretching, anger). — 常用于身体部位：*arch one's back*（猫弓背、伸展、生气）
- In architecture, often used in passive voice: "The ceiling is arched." — 在建筑学中，常用于被动语态："The ceiling is arched."
- In sports, *arch* describes the trajectory of a ball: "The ball arched over the goalkeeper." — 在体育中，*arch* 描述球的轨迹："The ball arched over the goalkeeper."

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- arch one's back/eyebrows
- an arched doorway/bridge/window
- highly arched
- arch gracefully
- arch the neck
- deeply arched


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 拱起背/挑起眉毛
- 拱形门口/桥/窗户
- 高拱的
- 优雅地拱起
- 拱起脖子
- 深深拱起的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- arch over sth
- arch through the air
- arch above sth
- arch from sth to sth
- arch toward sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 在……上方呈拱形
- 在空中划出弧线
- 在……上方拱起
- 从……拱到……
- 向……呈拱形延伸
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The cat arched its back when it saw the dog. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: The bridge arches gracefully over the river. (考研英语 2017)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The ceiling is arched, creating a sense of spaciousness. (TOEFL TPO 42)
- Academic: The spine naturally arches during this type of movement. (Journal of Biomechanics 2021)
- Common: She arched an eyebrow in surprise at the news.
- 介词搭配: A graceful stone bridge arches over the peaceful lake in the city's central park.
- 介词搭配: The ball arched gracefully through the air and landed perfectly in the goal.
- 介词搭配: A magnificent stone bridge arches above the rushing river below.
- 介词搭配: A brilliant rainbow arched from one end of the valley to the other after the summer storm.
- 介词搭配: The ancient Roman aqueduct arches majestically toward the city, carrying water from distant springs.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这座桥优雅地拱跨在河上。
- 天花板是拱形的，营造出一种宽敞感。
- 在这种类型的运动中，脊柱会自然拱起。
- 听到这个消息，她惊讶地挑起眉毛。
- 一座优雅的石拱桥横跨在城市中央公园平静的湖面上。
- 球在空中优美地划出一道弧线，精准地落入了球门。
- 一座宏伟的石桥拱跨在下方湍急的河流之上。
- 夏日的暴风雨后，一道绚丽的彩虹从山谷的一端拱向另一端。
- 古老的罗马高架水渠雄伟地向城市呈拱形延伸，从远处的泉眼引水而来。
</details>

### 101. archive <a id="101-archive"></a>
**Etymology:** From French *archives* (plural), from Latin *archivum*, from Greek *arkheion* ("public records, town hall"), from *arkhē* ("beginning, government").

**词源：** 源自法语 *archives*（复数），来自拉丁语 *archivum*，来自希腊语 *arkheion*（"公共记录，市政厅"），源自 *arkhē*（"开始，政府"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To store documents, records, or data in a place for long-term preservation.
- CN: 归档，存档，把……存档。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Used both in physical (documents/records) and digital contexts (files/emails/data). — 既用于实体（文件/记录）也用于数字环境（文件/电子邮件/数据）
- In computing, *archive* often implies compression for storage: "archive old files." — 在计算机领域，*archive* 通常意味着压缩存储："archive old files"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- archive documents/records/files
- archive data/information
- archive emails
- digital archive
- properly archive
- archive old files
- permanently archive


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 归档文件/记录/档案
- 存档数据/信息
- 归档电子邮件
- 数字存档
- 妥善归档
- 存档旧文件
- 永久存档
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- archive for preservation/reference
- archive sth for future use
- archive in a database
- archive in sth
- archive for sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 归档保存/备查
- 将某物归档以备将来使用
- 在数据库中存档
- 将某物存档于某处
- 为……而存档
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: All student records are archived for at least five years after graduation. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The library archives rare manuscripts in a climate-controlled room. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Historical newspapers have been digitized and archived online. (TOEFL TPO 52)
- Academic: The raw sequencing data were archived in a public database. (Nature Genetics 2021)
- Common: I archive all my old project files at the end of each semester.
- 介词搭配: The research team archived all experimental data in a secure database for long-term preservation.
- 介词搭配: It is advisable to archive important business correspondence for future use when legal issues arise.
- 介词搭配: The organization archives all historical documents for future reference by researchers.
- 介词搭配: All customer records were archived in a secure encrypted database to protect sensitive information.
- 介词搭配: The national library archives all newspapers for preservation and historical reference.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">

- 图书馆将珍贵手稿存放在恒温恒湿的房间里。

- 原始测序数据已存档在公共数据库中。
- 我每学期末都会归档所有旧的项目文件。
- 研究团队将所有实验数据存档在安全的数据库中以便长期保存。
- 建议将重要的商务信函存档以备将来出现法律问题时使用。
- 该组织将所有历史文件存档以供研究人员将来查阅。
- 所有客户记录都存档在一个安全的加密数据库中，以保护敏感信息。
- 国家图书馆归档所有报纸以供保存和历史参考。

### 102. argue <a id="102-argue"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *arguere* ("to make clear, prove, accuse"), from PIE *h₂erǵ-* ("to be white, bright").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *arguere*（"使清楚，证明，控告"），来自原始印欧语 *h₂erǵ-*（"白色，明亮"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To express disagreement with someone using reasons; 2) To give reasons for or against something.
- CN: 1) 争论，争辩；2) 论证，主张。

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- *Argue with someone about/over something*: "She argued with him about politics." — *与某人争论某事*："她和他就政治问题争论了起来"- *Argue for/against something*: "He argued against the proposal." — *论证支持/反对某事*："他反对这个提议"
- *Argue that + clause*: "Scientists argue that climate change is accelerating." — *主张……*："科学家们认为气候变化正在加速"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- argue that...
- argue a case/point
- argue forcefully
- argue convincingly/persuasively
- argue vehemently/hotly


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 主张……
- 论证一个案例/观点
- 有力论证
- 有说服力地论证
- 激烈地争论
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- argue with someone about/over something
- argue for/against a position
- argue in favor of
- argue over/about sth
- argue against
- argue sb into/out of sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 与某人争论某事
- 论证支持/反对某一立场
- 为……辩护
- 就某事争论
- 反对；论证……不对
- 说服某人做/不做某事
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Some people argue that social media has a negative impact on young people. (CET-4 2022-12)
- 考研: The author argues that economic growth does not necessarily lead to happiness. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Many environmentalists argue for stricter regulations on carbon emissions. (TOEFL TPO 36)
- Academic: We argue that this mechanism plays a crucial role in cell differentiation. (Cell 2020)
- Common: There is no point arguing with him when he is so stubborn.
- 介词搭配: The editorial argued against the proposed tax reform, citing its potential negative impact on small businesses.
- 介词搭配: Her passionate speech argued the committee into reconsidering the funding allocation for the project.
- 介词搭配: The two professors argued with each other about the validity of the new economic theory.
- 介词搭配: The environmental activist argued passionately in favor of stricter pollution controls at the public hearing.
- 介词搭配: The committee members spent the entire meeting arguing over the wording of the final resolution.
- 介词搭配: The senator argued for the new education bill, emphasizing its long-term benefits for the country's economic competitiveness.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 一些人认为社交媒体对年轻人有负面影响。
- 作者论证说经济增长不一定带来幸福。
- 许多环保人士主张对碳排放实行更严格的监管。
- 我们认为这一机制在细胞分化中起着关键作用。
- 他那么固执，跟他争论没有意义。
- 这篇社论反对提议的税收改革，理由是它可能对小型企业产生负面影响。
- 她那充满激情的演讲说服了委员会重新考虑该项目的资金分配。
- 两位教授就新经济理论的有效性互相争论。
- 环保活动家在公开听证会上充满激情地为更严格的污染控制辩护。
- 委员会成员在整场会议上就最终决议的措辞争论不休。
- 这位参议员主张新教育法案，强调其对该国经济竞争力的长期益处。
</details>

### 103. arise <a id="103-arise"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old English *ārīsan* ("to arise, get up"), from Proto-Germanic *uzrīsaną*, from *uz-* ("out") + *rīsaną* ("to rise").

**词源：** 源自古英语 *ārīsan*（"起来，起床"），来自原始日耳曼语 *uzrīsaną*，源自 *uz-*（"向外"）+ *rīsaną*（"上升"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To come into existence or become apparent; 2) To get up (formal); 3) To result from something.
- CN: 1) 出现，产生；2) 起身（正式）；3) 由……引起。

**Part of Speech:** vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Intransitive verb — does NOT take a direct object. No passive voice. — 不及物动词——不直接接宾语，无被动语态- *Arise from/out of something*: "Problems arise from poor communication." — *由……引起*："问题源于沟通不畅"
- Irregular: arise → arose → arisen. — 不规则动词：arise → arose → arisen
- Do not confuse with *rise* (to go up) or *raise* (to lift; transitive). — 不要与 *rise*（上升）或 *raise*（举起；及物）混淆

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- a problem/issue/question arises
- should the need arise / if the need arises
- complications/difficulties arise
- arise unexpectedly/suddenly
- opportunity arises
- arise naturally
- conflict/dispute arises
- questions/doubts arise


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 问题/议题/疑问出现
- 如有必要
- 并发症/困难出现
- 突然出现
- 机会出现
- 自然产生
- 冲突/争议出现
- 问题/疑问出现
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- arise from/out of
- arise as a result of
- arise in sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 由……引起/产生
- 作为……的结果而产生
- 出现于某处/某领域
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Several important questions arose during the discussion. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: New challenges have arisen as a result of the rapid technological changes. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Conflicts often arise when cultural expectations differ. (TOEFL TPO 30)
- Academic: Complications arose in 15% of the patients treated with the standard protocol. (The Lancet 2022)
- Common: We will deal with any issues that arise during the project.
- 介词搭配: Many misunderstandings arise from a lack of effective cross-cultural communication in multinational corporations.
- 介词搭配: Similar technical problems are likely to arise in any large-scale infrastructure project of this complexity.
- 介词搭配: Numerous legal disputes arose as a result of the ambiguous terms in the trade agreement.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 讨论中出现了几个重要问题。
- 技术的快速变化带来了新的挑战。
- 当文化期望不同时，冲突常常出现。
- 接受标准方案治疗的患者中有15%出现了并发症。
- 我们会处理项目期间出现的任何问题。
- 许多误解源于跨国公司中缺乏有效的跨文化交流。
- 类似的技术问题可能会出现在任何这种复杂程度的大型基础设施项目中。
- 由于贸易协定中的条款含糊不清，出现了许多法律纠纷。
</details>

### 104. arm <a id="104-arm"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *armer* ("to arm"), from Latin *armare* ("to furnish with weapons"), from *arma* ("tools, weapons").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *armer*（"武装"），来自拉丁语 *armare*（"用武器装备"），源自 *arma*（"工具，武器"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To supply or equip with weapons; 2) To provide with tools or information; 3) To activate a device.
- CN: 1) 武装，装备；2) 提供（工具、信息等）；3) 准备启动。

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- *Arm someone with something*: "They armed the soldiers with new rifles." — *用某物武装某人*："他们用新步枪武装了士兵"
- The figurative use (arm with information/tools) is very common in academic/business English. — 比喻用法（用信息/工具武装）在学术/商务英语中很常见
- The past participle *armed* is frequently used as an adjective: "armed forces," "armed with data." — 过去分词 *armed* 常作形容词："武装部队"，"用数据武装"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- arm the soldiers/troops
- armed forces/police
- arm a bomb/missile/alarm system
- arm heavily/lightly
- remain armed
- armed conflict/struggle


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 武装士兵/军队
- 武装部队/警察
- 安装炸弹/导弹/警报系统
- 重/轻武装
- 保持武装
- 武装冲突/斗争
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- arm oneself with something
- be armed with knowledge/information/facts
- arm with weapons/tools
- arm oneself against
- arm against sb/sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 用某物武装自己
- 以知识/信息/事实武装
- 用武器/工具装备
- 武装起来抵御
- 武装起来抵御某人/某物
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The negotiation team was armed with all the relevant data. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: Citizens should arm themselves with knowledge to avoid online fraud. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The rebels were heavily armed with weapons from foreign sources. (TOEFL TPO 50)
- Academic: Participants were armed with wearable sensors to track physical activity. (Nature Medicine 2021)
- Common: The security guard armed the alarm system before leaving the building.
- 介词搭配: Before attending the debate, she armed herself with the latest statistical data to support her arguments.
- 介词搭配: The new cybersecurity legislation aims to arm consumers against fraudulent online business practices.
- 介词搭配: The experienced diplomat was armed with detailed knowledge of the region's history before the negotiation.
- 介词搭配: The security guards were armed with batons and pepper spray for crowd control.
- 介词搭配: Before the debate, she armed herself with the latest research data and statistical evidence.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 谈判团队掌握了所有相关数据。
- 市民应该用知识武装自己以避免网络诈骗。
- 叛军配备了来自国外的重型武器。
- 参与者佩戴了可穿戴传感器来追踪身体活动。
- 保安在离开大楼前启动了警报系统。
- 参加辩论前，她用最新的统计数据武装了自己以支持她的论点。
- 新的网络安全立法旨在保护消费者免受欺诈性在线商业行为的侵害。
- 这位经验丰富的外交官在谈判前掌握了该地区历史的详细知识。
- 保安人员配备了警棍和胡椒喷雾用于人群控制。
- 辩论前，她用最新的研究数据和统计证据武装了自己。
</details>

### 105. arouse <a id="105-arouse"></a>
**Etymology:** From *a-* (intensive prefix) + *rouse*, from Old English *hrēosan* or Old French *reuser*.

**词源：** 源自 *a-*（加强前缀）+ *rouse*，来自古英语 *hrēosan* 或古法语 *reuser*。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To awaken a feeling, emotion, or response; 2) To awaken someone from sleep.
- CN: 1) 引起，激发（情感、反应）；2) 唤醒。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- *Arouse suspicion/interest/curiosity/anger*: very common collocations. — *引起怀疑/兴趣/好奇心/愤怒*：非常常见的搭配- *Arouse someone from sleep* is formal/literary; *wake someone up* is more common. — *唤醒某人*为正式/文学用法；*wake sb up* 更常见
- Do not confuse with *rouse* (to wake actively) or *raise* (to lift). — 不要与 *rouse*（唤醒）或 *raise*（举起）混淆

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- arouse suspicion/interest/curiosity
- arouse emotions/feelings/passion
- arouse anger/resentment/opposition
- arouse public attention/concern
- arouse public/social concern
- arouse debate/controversy
- strongly/greatly arouse
- arouse awareness/consciousness


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 引起怀疑/兴趣/好奇心
- 激发情感/感情/热情
- 激起愤怒/怨恨/反对
- 引起公众关注/关切
- 引起公众/社会关注
- 引起辩论/争议
- 强烈地引起
- 唤起意识/觉悟
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- arouse someone from sleep
- arouse sb from sleep/thought
- arouse in sb


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 把某人从睡梦中唤醒
- 把某人从睡梦/思绪中唤醒
- 在某人身上激起（某种情感）
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The professor's unusual theory aroused great interest among students. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: The government's decision aroused widespread public opposition. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The documentary aroused awareness of environmental issues. (TOEFL TPO 33)
- Academic: These findings have aroused considerable debate in the scientific community. (Nature 2020)
- Common: The strange noise aroused the guards from their posts.
- 介词搭配: The loud explosion aroused the entire neighborhood from their sleep in the middle of the night.
- 介词搭配: The professor's lecture on climate change aroused in her a deep passion for environmental conservation.
- 介词搭配: The persistent ringing of the telephone finally aroused her from a deep afternoon nap.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 教授不寻常的理论引起了学生们极大的兴趣。
- 政府的决定引起了广泛的公众反对。
- 这部纪录片唤起了人们对环境问题的意识。
- 这些发现在科学界引起了相当大的争论。
- 奇怪的响声惊动了岗位上的警卫。
- 巨大的爆炸声在半夜把整个街区的人从睡梦中惊醒。
- 教授关于气候变化的讲座在她心中激起了对环境保护的深厚热情。
- 电话持续不断的铃声 finally 把她从午后的沉睡中唤醒。
</details>

### 106. arrange <a id="106-arrange"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *arengier* ("to put in order"), from *a-* + *rengier* ("to arrange, line up").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *arengier*（"整理，排列"），来自 *a-* + *rengier*（"安排，排列"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To organize or put things in a particular order; 2) To plan or make preparations.
- CN: 1) 排列，整理；2) 安排，筹备。

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- For objects: *arrange something in/by/on something*: "arrange the books in alphabetical order." — 用于物体：*arrange sth in/by/on sth*："按字母顺序排列书籍"
- For events: *arrange something* or *arrange for someone to do something*. — 用于活动：*arrange sth* 或 *arrange for sb to do sth*
- *Arrange that + clause* (formal): "We arranged that the meeting would be postponed." — *Arrange that + 从句*（正式）："我们安排了会议延期"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- arrange a meeting/appointment/event
- arrange flowers/furniture/books
- arrange transportation/accommodation
- arrange neatly/beautifully
- arrange in advance
- arrange carefully/properly


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 安排会议/约会/活动
- 摆放鲜花/家具/书籍
- 安排交通/住宿
- 整齐/漂亮地排列
- 提前安排
- 仔细/恰当地安排
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- arrange for someone to do something
- arrange in order/alphabetically/by category
- arrange sth for sb
- arrange by date/size/category
- arrange with sb
- arrange sth in/into sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 安排某人做某事
- 按顺序/字母顺序/类别排列
- 为某人安排某事
- 按日期/尺寸/类别排列
- 与某人商定安排
- 将某物排列成……
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The company arranged a training session for all new employees. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: It is the secretary's job to arrange the manager's schedule. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Can you arrange for a shuttle bus to pick us up from the airport? (IELTS Cambridge 15)
- Academic: The data were arranged in ascending order for analysis. (Nature Communications 2021)
- Common: She arranged the flowers beautifully in a vase on the table.
- 介词搭配: The coordinator arranged with the local authorities to secure the necessary permits for the outdoor event.
- 介词搭配: The librarian arranged the newly acquired reference books by category for easier navigation.
- 介词搭配: The conference organizer arranged for a professional sign language interpreter to assist the participants.
- 介词搭配: The travel agency arranged a customized sightseeing itinerary for the group of international tourists.
- 介词搭配: The event planner arranged the chairs into a semicircle to encourage group discussion.
- 介词搭配: The hotel arranged for a taxi to pick up the guests from the airport early in the morning.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 公司为所有新员工安排了培训。
- 安排经理的日程是秘书的工作。
- 你能安排一辆班车到机场接我们吗？
- 数据按升序排列以便分析。
- 她将鲜花漂亮地插在桌上的花瓶里。

- 图书管理员将新购的参考书按类别排列以便查找。
- 会议组织者安排了一名专业手语翻译来协助参与者。
- 旅行社为那组国际游客安排了一次定制观光行程。
- 活动策划者将椅子排成半圆形以鼓励小组讨论。
- 酒店安排了一辆出租车清晨到机场接客人。
</details>

### 107. array <a id="107-array"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *areier* ("to arrange, array"), from Vulgar Latin *arredare*, from Germanic *raidō* ("order, row").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *areier*（"排列，部署"），来自通俗拉丁语 *arredare*，源自日耳曼语 *raidō*（"顺序，行"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To arrange or display a group of things in a particular order; 2) To dress someone in fine clothing (literary).
- CN: 1) 排列，配置，部署；2) 盛装打扮（文学用语）。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used in passive or as past participle: "The goods were arrayed on the shelves." — 常用于被动语态或过去分词："货物排列在架子上"
- In computing, *array* is a noun for a data structure. — 在计算机领域，*array* 是表示数据结构的名词
- The noun form is far more common: "an array of options/products/tools." — 名词形式更常用："一系列选择/产品/工具"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- an array of (noun usage; common)
- array troops/forces
- array data/elements
- beautifully/impressively arrayed
- carefully arrayed
- array forces/resources


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 一系列（名词用法；常见）
- 部署军队/部队
- 排列数据/元素
- 漂亮/令人印象深刻地排列
- 精心排列
- 部署兵力/资源
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be arrayed against
- arrayed in/with
- array in rows/columns
- array on shelves/display
- array in sth
- array against sb/sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 列阵对抗
- 穿着/装饰着
- 排列成行/列
- 陈列在架子上/展示
- 以……排列
- 列阵对抗某人/某物
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: A wide array of products was on display at the exhibition. (CET-4 2022-06)
- 考研: Various statistical tools were arrayed for the researcher to choose from. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The museum arrayed the artifacts chronologically. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The solar panels were arrayed across the entire roof surface. (Nature Energy 2021)
- Common: The guests were arrayed in their finest evening gowns and suits.
- 介词搭配: The general arrayed his forces against the approaching enemy troops under the cover of darkness.
- 介词搭配: The solar panels were arrayed in neat rows across the vast desert landscape to maximize sunlight absorption.
- 介词搭配: The bride was arrayed in a stunning white silk gown and a sparkling diamond tiara for her wedding.
- 介词搭配: The rare ancient artifacts were arrayed on display in the museum's newly renovated exhibition hall.
- 介词搭配: The guests were arrayed in their finest evening attire for the awards ceremony.
- 介词搭配: The rebel forces were arrayed against the government army along the border of the disputed region.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 展会上展出了各种各样的产品。
- 各种统计工具排列整齐供研究人员选择。
- 博物馆按时间顺序陈列了文物。

- 将军在夜色掩护下部署他的部队以抵御逼近的敌军。

- 罕见的古代文物陈列在博物馆新装修的展厅中。
- 宾客们身着最精美的晚装参加颁奖典礼。
- 叛军沿争议地区边界列阵对抗政府军。

### 108. arrest <a id="108-arrest"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *arester* ("to stop, stay"), from Vulgar Latin *arrestare*, from Latin *ad-* + *restare* ("to stop, remain").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *arester*（"停止，停留"），来自通俗拉丁语 *arrestare*，源自拉丁语 *ad-* + *restare*（"停止，停留"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To seize someone by legal authority; 2) To stop or check a process; 3) To attract attention.
- CN: 1) 逮捕，拘捕；2) 阻止，抑制；3) 吸引（注意力）。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Legal meaning: "arrest someone for something": "He was arrested for theft." — 法律含义："因某事逮捕某人"："他因盗窃被捕"
- Medical meaning: "arrest the spread of disease" / "cardiac arrest" (noun). — 医学含义："阻止疾病传播"/"心脏骤停"（名词）
- Figurative: "arrest someone's attention/notice/gaze." — 比喻用法："吸引某人的注意/目光"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- arrest the development/progress/spread
- arrest one's attention/notice
- be under arrest
- arrest warrant
- arrest immediately/quickly
- arrest the decline/rise
- place/put under arrest
- wrongfully/falsely arrest


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 阻止发展/进展/蔓延
- 吸引某人的注意
- 被逮捕
- 逮捕令
- 立即/迅速逮捕
- 阻止下降/上升
- 逮捕
- 错误/冤枉逮捕
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- arrest someone for a crime
- arrest on suspicion/charges
- arrest sb on charges of


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 因犯罪逮捕某人
- 因嫌疑/指控逮捕
- 以……罪名逮捕某人
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The police arrested the suspect after a brief chase. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: Early treatment can arrest the progression of the disease. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The exhibition arrested the attention of every visitor. (TOEFL TPO 38)
- Academic: The drug was shown to arrest tumor growth in animal models. (Nature Oncology 2022)
- Common: The beautiful sunset arrested our attention as we walked along the beach.
- 介词搭配: The businessman was arrested on charges of tax evasion and money laundering totaling over $10 million.
- 介词搭配: Several protesters were arrested for violating the public assembly law during the demonstration.
- 介词搭配: The police arrested the suspect for armed robbery after gathering sufficient evidence.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 警方在短暂追捕后逮捕了嫌疑人。
- 早期治疗可以阻止疾病的进展。
- 展览吸引了每一位参观者的注意。
- 该药物在动物模型中显示出抑制肿瘤生长的作用。
- 我们沿着海滩散步时，美丽的日落吸引了我们的注意。
- 这名商人因涉嫌逃税和洗钱而被捕，涉案金额超过1000万美元。
- 几名抗议者在示威期间因违反公共集会法而被捕。
- 警方在收集到足够证据后以武装抢劫罪逮捕了嫌疑人。

### 109. arrive <a id="109-arrive"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *ariver* ("to come to shore"), from Vulgar Latin *arripare* ("to come to shore"), from Latin *ad-* + *ripa* ("shore").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *ariver*（"靠岸"），来自通俗拉丁语 *arripare*（"靠岸"），源自拉丁语 *ad-* + *ripa*（"岸"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To reach a destination; 2) To happen or come; 3) To reach an agreement or decision.
- CN: 1) 到达，抵达；2) 来临，发生；3) 达成（协议、决定等）。

**Part of Speech:** vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Intransitive verb — cannot take a direct object. "I arrived the station" ✗ → "I arrived AT the station" ✓. — 不及物动词——不能直接接宾语。"I arrived the station" ✗ → "I arrived AT the station" ✓
- Use *arrive at* for smaller places and *arrive in* for larger areas. — 小地方用 *arrive at*，大地方用 *arrive in*
- *Arrive at a decision/conclusion/agreement*: fixed phrases. — *达成决定/结论/协议*：固定短语

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- arrive home/here/there
- arrive on time / early / late
- arrive safely / safely arrived
- arrive early/late/punctually
- arrive safely/soundly
- arrive unannounced/unexpectedly
- newly arrived


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 到家/到这里/到那里
- 准时/早到/晚到
- 安全到达
- 早到/晚到/准时到达
- 安全到达
- 不请自来/突然到达
- 新到的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- arrive at/in a place
- arrive at a decision/conclusion/compromise
- arrive by plane/train/car
- arrive from sth
- arrive on sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 达成决定/结论/妥协

- 到达（某交通工具/某日）
</details>

<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The train is expected to arrive at Platform 3 in five minutes. (CET-4 2018-12)

- 介词搭配: The distinguished delegation arrived from Beijing early this morning to attend the two-day summit.

- 介词搭配: After three hours of discussion, the negotiating team arrived at a mutually beneficial agreement.
- 介词搭配: Most of the conference attendees arrived by high-speed train to reduce their carbon footprint.
</details>

- 卫星数据每六小时到达地面站一次。
- 包裹今天早上由快递送达。
- 尊贵的代表团今天清晨从北京抵达，参加为期两天的峰会。
- 新鲜医疗物资在台风袭击沿海城市前仅几小时通过货船运到。

- 经过三小时的讨论，谈判团队达成了互利的协议。

</details>

### 110. articulate <a id="110-articulate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *articulatus* ("distinct, jointed"), past participle of *articulare* ("to divide into joints, utter distinctly"), from *articulus* ("small joint").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *articulatus*（"清晰的，有关节的"），*articulare*（"分成关节，清晰发音"）的过去分词，源自 *articulus*（"小关节"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To express ideas or feelings clearly; 2) To pronounce words distinctly; 3) To be jointed.
- CN: 1) 明确表达，清晰说明；2) 清晰地发音；3) 用关节连接。

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- The verb is pronounced /ɑrˈtɪkjəˌleɪt/; the adjective is /ɑrˈtɪkjələt/. — 动词发音为 /ɑrˈtɪkjəˌleɪt/；形容词为 /ɑrˈtɪkjələt/- *Articulate something*: "She articulated her concerns clearly." — *清晰表达某事*："她清晰地表达了自己的担忧"
- In public speaking and writing, this verb signals precision and clarity. — 在公共演讲和写作中，该词表示精确和清晰

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- articulate one's thoughts/ideas/feelings
- articulate a vision/goal/strategy
- clearly articulate / well articulated
- articulate words/speech
- articulate clearly/effectively
- articulate one's position
- articulate a plan/strategy
- well/poorly articulated


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 清晰地表达想法/观点/感受
- 阐明愿景/目标/策略
- 清晰地表达 / 表达得很好
- 清晰地发音/说话
- 清晰/有效地表达
- 阐明立场
- 阐明计划/策略
- 表达得好/不好
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- articulate with (anatomy/mechanics)
- articulate in speech/writing
- articulate to sb


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 与……连接（解剖学/机械学）
- 在口语/写作中表达
- 向某人清晰表达
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The speaker articulated his arguments so clearly that everyone understood. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: It is important to articulate your career goals during the interview. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The professor articulated the complex theory in simple terms. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The paper articulates a novel framework for understanding neural plasticity. (Neuron 2020)
- Common: She struggled to articulate her feelings in the moment.
- 介词搭配: The CEO clearly articulated the company's new strategic vision to all employees during the annual shareholders' meeting.
- 介词搭配: It is essential for researchers to articulate their findings in writing when submitting papers to international journals.
- 介词搭配: In the human skeletal system, the femur articulates with the hip bone to form the hip joint.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 演讲者清晰地阐述了他的论点，每个人都听懂了。
- 面试时清晰地表达你的职业目标很重要。
- 教授用简单的语言阐述了复杂的理论。
- 这篇论文阐述了一个理解神经可塑性的新框架。
- 那一刻她难以表达自己的感受。
- 首席执行官在年度股东大会上向所有员工清晰地阐述了公司的新战略愿景。
- 研究人员在向国际期刊提交论文时必须用书面形式清晰地阐述他们的发现。
- 在人体骨骼系统中，股骨与髋骨连接构成髋关节。
</details>

### 111. ascend <a id="111-ascend"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *ascendere* ("to climb up, mount"), from *ad-* ("to") + *scandere* ("to climb").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *ascendere*（"攀登，登上"），来自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *scandere*（"攀登"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To go up or climb something; 2) To rise to a higher level or position; 3) To slope upward.
- CN: 1) 攀登，登上；2) 晋升，升迁；3) （斜坡）向上。

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- More formal than *climb* or *go up*; used in literary, technical, and formal contexts. — 比 *climb* 或 *go up* 更正式；用于文学、技术和正式语境- *Ascend to power/the throne*: to become a ruler. — *掌权/登上王位*：成为统治者
- Opposite: *descend*. — 反义词：*descend*

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- ascend a mountain/peak/stairs
- ascending order (opposite of descending)
- ascend rapidly/slowly/steadily
- ascend quickly/slowly
- ascend gradually/steeply
- ascend the ladder/ranks
- ascend the throne


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 攀登高山/顶峰/楼梯
- 升序（降序的反义）
- 快速/缓慢/稳定地上升
- 快速/缓慢上升
- 逐渐/陡峭地上升
- 晋升阶梯/级别
- 登上王位
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- ascend to power/the throne
- ascend to heaven (religious context)
- ascend to the top/summit
- ascend from sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 上台掌权/登上王位
- 升天（宗教语境）
- 登上顶端/顶峰
- 从……上升/攀登
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The hikers began to ascend the steep mountain trail early in the morning. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: He quickly ascended to a senior management position within the company. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: As the balloon ascended, the temperature dropped significantly. (TOEFL TPO 41)
- Academic: The aircraft ascended to a cruising altitude of 35,000 feet. (Science 2021)
- Common: We watched the smoke ascend into the clear blue sky.
- 介词搭配: The hot air balloon ascended gracefully from the valley floor and floated over the mountain range.
- 介词搭配: The young prince ascended to the throne at the age of eighteen following his father's sudden abdication.
- 介词搭配: According to traditional Christian belief, Jesus Christ ascended to heaven forty days after his resurrection.
- 介词搭配: The determined mountaineers finally ascended to the summit after weeks of difficult climbing in extreme conditions.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 徒步者们一大早就开始攀登陡峭的山路。
- 他很快晋升到了公司的高级管理职位。

- 飞机上升到35,000英尺的巡航高度。
- 我们看着烟雾升入清澈的蓝天。
- 热气球优雅地从谷底升起，飘过山脉。
- 年轻的王子在他父亲突然退位后于十八岁登基。
- 根据传统基督教信仰，耶稣基督在复活四十天后升天。
- 坚定的登山者在极端条件下经过数周的艰苦攀登终于登上了顶峰。
</details>

### 112. ascertain <a id="112-ascertain"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *acertener* ("to make certain"), from *a-* ("to") + *certain* ("certain").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *acertener*（"使确定"），来自 *a-*（"向"）+ *certain*（"确定的"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To find out or discover something with certainty; to make sure of.
- CN: 查明，确定，弄清。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Formal verb; rare in everyday conversation. Common in legal, academic, and official writing. — 正式动词；日常对话中少见，常用于法律、学术和官方写作- *Ascertain that + clause*: "We ascertained that the data were accurate." — *查明……*："我们查明数据是准确的"
- *Ascertain whether/what/who/why*: "The police are trying to ascertain the cause of the accident." — *查明是否/什么/谁/为什么*："警方正试图查明事故原因"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- ascertain the truth/facts/cause
- ascertain whether/if...
- ascertain the identity/location/nature
- difficult to ascertain
- ascertain accurately/exactly
- ascertain the cause/reason
- be difficult to ascertain
- be possible to ascertain


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 查明真相/事实/原因
- 查明是否……
- 查明身份/位置/性质
- 难以查明
- 准确/确切地查明
- 查明原因/理由
- 难以查明
- 可以查明
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- ascertain through investigation/research
- ascertain by investigation/testing
- ascertain by/through sth
- ascertain from sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 通过调查/研究查明
- 通过调查/测试查明
- 通过……查明
- 从……查明
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- 考研: Researchers are trying to ascertain the relationship between diet and disease. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: It is difficult to ascertain the accuracy of historical records from that period. (TOEFL TPO 49)
- Academic: We ascertained the genetic sequence of the novel virus through whole-genome sequencing. (Nature 2020)
- Common: The doctor could not immediately ascertain the cause of the patient's symptoms.
- 介词搭配: The detective was able to ascertain the truth from the contradictory statements provided by the witnesses.
- 介词搭配: The authenticity of the ancient painting was ascertained by carbon dating and comprehensive expert analysis.
- 介词搭配: The exact cause of the plane crash was finally ascertained through months of investigation by the aviation safety board.
- 介词搭配: The truth about the incident was ascertained through a thorough and impartial investigation.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 研究人员正试图确定饮食与疾病之间的关系。
- 很难确定那个时期历史记录的准确性。
- 我们通过全基因组测序确定了这种新型病毒的基因序列。
- 医生无法立即确定患者症状的原因。
- 侦探能够从证人提供的相互矛盾的陈述中查明真相。
- 这幅古代绘画的真实性通过碳定年和全面的专家分析得以确认。
- 飞机失事的确切原因最终通过航空安全委员会数月的调查得以查明。
- 事件的真相通过彻底公正的调查得以查明。
</details>

### 113. ascribe <a id="113-ascribe"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *ascribere* ("to write in, add to in writing, attribute"), from *ad-* ("to") + *scribere* ("to write").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *ascribere*（"写入，归于"），来自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *scribere*（"写"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To attribute something to a cause or source; 2) To consider something as belonging to or originating from someone.
- CN: 1) 归因于；2) 认为……属于（某人/某物）。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Pattern: *ascribe something to someone/something*: "The painting is ascribed to Picasso." — 搭配：*把某事归因于某人/某物*："这幅画被认为是毕加索所作"
- Often used in passive voice: "The discovery is ascribed to Newton." — 常用于被动语态："这个发现归功于牛顿"
- Do not confuse with *subscribe* (to agree with) or *describe* (to depict). — 不要与 *subscribe*（同意）或 *describe*（描述）混淆

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- ascribe correctly/incorrectly
- commonly/widely ascribed
- primarily/mainly ascribe


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 正确/错误地归因于
- 普遍认为
- 主要归因于
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- ascribe to a cause/source
- be ascribed to someone
- ascribe meaning/significance/value to
- ascribe motives/intentions to
- commonly/widely ascribed to
- ascribe to fate/luck/chance


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 归因于某个原因/来源
- 被认为是某人所为
- 赋予……意义/重要性/价值
- 认为……有动机/意图
- 普遍认为属于……
- 归因于命运/运气/偶然
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- 考研: The success of the project can be ascribed to the team's hard work. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: This ancient temple is ascribed to the 12th century. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The authors ascribe the observed effect to a previously unknown mechanism. (Nature Chemistry 2021)
- Common: People often ascribe their failures to bad luck rather than lack of effort.
- 介词搭配: Historians ascribe great historical significance to the discovery of the ancient manuscripts in the cave.
- 介词搭配: It is unfair to ascribe negative motives to people without fully understanding their circumstances and intentions.
- 介词搭配: The region's rapid economic growth is widely ascribed to its strategic location and favorable government policies.
- 介词搭配: The famous quote "Imagination is more important than knowledge" is commonly ascribed to Albert Einstein.
- 介词搭配: He ascribed his remarkable recovery from the serious illness entirely to good fortune and the dedicated care of the medical staff.
- 介词搭配: The historian ascribed the fall of the ancient civilization to a combination of climate change and external invasions.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 项目的成功可归因于团队的努力工作。
- 这座古庙被认为是建于12世纪。
- 作者将观察到的效应归因于一种此前未知的机制。
- 人们常常将失败归因于运气不好，而不是缺乏努力。
- 历史学家将洞穴中发现的古代手稿赋予重大的历史意义。
- 在不完全了解他人处境和意图的情况下，将负面动机归咎于他们是不公平的。
- 该地区的快速增长被广泛归因于其战略位置和有利的政府政策。
- "想象力比知识更重要"这句名言通常被认为是阿尔伯特·爱因斯坦说的。
- 他把自己从重病中的显著康复完全归功于好运和医护人员的悉心照料。
- 历史学家将古代文明的衰落归因于气候变化和外部入侵的 combination。
</details>

### 114. ask <a id="114-ask"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old English *āxian/āscian* ("to ask, inquire"), from Proto-Germanic *aiskōną*.

**词源：** 源自古英语 *āxian/āscian*（"问，询问"），来自原始日耳曼语 *aiskōną*。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To put a question to someone; 2) To request something; 3) To invite someone; 4) To demand a price.
- CN: 1) 问，询问；2) 请求，要求；3) 邀请；4) 索要（价格）。

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Key patterns: *ask someone something* / *ask for something* / *ask someone to do something* / *ask someone out*. — 主要搭配：*ask sb sth* / *ask for sth* / *ask sb to do sth* / *ask sb out*- *Ask* is the standard neutral verb; *inquire* is more formal. — *Ask* 是标准中性动词；*inquire* 更正式
- Common error: "ask to someone" ✗ → "ask someone" ✓ — 常见错误："ask to someone" ✗ → "ask someone" ✓

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- ask a question
- ask someone out (on a date)
- ask around (inquire among various people)
- ask politely/rudely
- ask repeatedly/again
- ask sb's opinion/advice


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 问一个问题
- 约某人出去（约会）
- 四处打听
- 礼貌/粗鲁地问
- 反复/再次询问
- 征求某人的意见/建议
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- ask for help/advice/permission
- ask after someone (inquire about health)
- ask about sb/sth
- ask for directions/help/permission
- ask sb about sth
- ask sth of sb


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 请求帮助/建议/许可
- 问候某人（询问健康状况）
- 询问某人/某事
- 问路/请求帮助/请求许可
- 向某人询问某事
- 向某人要求某事物
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: If you don't understand, don't hesitate to ask the teacher. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: The researcher asked the participants to complete a detailed questionnaire. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The immigration officer asked me about the purpose of my visit. (IELTS Cambridge 17)
- Academic: We asked whether the treatment had any significant effect on survival rates. (BMJ 2021)
- Common: He asked for the bill after finishing his meal.
- 介词搭配: I ran into your mother yesterday at the supermarket, and she asked after you and your sister's well-being.
- 介词搭配: The challenging project asked a great deal of patience and dedication of every team member.
- 介词搭配: The young entrepreneur decided to ask for professional advice on securing venture capital.
- 介词搭配: The curious students asked the museum guide about the historical significance of the artifacts.
- 介词搭配: The journalist asked the government spokesperson about the new environmental regulations during the press conference.
- 介词搭配: If you are unsure about the procedure, do not hesitate to ask for help from your supervisor.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 如果不明白，请毫不犹豫地问老师。
- 研究人员要求参与者填写一份详细的问卷。
- 移民官询问了我此行的目的。
- 我们询问了治疗对生存率是否有显著影响。
- 他吃完饭要了账单。
- 我昨天在超市碰到你母亲了，她问候了你和你妹妹的近况。
- 这个具有挑战性的项目要求每个团队成员付出极大的耐心和奉献精神。
- 这位年轻的企业家决定就获得风险投资寻求专业建议。
- 好奇的学生们向博物馆导游询问了文物的历史意义。
- 记者在新闻发布会上向政府发言人询问了新的环保法规。
- 如果你对程序不确定，请毫不犹豫地向主管寻求帮助。
</details>

### 115. aspire <a id="115-aspire"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *aspirare* ("to breathe toward, aim at"), from *ad-* ("to") + *spirare* ("to breathe").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *aspirare*（"向往，追求"），来自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *spirare*（"呼吸"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To have a strong desire or ambition to achieve something great or important.
- CN: 渴望，有志于，追求。

**Part of Speech:** vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Pattern: *aspire to something* or *aspire to do something*. — 搭配：*aspire to sth* 或 *aspire to do sth*
- Do NOT use *aspire for* or *aspire after* (archaic). — 不要用 *aspire for* 或 *aspire after*（古旧用法）
- The noun *aspiration* is very common: "career aspirations." — 名词 *aspiration* 很常见："职业抱负"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- aspiring artist/writer/professional
- high aspirations / personal aspirations
- constantly/always aspire
- earnestly/passionately aspire
- highly/greatly aspire


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 有抱负的艺术家/作家/专业人士
- 远大的抱负 / 个人抱负
- 一直追求
- 热切/充满激情地追求
- 志存高远
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- aspire to greatness/excellence/success
- aspire to be/become something
- aspire to a position/career
- aspire to leadership/management
- aspire after fame/wealth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 追求伟大/卓越/成功
- 渴望成为……
- 追求某个职位/职业
- 渴望成为领导/管理层
- 追求名声/财富
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Many young people aspire to work in the technology industry. (CET-4 2022-12)
- 考研: She aspired to become a leading scientist in her field. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Students who aspire to study abroad must prepare well in advance. (TOEFL TPO 43)
- Academic: The organization aspires to eliminate poverty by 2030. (Nature Sustainability 2020)
- Common: He has always aspired to a career in music.
- 介词搭配: The institution encourages all its students to aspire to academic excellence and innovative thinking.
- 介词搭配: In modern consumer society, many young people aspire after material wealth rather than spiritual fulfillment.
- 介词搭配: Many talented young athletes aspire to represent their country at the Olympic Games and win gold medals.
- 介词搭配: She has always aspired to a senior leadership position where she could shape corporate strategy.
- 介词搭配: The young musician always aspired to excellence in every performance she gave.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 许多年轻人渴望在科技行业工作。
- 她立志成为所在领域的顶尖科学家。
- 有志出国留学的学生必须提前做好充分准备。
- 该组织立志到2030年消除贫困。
- 他一直渴望从事音乐事业。
- 该机构鼓励所有学生追求学术卓越和创新思维。
- 在现代消费社会中，许多年轻人追求物质财富而非精神满足。
- 许多有天赋的年轻运动员渴望代表国家参加奥运会并赢得金牌。
- 她一直渴望担任高级领导职务，以便能够制定公司战略。
- 这位年轻音乐家始终追求每场演出的卓越表现。
</details>

### 116. assassinate <a id="116-assassinate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Arabic *ḥaššāšīn* ("hashish users"), referring to a medieval Ismaili sect known for political murders.

**词源：** 源自阿拉伯语 *ḥaššāšīn*（"吸食大麻者"），指一个以政治暗杀闻名的中世纪伊斯玛仪派教派。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To murder a prominent or important person, usually for political or ideological reasons.
- CN: 暗杀，行刺（重要人物）。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Reserved for the killing of important figures (political leaders, royalty, activists). — 专指杀害重要人物（政治领袖、王室成员、活动家）
- NOT used for ordinary murders or crimes of passion. — 不用于普通谋杀或激情犯罪
- The noun *assassination* is very common: "political assassination." — 名词 *assassination* 很常见："政治暗杀"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- assassinate a leader/president/politician
- attempt to assassinate
- political assassination
- assassination attempt/plot
- plan/plot to assassinate
- brutally/cold-bloodedly assassinate
- order to assassinate


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 暗杀领导人/总统/政治家
- 企图暗杀
- 政治暗杀
- 暗杀企图/阴谋
- 策划暗杀
- 残忍/冷血地暗杀
- 下令暗杀
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be assassinated by
- assassinate for political reasons
- assassinate by sb


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 被……暗杀
- 因政治原因暗杀
- 被某人暗杀
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- 考研: Several prominent leaders have been assassinated for their beliefs. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The documentary examines events leading up to the assassination of the civil rights leader. (TOEFL TPO 55)
- Academic: Archduke Franz Ferdinand was assassinated in 1914, triggering World War I. (American Historical Review 2019)
- Common: The plot to assassinate the ambassador was foiled by intelligence agents.
- 介词搭配: The revolutionary leader was assassinated by an extremist group while attending a peace rally in the capital.
- 介词搭配: Several prominent journalists have been assassinated for political reasons in regions affected by armed conflict.
- 介词搭配: The prime minister was assassinated by a lone gunman during a public rally.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 几位杰出领导人因信仰而被暗杀。
- 这部纪录片审视了导致民权领袖被暗杀的事件。
- 弗朗茨·斐迪南大公于1914年被暗杀，引发了第一次世界大战。
- 暗杀大使的阴谋被情报人员挫败了。
- 这位革命领袖在首都参加和平集会时被一个极端组织暗杀。
- 在受武装冲突影响的地区，几名著名记者因政治原因被暗杀。
- 首相在一次 public 集会上被一名 lone 枪手暗杀。
</details>

### 117. assault <a id="117-assault"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *assauter*, from Latin *assilīre* ("to leap upon, attack"), from *ad-* ("to") + *salīre* ("to leap").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *assauter*，来自拉丁语 *assilīre*（"扑向，攻击"），源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *salīre*（"跳跃"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To make a physical attack on someone; 2) To attack something vigorously (e.g., a task, challenge).
- CN: 1) 攻击，袭击；2) 猛攻，奋力应对。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Both physical violence and figurative use are common. — 身体暴力和比喻用法都很常见
- Legal term: *assault* refers to threat of violence, while *battery* is actual physical contact. — 法律术语：*assault* 指暴力威胁，*battery* 指实际身体接触
- Also a very common noun: "sexual assault," "assault charge." — 也是常用名词："性侵犯"，"攻击指控"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- assault someone physically/verbally
- assault a person/officer
- sexual assault
- assault a problem/task/challenge (figurative)
- assault brutally/savagely
- assault and battery
- verbally/physically assault
- assault the senses


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 身体上/言语上攻击某人
- 袭击某人/警官
- 性侵犯
- 攻克难题/任务/挑战（比喻）
- 残忍/野蛮地攻击
- 人身攻击与殴打
- 言语/身体攻击
- 冲击感官
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- assault with a deadly weapon
- assault with a weapon
- assault sb with sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 使用致命武器攻击
- 用武器攻击
- 用某物攻击某人
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The man was charged with assaulting a police officer. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: The army assaulted the enemy's defensive position at dawn. (考研英语 2017)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Verbal assault in the workplace is a serious offense. (IELTS Cambridge 14)
- Academic: The study examines long-term psychological effects of sexual assault on survivors. (Journal of Traumatic Stress 2020)
- Common: They assaulted the difficult problem with renewed determination.
- 介词搭配: The intruder assaulted the security guard with a metal pipe before fleeing the scene of the crime.
- 介词搭配: The angry crowd assaulted the politician with harsh criticism during the heated public forum.
- 介词搭配: The attacker was charged with assault with a deadly weapon after the violent robbery.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该男子被指控袭击警察。
- 军队在黎明时进攻了敌人的防御阵地。
- 工作场所的语言攻击是严重的违规行为。
- 该研究考察了性侵犯对幸存者的长期心理影响。
- 他们以新的决心攻克了这个难题。
- 闯入者在逃离犯罪现场前用金属管袭击了保安。
- 愤怒的人群在激烈的公共论坛上用尖锐的批评猛烈抨击了这位政治家。
- 袭击者在 violent 抢劫后被指控使用致命武器攻击。
</details>

### 118. assemble <a id="118-assemble"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *assembler* ("to assemble, gather"), from Latin *assimulare* ("to bring together"), from *ad-* + *simul* ("together").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *assembler*（"集合，聚集"），来自拉丁语 *assimulare*（"使在一起"），源自 *ad-* + *simul*（"一起"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To gather or bring people or things together; 2) To fit together the parts of something.
- CN: 1) 集合，聚集；2) 装配，组装。

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- For people (intransitive): "The students assembled in the hall." — 用于人（不及物）："学生们在大厅集合"
- For people (transitive): "The teacher assembled the students." — 用于人（及物）："老师把学生集合起来"
- For objects (transitive): "assemble furniture/a computer/a car." — 用于物（及物）："组装家具/电脑/汽车"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- assemble people/the crowd/the team
- assemble furniture/a device/a product
- assemble evidence/data/information
- assemble quickly/easily
- assemble a team/staff
- assemble parts/components
- assemble evidence/data


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 集合人员/人群/团队
- 组装家具/设备/产品
- 收集证据/数据/信息
- 快速/容易地组装
- 组建团队/人员
- 组装零件/部件
- 收集证据/数据
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- assemble in/at a place
- assemble for a meeting/purpose
- assemble in groups/crowds
- assemble from sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 在某地聚集
- 为会议/目的而聚集
- 成群/聚集
- 从……组装/收集
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The workers assemble car parts on the production line. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: All the relevant documents were assembled for the review process. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The conference assembled experts from around the world. (TOEFL TPO 37)
- Academic: The genome was assembled from short DNA sequence reads. (Nature Biotechnology 2021)
- Common: It took us three hours to assemble the bookshelf from the kit.
- 介词搭配: The demonstrators assembled in the central square to protest against the government's new environmental policy.
- 介词搭配: Leading scientists from around the world assembled for the international conference on climate change and sustainability.
- 介词搭配: The curious children assembled in small groups around the science exhibit, eagerly discussing what they had just learned.
- 介词搭配: The unique furniture was assembled from recycled wooden pallets and metal parts.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 工人们在生产线上组装汽车零件。
- 所有相关文件都已收集齐备以供审查。
- 会议汇集了来自世界各地的专家。
- 基因组是从短的DNA序列片段组装而成的。
- 我们花了三个小时把配套的书架组装起来。
- 示威者聚集在中央广场抗议政府的新环境政策。
- 来自世界各地的顶尖科学家聚集在一起参加气候变化与可持续发展国际会议。
- 好奇的孩子们围在科学展品周围三五成群地聚集，热切地讨论着他们刚刚学到的东西。
- 这件独特的家具是用回收的木托盘和金属零件组装而成的。
</details>

### 119. assert <a id="119-assert"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *asserere* ("to claim, affirm, declare"), from *ad-* ("to") + *serere* ("to join, connect").

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *asserere*（"声称，断言，宣告"），来自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *serere*（"连接"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To state something confidently and forcefully; 2) To behave in a way that expresses confidence.
- CN: 1) 断言，宣称；2) 坚持（权威、自信）；3) 主张，维护（权利）。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- *Assert that + clause*: "He asserted that the report was inaccurate." — *断言……*："他断言报告不准确"- *Assert oneself*: to behave confidently: "She needs to assert herself in meetings." — *坚持自己*：表现自信："她需要在会议上展现自信"
- More forceful than *claim* or *state*; implies confidence. — 比 *claim* 或 *state* 更有力；暗含自信

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- assert that...
- assert oneself
- assert authority/control/independence
- assert a right/claim
- confidently assert / boldly assert
- assert strongly/forcefully
- assert repeatedly/constantly
- assert dominance/superiority
- assert one's innocence/rights
- assert control/power


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 断言……
- 坚持自己的主张；展现自信
- 维护权威/控制/独立
- 主张权利/要求
- 自信地断言 / 大胆地断言
- 强烈/有力地断言
- 反复/不断断言
- 维护支配地位/优越性
- 坚持某人的清白/权利
- 维护控制/权力
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- assert to sb


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 向某人断言
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The scientist asserted that the data supported her hypothesis. (CET-4 2020-09)
- 考研: It is important to assert your own opinion during group discussions. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The author asserts that economic development does not guarantee social progress. (TOEFL TPO 39)
- Academic: We assert that this new classification system better reflects evolutionary relationships. (Nature Ecology & Evolution 2022)
- Common: If you don't assert yourself, your ideas will be overlooked.
- 介词搭配: The witness firmly asserted to the court that he had seen the defendant at the scene of the crime.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 科学家断言数据支持她的假设。
- 在小组讨论中坚持自己的观点很重要。
- 作者断言经济发展并不保证社会进步。
- 我们认为这一新的分类系统更好地反映了进化关系。
- 如果你不坚持自己，你的想法就会被忽视。
- 证人在法庭上坚定地声称他在犯罪现场看到了被告。
</details>
