### 120. assess <a id="120-assess"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *assesser* ("to assess, tax"), from Latin *assidēre* ("to sit beside, assist in office"), from *ad-* ("to") + *sedēre* ("to sit").

**词源：** 源自古法语 *assesser*（"评估，征税"），来自拉丁语 *assidēre*（"坐在旁边，辅助办公"），源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *sedēre*（"坐"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: 1) To evaluate or estimate the nature, quality, ability, or significance of something; 2) To calculate the value.
- CN: 1) 评估，评价；2) 估算，评定（金额或价值）。

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Very common in academic, business, and educational contexts. — 在学术、商务和教育语境中非常常见
- *Assess someone/something*: "Teachers assess students' performance." — *评估某人/某事*："老师评估学生的表现"
- *Assess the impact/effectiveness/risk/damage*: common collocations. — *评估影响/效果/风险/损失*：常见搭配
- Do not confuse with *access* (to enter) or *excess* (too much). — 不要与 *access*（进入）或 *excess*（过量）混淆

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- assess the situation/risk/damage
- assess someone's performance/ability/skills
- assess the impact/effectiveness/value
- assess a property/tax/fine
- critically assess / carefully assess
- assess accurately/objectively
- assess fairly/properly
- assess the extent/degree/level
- regularly/periodically assess


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 评估情况/风险/损失
- 评估某人的表现/能力/技能
- 评估影响/效果/价值
- 评估财产/税额/罚款
- 批判性评估 / 仔细评估
- 准确/客观地评估
- 公正/恰当地评估
- 评估程度/水平
- 定期评估
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- assess for suitability/quality
- assess for sth
- assess at sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 评估适用性/质量
- 为……评估
- 评估为（某数值）
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Teachers use various methods to assess students' progress. (CET-4 2022-06)
- 考研: It is necessary to assess the potential risks before making a decision. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The environmental impact of the project must be carefully assessed. (TOEFL TPO 34)
- Academic: We assessed cognitive function in 1,500 participants over a 10-year period. (The Lancet Neurology 2021)
- Common: The insurance company sent an expert to assess the damage to the house.
- 介词搭配: The antique porcelain vase was assessed at over $50,000 by the professional appraiser from the auction house.
- 介词搭配: Before recruitment, the company assesses all candidates for their communication skills and team collaboration ability.
- 介词搭配: All job applicants are carefully assessed for their suitability before being invited for an interview.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 老师用各种方法评估学生的进步。
- 在做决定之前有必要评估潜在风险。
- 必须仔细评估该项目对环境的影响。
- 我们对1500名参与者进行了为期10年的认知功能评估。
- 保险公司派了专家评估房屋的损坏情况。
- 这只古董瓷花瓶被拍卖行的专业鉴定师估价超过5万美元。
- 在招聘前，公司评估所有候选人的沟通技巧和团队协作能力。
- 所有求职者在被邀请面试前都会经过 suitability 的仔细评估。
</details>

### 121. assign <a id="121-assign"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *assigner* "to appoint, allot," from Latin *assignare* "to mark out, allot, appoint," from *ad-* "to" + *signare* "to mark, sign."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *assigner*（"任命，分配"），来自拉丁语 *assignare*（"指定，分配，任命"），源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *signare*（"标记，签署"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to allocate, designate, or appoint someone to a task or position; to attribute or ascribe something to a cause.
- CN: 分配；指派；指定；归因于

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Commonly used in the pattern *assign sb. sth.* or *assign sth. to sb.* — 常用搭配 *assign sb sth* 或 *assign sth to sb*
- In academic writing, *assign* is often used with *value*, *weight*, or *importance* — 在学术写作中，*assign* 常与 *value*、*weight* 或 *importance* 搭配
- Passive construction (*be assigned to*) is frequent in formal contexts — 被动结构（*be assigned to*）在正式语境中常见

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- assign a task / responsibility / role
- assign homework / assignment
- assign randomly/evenly
- assign blame/responsibility
- assign a number/letter/code
- assign specifically/directly


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 分配任务/责任/角色
- 布置家庭作业/任务
- 随机/均匀分配
- 归咎责任/分配责任
- 分配编号/字母/代码
- 明确/直接指派
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- assign value / weight / importance (to)
- assign sb. to a position / department
- be assigned to do sth.
- assign to a project/team
- assign to sb
- assign as sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 赋予价值/权重/重要性
- 指派某人到某个职位/部门
- 被指派做某事
- 分配到项目/团队
- 分配给某人
- 指派为……
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Each student was assigned a different topic for the research paper. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The manager assigned the new employee to the marketing department. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Participants were randomly assigned to either the control group or the experimental group. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The researchers assigned a numerical value to each variable in the model. (Nature Communications 2021)
- Common: I was assigned the task of organizing the annual company retreat.
- 介词搭配: Modern educators assign greater importance to cultivating students' critical thinking rather than rote memorization.
- 介词搭配: She was assigned as the lead researcher for the new clinical trial due to her extensive experience in oncology.
- 介词搭配: The experienced engineer was assigned to the research and development department to lead the new product design.
- 介词搭配: Three junior officers were assigned to investigate the security breach in the computer system.
- 介词搭配: In the final grading rubric, the professor assigned equal weight to both the midterm and final exam.
- 介词搭配: The most difficult tasks were assigned to the most experienced members of the research team.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 每个学生被分配了不同的研究论文题目。
- 经理把新员工分配到了市场部。
- 参与者被随机分配到对照组或实验组。
- 研究人员为模型中的每个变量赋予了一个数值。
- 我被分配了组织公司年度团建的任务。
- 现代教育工作者更加重视培养学生的批判性思维而非死记硬背。
- 由于她在肿瘤学领域的丰富经验，她被指派为这项新临床试验的首席研究员。
- 这位经验丰富的工程师被指派到研发部门领导新产品设计工作。
- 三名初级官员被指派调查计算机系统的安全漏洞。
- 在最终评分标准中，教授为期中考试和期末考试分配了相同的权重。
- 最困难的任务被分配给了研究团队中最有经验的成员。
</details>

### 122. assimilate <a id="122-assimilate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *assimilare* "to make similar," from *ad-* "to" + *similis* "like, similar."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *assimilare*（"使相似"），来自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *similis*（"相似的"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to absorb and integrate (information, ideas, or culture); to cause (food) to be absorbed into the body.
- CN: 吸收；消化；同化；融入

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- As a transitive verb: *assimilate sth.* (knowledge, nutrients, immigrants) — 作为及物动词：*assimilate sth*（知识、营养、移民）
- As an intransitive verb: *assimilate into/to* (a new culture or group) — 作为不及物动词：*assimilate into/to*（新文化或群体）
- In sociology, *assimilate* carries significant weight concerning cultural integration — 在社会学中，*assimilate* 在文化融合方面具有重要含义

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- assimilate knowledge / information
- assimilate nutrients / food
- assimilate new ideas
- assimilate fully/completely
- assimilate quickly/slowly
- assimilate new technology
- assimilate foreign customs/ideas


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 吸收知识/信息
- 吸收营养/食物
- 吸收新思想
- 完全吸收
- 快速/缓慢吸收
- 吸收新技术
- 吸收外来习俗/思想
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- assimilate into a culture / society
- assimilate with the local population
- assimilate into the mainstream
- assimilate to sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 融入文化/社会
- 与当地居民同化
- 融入主流
- 被同化为；适应
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: It takes time for immigrants to assimilate into a new culture. (CET-6 2021-06)
- 考研: Children assimilate new information more quickly than adults. (考研英语 2017)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The digestive system breaks down food so that nutrients can be assimilated. (TOEFL TPO 28)
- Academic: Minority groups often face pressure to assimilate into the dominant culture. (American Journal of Sociology 2020)
- Common: It took me a few months to fully assimilate into the local community.
- 介词搭配: The new software system must assimilate with the existing IT infrastructure to ensure a smooth transition.
- 介词搭配: Immigrants gradually assimilate to the customs and values of their adopted country while preserving their own cultural heritage.
- 介词搭配: When moving to a foreign country, it takes time for newcomers to fully assimilate into the local community and understand its social norms.
- 介词搭配: It takes time for newcomers to fully assimilate into a foreign culture and understand its social norms.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 移民融入新文化需要时间。
- 儿童吸收新信息比成人更快。
- 消化系统分解食物以便营养被吸收。
- 少数群体常常面临融入主流文化的压力。
- 我花了几个月才完全融入当地社区。
- 新的软件系统必须与现有的IT基础设施相融合，以确保平稳过渡。
- 移民逐渐适应移居国的习俗和价值观，同时保留自己的文化遗产。
- 搬到外国时，新来者需要时间才能完全融入当地社区并理解其社会规范。
- 新来者需要时间才能完全融入 foreign 文化并理解其社会规范。
</details>

### 123. assist <a id="123-assist"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *assistere* "to stand by, attend, help," from *ad-* "to" + *sistere* "to stand."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *assistere*（"站在一旁，出席，帮助"），来自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *sistere*（"站立"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to help or support someone in doing a task; to aid or facilitate a process.
- CN: 帮助；协助；援助

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Commonly used as *assist sb. in/with sth.* or *assist sb. to do sth.* — 常用搭配为 *assist sb in/with sth* 或 *assist sb to do sth*
- More formal than *help*; preferred in official, academic, and professional contexts — 比 *help* 更正式；在官方、学术和专业语境中更受青睐
- Can be used intransitively: *assist with/in* — 可作不及物动词：*assist with/in*

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- assist customers / patients / users
- assist directly/indirectly
- assist willingly/gladly
- assist sb's recovery/development


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 协助客户/患者/用户
- 直接/间接协助
- 乐意协助
- 协助某人恢复/发展
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- assist sb. with / in (doing) sth.
- assist in the development / implementation of
- assist with the investigation / research
- assist at an event / ceremony
- assist in the process/effort
- assist with tasks/duties


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 协助某人做某事
- 协助开发/实施
- 协助调查/研究
- 在活动/仪式上协助
- 协助过程/努力
- 协助完成任务/职责
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Teaching assistants are hired to assist professors with grading and research. (CET-4 2022-06)
- 考研: The new software was designed to assist doctors in diagnosing diseases. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The laboratory technician assisted the researchers in setting up the experiment. (IELTS Cambridge 15)
- Academic: CRISPR-Cas9 technology assists scientists in editing specific genes with precision. (Science 2021)
- Common: Can I assist you with anything else today?
- 介词搭配: Several volunteers assisted at the charity gala, helping with registration and guest reception throughout the evening.
- 介词搭配: The government-funded program assists in the development of sustainable energy technologies in rural areas.
- 介词搭配: The trained nurse assisted the elderly patient with his daily rehabilitation exercises after the hip surgery.
- 介词搭配: Several graduate students were recruited to assist with the research on climate change impacts.
- 介词搭配: The consulting firm was hired to assist in the development of a comprehensive marketing strategy.
- 介词搭配: The teaching assistant helped assist the professor with grading papers and preparing lecture materials.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 助教被雇用来协助教授评分和研究。
- 这款新软件旨在协助医生诊断疾病。
- 实验室技术员协助研究人员搭建实验装置。
- CRISPR-Cas9技术帮助科学家精确编辑特定基因。
- 今天还有什么需要我帮忙的吗？
- 几名志愿者在慈善晚宴上协助工作，整晚帮助处理签到和宾客接待。
- 政府资助的项目协助在农村地区开发可持续能源技术。
- 训练有素的护士帮助老年患者进行髋部手术后的日常康复训练。
- 几名研究生被招募来协助气候变化影响的研究。
- 这家咨询公司被雇来协助制定全面的营销策略。
- 助教协助教授批改论文和准备课堂材料。
</details>

### 124. associate <a id="124-associate"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *associare* "to join with, unite," from *ad-* "to" + *socius* "companion, ally."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *associare*（"联合，结合"），来自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *socius*（"同伴，盟友"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to connect or link in the mind; to join as a partner or friend; to be involved with a group.
- CN: 联系；关联；联想；交往；联合

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- As transitive: *associate A with B* (connect two things) — 及物：*associate A with B*（将两者联系起来）
- As intransitive: *associate with sb.* (spend time with) — 不及物：*associate with sb*（与某人交往）
- Frequent in academic writing for indicating correlation (NOT causation) — 学术写作中常用以表示相关性（而非因果关系）
- Common mistake: "associate to" is incorrect; use "associate with" — 常见错误："associate to" 不正确；应用 "associate with"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- closely / strongly associate
- associate member / professor / director
- commonly/frequently associate
- positively/negatively associate
- freely/openly associate
- be closely/intimately associated


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 紧密/强烈关联
- 准会员/副教授/副总监
- 通常/经常联系
- 积极/消极联系
- 自由/公开交往
- 紧密/密切相关
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- associate A with B
- associate with sb.
- associate oneself with
- associate with a group/organization


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 将A与B联系起来
- 与某人交往
- 使自己与……关联
- 与团体/组织交往
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Many people associate the color red with danger or passion. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: The study found that high levels of stress are associated with poor sleep quality. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Historians associate the Renaissance with a revival of classical learning. (TOEFL TPO 34)
- Academic: The researchers associated the genetic mutation with an increased risk of disease. (The New England Journal of Medicine 2022)
- Common: I don't want to associate myself with that controversial project.
- 介词搭配: The politician refused to associate with any radical organization during his decades-long career in public service.
- 介词搭配: The symptoms described by the patient are commonly associated with a viral infection rather than a bacterial one.
- 介词搭配: The renowned professor refused to associate himself with any political party to maintain his academic neutrality.
- 介词搭配: Most people associate the color green with nature, health, and environmental awareness.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 许多人将红色与危险或激情联系起来。
- 研究发现高水平的压力与睡眠质量差有关。
- 历史学家将文艺复兴与古典学术的复兴联系起来。
- 研究人员将该基因突变与疾病风险增加联系起来。
- 我不想和那个有争议的项目扯上关系。
- 这位政治家在其数十年的公共服务生涯中拒绝与任何激进组织有牵连。
- 患者描述的症状通常与病毒感染有关，而非细菌感染。
- 这位著名教授拒绝与任何政党产生关联，以保持其学术中立性。
- 大多数人将绿色与自然、健康和环保意识联系起来。
</details>

### 125. assume <a id="125-assume"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *assumere* "to take up, take to oneself, adopt," from *ad-* "to" + *sumere* "to take."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *assumere*（"拿起，承担，采纳"），来自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *sumere*（"拿取"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to suppose or take for granted without proof; to take on (a role, responsibility, or form).
- CN: 假设；假定；承担；呈现

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Two distinct meanings: (1) suppose without evidence, (2) take on responsibility — 两种不同含义：（1）无证据地假设，（2）承担责任
- Pattern: *assume (that) + clause* for the supposition sense — 搭配：表示假设时用 *assume (that) + 从句*
- Pattern: *assume responsibility / control / command* for the "take on" sense — 搭配：表示"承担"时用 *assume responsibility/control/command*
- Not to be confused with *presume* — *assume* often implies less confidence than *presume* — 不要与 *presume* 混淆——*assume* 通常信心程度更低

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- assume responsibility / control / command
- assume the role / position / title of
- assume that...
- assume an expression / appearance / form
- assume liability / ownership
- assume incorrectly/wrongly
- assume automatically/instinctively
- assume the worst/the best
- be commonly/generally assumed
- assume office/duties


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 承担责任/控制/指挥
- 担任……角色/职位/头衔
- 假设……
- 呈现某种表情/外观/形式
- 承担责任/所有权
- 错误地假设
- 自动/本能地假设
- 做最坏/最好的打算
- 普遍认为
- 上任/履行职责
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- assume in sb


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 认为某人具有（某种品质）
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: It would be a mistake to assume that all students learn in the same way. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The new CEO will assume office next month after the board's approval. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: In economic models, we often assume that consumers behave rationally. (IELTS Cambridge 14)
- Academic: We assume the null hypothesis is true unless the data provide sufficient evidence to reject it. (Nature Methods 2020)
- Common: Don't assume he knows the answer — you should ask him directly.
- 介词搭配: The experienced teacher assumed in her students a solid grasp of basic algebra before introducing calculus concepts.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 假设所有学生的学习方式都一样是错误的。
- 新任CEO将在下月获得董事会批准后上任。
- 在经济模型中，我们常常假设消费者行为是理性的。
- 我们假设零假设成立，除非数据提供足够的证据来推翻它。
- 不要假定他知道答案——你应该直接问他。
- 这位经验丰富的老师在引入微积分概念之前，认为她的学生已经扎实掌握了基础代数。
</details>

### 126. assure <a id="126-assure"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *asseurer* "to reassure, calm, protect," from Latin *ad-* + *securus* "secure, safe."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *asseurer*（"使安心，安抚，保护"），来自拉丁语 *ad-* + *securus*（"安全的"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to tell someone positively to remove doubt; to guarantee or make certain of something.
- CN: 向…保证；使确信；确保

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Pattern: *assure sb. (that)...* — must take a person as a direct object — 搭配：*assure sb (that)*——必须接人作直接宾语
- Distinguish from *ensure* (to make certain) and *insure* (to provide insurance) — 区分 *ensure*（确保）和 *insure*（投保）
- Cannot be used as *assure + infinitive*; use *assure sb. that* — 不能用作 *assure + 不定式*；用 *assure sb that*
- In formal writing, *rest assured* is a common set phrase — 在正式写作中，*rest assured* 是常见固定短语

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- rest assured (that)...
- assure quality / success / safety
- I can assure you...
- assure firmly/confidently
- assure personally/repeatedly
- assure customers/clients
- assure full cooperation/support


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 请放心……
- 保证质量/成功/安全
- 我可以向你保证……
- 坚定/自信地保证
- 亲自/反复保证
- 向客户/顾客保证
- 保证全力合作/支持
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- assure sb. that...
- assure sb. of sth.
- be assured of success/safety


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 向某人保证……
- 向某人保证某事
- 确保成功/安全
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The airline assured passengers that all safety measures had been taken. (CET-6 2020-12)
- 考研: The manager assured the clients of the company's commitment to quality service. (考研英语 2017)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The professor assured the students that the exam would only cover material from the lectures. (TOEFL TPO 40)
- Academic: The researchers assured the participants that all data would remain confidential. (Journal of Medical Ethics 2021)
- Common: I assure you, the package will arrive by tomorrow morning.
- 介词搭配: The manager assured the team of his full support regardless of the final outcome of the negotiation.
- 介词搭配: Students who complete the rigorous training program can be assured of excellent career opportunities upon graduation.
- 介词搭配: The project manager assured the stakeholders that the ambitious construction project would be delivered within the agreed budget and timeline.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 航空公司向乘客保证已采取所有安全措施。
- 经理向客户保证了公司对优质服务的承诺。
- 教授向学生保证考试只涵盖课堂讲授的内容。
- 研究人员向参与者保证所有数据将保密。
- 我向你保证，包裹明天早上之前会到。
- 经理向团队保证，无论谈判的最终结果如何，他都会全力支持他们。
- 完成严格培训课程的学生可以确信毕业后会有极好的职业机会。
- 项目经理向利益相关者保证，这个雄心勃勃的建筑项目将在约定的预算和时间范围内交付。
</details>

### 127. astonish <a id="127-astonish"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *estoner* "to stun, daze, deafen," from Vulgar Latin *extonare* "to leave thunderstruck," from Latin *ex-* "out" + *tonare* "to thunder."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *estoner*（"使震惊，使眩晕，使聋"），来自通俗拉丁语 *extonare*（"使震惊"），源自拉丁语 *ex-*（"向外"）+ *tonare*（"打雷"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to greatly surprise or impress someone; to fill with sudden wonder or amazement.
- CN: 使惊讶；使震惊；使惊愕

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Stronger than *surprise*; similar to *amaze* — 比 *surprise* 语气更强；与 *amaze* 类似
- Often used in the passive: *be astonished at/by/to do/that* — 常用于被动语态：*be astonished at/by/to do/that*
- The adjective *astonishing* is extremely common in both spoken and written English — 形容词 *astonishing* 在口语和书面语中都非常常见

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- be astonished to find/see/learn
- it astonishes sb. that...
- utterly / quite / simply astonished
- absolutely/truly astonish
- never cease to astonish
- astonish everyone/all
- quite/thoroughly astonished


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 惊讶地发现/看到/得知
- 让某人惊讶的是……
- 完全/相当/简直惊呆了
- 完全/确实使惊讶
- 从未停止令人惊讶
- 使所有人惊讶
- 相当/十分惊讶
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- astonish sb. with/by sth.
- be astonished at/by


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 用某事使某人惊讶
- 对……感到惊讶
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The magician's performance astonished the entire audience. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: Visitors are often astonished by the rapid pace of development in the city. (考研英语 2016)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The diversity of marine life in the Great Barrier Reef never ceases to astonish researchers. (IELTS Cambridge 13)
- Academic: The team's discovery astonished the scientific community, challenging decades of established theory. (Nature 2022)
- Common: It astonishes me that he still hasn't learned to use the new software.
- 介词搭配: The young pianist astonished the audience with her flawless and emotionally moving performance of the concerto.
- 介词搭配: The research team was astonished at the completely unexpected results of their clinical trial data analysis.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 魔术师的表演让全体观众感到惊讶。
- 游客常常对这座城市的发展速度感到惊讶。
- 大堡礁海洋生物的多样性从未停止让研究人员感到惊讶。
- 该团队的发现震惊了科学界，挑战了数十年的既有理论。
- 让我惊讶的是他还没学会使用新软件。
- 这位年轻钢琴家用她对协奏曲完美无瑕且充满情感的演奏让观众惊叹不已。
- 研究团队对他们临床试验数据分析得出的完全意想不到的结果感到惊讶。
</details>

### 128. attach <a id="128-attach"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *attachier* "to fasten, fix," from *a-* "to" + Frankish *stakka* "stake, post."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *attachier*（"固定，系牢"），来自 *a-*（"向"）+ 法兰克语 *stakka*（"桩，柱"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to fasten or join one thing to another; to connect or assign; to be associated with.
- CN: 附上；贴上；连接；附加；使依恋

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Core pattern: *attach A to B* — 基本搭配：*attach A to B*- *attach importance / significance / value to* is very common in academic writing — *attach importance/significance/value to* 在学术写作中很常见- *be attached to sb.* means "to be fond of or devoted to" — *be attached to sb* 意思是"喜欢或依恋某人"
- In computing: *attach a file* (to an email) — 在计算机领域：*attach a file*（附上文件）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- attach a file / document
- attach conditions / strings (to an agreement)
- attach securely/firmly
- attach a label/tag/signature
- attached please find
- be directly/indirectly attached


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 附上文件/文档
- 附加条件（给协议）
- 牢固地附上
- 贴上标签/签名
- 随函附上
- 直接/间接连接
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- attach A to B
- attach importance / significance / value to
- be attached to sb./sth. (emotionally)
- attach to an email/document


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 将A附在B上
- 重视/认为……重要/赋予……价值
- 依恋某人/某物
- 附在电子邮件/文档上
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Please attach your resume to the application form before submitting it. (CET-4 2023-06)
- 考研: Many scholars attach great importance to the role of education in economic development. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Electrodes were attached to the subjects' scalps to measure brain activity. (TOEFL TPO 48)
- Academic: The researchers attached tracking devices to the migratory birds to monitor their routes. (Science 2021)
- Common: I've attached the photos from our trip to this email.
- 介词搭配: Modern educational theorists attach great importance to developing students' independent critical thinking abilities.
- 介词搭配: After living in that quaint coastal village for over twenty years, she became deeply attached to the place and its people.
- 介词搭配: Before mailing the package, she attached the shipping label securely to the cardboard box with strong tape.
- 介词搭配: Please attach the completed application form to the email and send it to the admissions office.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 请在提交前将简历附在申请表上。
- 许多学者非常重视教育在经济发展中的作用。
- 电极被连接到受试者的头皮上以测量大脑活动。
- 研究人员给候鸟安装了追踪装置以监测它们的迁徙路线。
- 我把我们旅行的照片附在这封邮件里了。
- 现代教育理论家非常重视培养学生独立的批判性思维能力。
- 在那个古朴的海滨小村生活了二十多年后，她深深眷恋上了那个地方和那里的人们。
- 寄包裹前，她用强力胶带将运输标签牢牢地贴在纸箱上。
- 请将填写好的申请表附在邮件中发送给招生办公室。
</details>

### 129. attack <a id="129-attack"></a>
**Etymology:** From French *attaquer*, from Italian *attaccare* "to attach, join, attack," from Vulgar Latin *attaccare* "to attach."

**词源：** 源自法语 *attaquer*，来自意大利语 *attaccare*（"系上，连接，攻击"），源自通俗拉丁语 *attaccare*（"系上"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to act aggressively against someone or something; to criticize severely; to begin working on a problem.
- CN: 攻击；进攻；抨击；着手处理

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi./n.

**Usage Notes:**

- Can be physical (military attack), verbal (attack someone's argument), or figurative (attack a problem) — 可以是身体上（军事攻击）、言语上（抨击论点）或比喻意义上（着手解决问题）
- As a noun: *launch / carry out an attack on* — 作为名词：*launch/carry out an attack on*
- Medical context: *heart attack*, *panic attack* — 医学语境：*heart attack*（心脏病发作），*panic attack*（惊恐发作）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- attack sb./sth. (physically or verbally)
- attack a problem / task (figurative)
- heart attack / cyber attack / terrorist attack
- attack fiercely/savagely
- attack verbally/physically
- attack suddenly/unexpectedly
- attack the enemy/opponent
- attack a problem/difficulty


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 攻击某人/某物（身体上或言语上）
- 着手处理问题/任务（比喻）
- 心脏病发作/网络攻击/恐怖袭击
- 猛烈/野蛮地攻击
- 言语/身体攻击
- 突然/意外地攻击
- 攻击敌人/对手
- 着手解决问题/困难
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- attack sb for sth
- attack with sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 因某事攻击某人
- 用某物攻击
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The editorial attacked the government's new policy on environmental protection. (CET-6 2021-12)
- 考研: Critics attacked the proposal for failing to address the root causes of poverty. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The immune system attacks foreign pathogens that enter the body. (TOEFL TPO 32)
- Academic: The paper attacks the prevailing assumption that economic growth necessarily leads to increased well-being. (Journal of Economic Perspectives 2020)
- Common: We need to attack this problem before it gets any worse.
- 介词搭配: The newspaper editorial fiercely attacked the government with sharp criticism for its mishandling of the economic recession.
- 介词搭配: Several senators attacked the new bill for its potential to increase the national debt and burden taxpayers.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这篇社论抨击了政府的新环保政策。
- 批评者抨击该提案未能解决贫困的根源。
- 免疫系统攻击进入体内的外来病原体。
- 这篇论文驳斥了经济增长必然带来幸福感提升的普遍假设。
- 我们需要在问题变得更糟之前解决它。
- 这家报纸的社论用尖锐的批评猛烈抨击了政府对经济衰退的失当处理。
- 几位参议员抨击了新法案，认为它可能增加国家债务并加重纳税人负担。
</details>

### 130. attain <a id="130-attain"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *ataindre* "to come up to, reach, attain," from Latin *attangere* "to touch, reach," from *ad-* "to" + *tangere* "to touch."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *ataindre*（"达到，触及，获得"），来自拉丁语 *attangere*（"触摸，达到"），源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *tangere*（"触摸"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to achieve or reach (a goal, objective, or level); to succeed in gaining.
- CN: 达到；获得；实现

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- More formal than *reach* or *achieve* — 比 *reach* 或 *achieve* 更正式
- Frequently used with abstract goals: *attain success*, *attain a goal*, *attain a level* — 常与抽象目标连用：*attain success*、*attain a goal*、*attain a level*
- In academic writing, very common with *level*, *status*, *objective* — 在学术写作中，常与 *level*、*status*、*objective* 搭配

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- attain a goal / objective / target
- attain a level / status / rank
- attain success / mastery / proficiency
- attainable goal (adjective form)
- attain finally/eventually
- attain a degree/diploma
- attain one's goal/aim
- easily/hardly attainable
- strive to attain


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 实现目标/目的/指标
- 达到水平/地位/级别
- 获得成功/精通/熟练
- 可达到的目标（形容词形式）
- 最终达到
- 获得学位/文凭
- 实现某人的目标/目的
- 容易/难以达到
- 努力达到
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- attain to (formal, intransitive)


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 达到（正式，不及物）
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: With hard work and dedication, she was able to attain her dream of becoming a doctor. (CET-4 2021-12)
- 考研: The company has attained a leading position in the global market. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: To attain fluency in a foreign language, learners must practice consistently. (TOEFL TPO 50)
- Academic: The spacecraft will attempt to attain orbit around Mars in February. (Nature Astronomy 2021)
- Common: He worked tirelessly to attain the level of expertise required for the position.
- 介词搭配: Few philosophers throughout history have attained to the level of enduring influence that Aristotle continues to exert on Western thought.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 通过努力工作和奉献精神，她实现了成为医生的梦想。
- 该公司已在全球市场上占据了领先地位。
- 要流利掌握一门外语，学习者必须持续练习。
- 该航天器将于二月尝试进入环绕火星的轨道。
- 他不知疲倦地工作，以达到该职位所需的专业水平。
- 纵观历史，很少有哲学家达到亚里士多德对西方思想所产生的持久影响力水平。
</details>

### 131. attempt <a id="131-attempt"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *attempter* "to try, test," from Latin *attemptare* "to try, test, attack," from *ad-* "to" + *temptare* "to try."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *attempter*（"尝试，测试"），来自拉丁语 *attemptare*（"尝试，测试，攻击"），源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *temptare*（"尝试"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to try to do or accomplish something, especially something difficult or uncertain.
- CN: 尝试；试图；企图

**Part of Speech:** vt./n.

**Usage Notes:**

- As a verb: *attempt to do sth.* (NOT *attempt doing*) — 作为动词：*attempt to do sth*（不是 *attempt doing*）
- As a noun: *make an attempt to do/at doing*, *in an attempt to do* — 作为名词：*make an attempt to do/at doing*、*in an attempt to do*
- More formal than *try*; suitable for academic and official writing — 比 *try* 更正式；适用于学术和官方写作

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- attempt to do sth.
- attempted murder / robbery / suicide
- attempt a record / feat
- attempt bravely/daringly
- attempt unsuccessfully/vainly
- abandon/give up an attempt
- a deliberate/conscious attempt
- attempt the impossible


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 尝试做某事
- 谋杀未遂/抢劫未遂/自杀未遂
- 尝试创造纪录/壮举
- 勇敢/大胆地尝试
- 尝试未成功/徒劳地尝试
- 放弃尝试
- 有意识/刻意的尝试
- 尝试不可能的事
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- make an attempt (to do / at sth.)
- in a / an attempt to do
- attempt at sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 尝试（做某事）
- 为了尝试做……
- 在某方面的尝试
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The climbers made a final attempt to reach the summit before the weather worsened. (CET-4 2022-12)
- 考研: In an attempt to reduce costs, the company decided to relocate its manufacturing operations. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Many species attempt to migrate long distances, but only a fraction survive. (TOEFL TPO 41)
- Academic: The researchers attempted to replicate the findings of the original study but were unsuccessful. (Nature Human Behaviour 2021)
- Common: Don't be afraid to attempt new challenges — that's how we grow.
- 介词搭配: The student made a brave attempt at solving the extraordinarily complex mathematical problem during the final exam.
- 介词搭配: The central bank raised interest rates significantly in an attempt to curb the rapidly rising inflation and stabilize the economy.
- 介词搭配: The climbers made a brave attempt to reach the summit despite the worsening weather conditions.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 登山者在天气恶化前做了最后一次登顶尝试。
- 为了降低成本，公司决定搬迁其制造业务。
- 许多物种尝试长距离迁徙，但只有一小部分存活下来。
- 研究人员试图复现原始研究的结果，但没有成功。
- 不要害怕尝试新的挑战——我们就是这样成长的。
- 这个学生在期末考试中勇敢地尝试解决那道极其复杂的数学问题。
- 中央银行大幅提高利率以试图抑制快速上升的通胀并稳定经济。
- 登山者勇敢地尝试在天气恶化的情况下登顶。
</details>

### 132. attend <a id="132-attend"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *atendre* "to expect, wait for, pay attention," from Latin *attendere* "to give heed to," from *ad-* "to" + *tendere* "to stretch."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *atendre*（"期望，等待，注意"），来自拉丁语 *attendere*（"注意"），源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *tendere*（"伸展"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to be present at an event or activity; to go regularly to a place; to pay attention or take care of.
- CN: 出席；参加；上（学/课）；照料；注意

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- As transitive: *attend school / a meeting / a conference* (be present) — 及物用法：*attend school/meeting/conference*（出席）
- As intransitive: *attend to sb./sth.* (deal with, take care of) — 不及物用法：*attend to sb/sth*（处理、照料）
- *Attend* in the sense of "be present" is usually transitive; no preposition needed — 表示"出席"时 *attend* 通常是及物的；不需要介词

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- attend school / college / university
- attend a meeting / conference / lecture
- attend a ceremony / funeral / wedding
- well-attended (event)
- attend regularly/faithfully
- attend a course/training/seminar
- well-attended/poorly-attended
- attend church/temple/college


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 上学/上大学
- 参加会议/大会/讲座
- 参加典礼/葬礼/婚礼
- 参加人数众多的（活动）
- 定期/按时出席
- 参加课程/培训/研讨会
- 出席人数多/少的
- 去教堂/寺庙/上大学
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- attend to sb./sth. (deal with / take care of)
- attend to business/matters
- attend at sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 处理某事/照顾某人
- 处理事务/事情
- 出席某活动
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: All students are required to attend at least 80% of the classes. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: The CEO personally attended the negotiation meeting with the international partners. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Students who attend classes regularly tend to perform better on standardized tests. (IELTS Cambridge 16)
- Academic: Over 5,000 researchers attended the annual conference on climate change. (Nature 2022)
- Common: I need to attend to some urgent matters before I can join you.
- 介词搭配: The CEO will personally attend at the official groundbreaking ceremony for the new research facility next Monday.
- 介词搭配: The emergency room nurse quickly attended to the seriously injured patient before calling the specialist doctor.
- 介词搭配: The emergency room doctor immediately attended to the critically injured patient from the car accident.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 所有学生必须参加至少80%的课程。
- CEO亲自出席了与国际合作伙伴的谈判会议。
- 定期上课的学生在标准化考试中往往表现更好。
- 超过5000名研究人员参加了气候变化年度会议。
- 我需要先处理一些紧急事务才能加入你们。
- CEO将于下周一亲自出席新研究设施的正式奠基仪式。
- 急诊室护士在呼叫专科医生之前迅速处理了重伤患者。
- 急诊室医生立即处理了车祸中受重伤的患者。
</details>

### 133. attract <a id="133-attract"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *attrahere* "to pull, draw, drag toward," from *ad-* "to" + *trahere* "to pull, draw."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *attrahere*（"拉，吸引"），来自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *trahere*（"拉，拖"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to cause someone or something to come closer or be drawn toward; to arouse interest or admiration.
- CN: 吸引；引起（兴趣、注意等）

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Pattern: *attract sb./sth. (to...)* — 搭配：*attract sb/sth (to)*
- Common in both concrete (magnetic attraction) and abstract (attract attention) senses — 在具体（磁力吸引）和抽象（吸引注意）意义上都常见
- In business and marketing: *attract customers / investment / talent* — 在商务和营销中：*attract customers/investment/talent*
- *Attract* + *attention* is an extremely common collocation — *Attract* + *attention* 是非常常见的搭配

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- attract attention / interest / criticism
- attract customers / tourists / investors
- attract investment / funding
- attract talent / top talent
- like attracts like / opposites attract
- attract strongly/powerfully
- attract mainly/primarily


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 引起注意/兴趣/批评
- 吸引顾客/游客/投资者
- 吸引投资/资金
- 吸引人才/顶尖人才
- 物以类聚/异性相吸
- 强烈吸引
- 主要吸引
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- attract attention from
- attract people/visitors to
- be attracted by/to


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 引起……的注意
- 吸引人们/游客到
- 被……吸引
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The beautiful beaches and sunny weather attract millions of tourists. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: The government has implemented new policies to attract foreign direct investment. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Brightly colored flowers attract bees and other pollinators. (TOEFL TPO 36)
- Academic: The discovery attracted worldwide attention from the media and the scientific community. (Nature 2020)
- Common: The new exhibition is expected to attract large crowds over the weekend.
- 介词搭配: Many talented young professionals are attracted to the vibrant technology startup culture in cities like Shenzhen and Hangzhou.
- 介词搭配: The professor's groundbreaking research on renewable energy attracted attention from leading scientific institutions worldwide.
- 介词搭配: The new national park, with its stunning natural landscapes and diverse wildlife, attracted millions of visitors from around the world in its first year.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 美丽的海滩和晴朗的天气吸引了数百万游客。
- 政府实施了新政策以吸引外国直接投资。
- 颜色鲜艳的花朵吸引蜜蜂和其他传粉者。
- 这一发现引起了媒体和科学界的全球关注。
- 新展览预计周末将吸引大量人群。
- 许多有才华的年轻专业人士被深圳和杭州等城市充满活力的科技创业文化所吸引。
- 这位教授在可再生能源方面的开创性研究引起了全球顶尖科学机构的关注。
- 这个拥有壮丽自然景观和多样野生动植物的新国家公园在第一年就吸引了来自世界各地的数百万游客。
</details>

### 134. attribute <a id="134-attribute"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *attribuere* "to assign, bestow, give," from *ad-* "to" + *tribuere* "to assign, give out."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *attribuere*（"分配，给予"），来自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *tribuere*（"分配，分发"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to regard something as belonging to or caused by a particular person or thing.
- CN: 把…归因于；认为是…的属性

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Pattern: *attribute A to B* — A is the result, B is the cause — 搭配：*attribute A to B*——A是结果，B是原因
- Very common in academic writing for expressing causation or origin — 在学术写作中常用以表示因果关系或来源
- Distinguish from *contribute* (to give) and *distribute* (to spread) — 区分 *contribute*（贡献）和 *distribute*（分配）
- NOT used as "attribute A as B" — always "attribute A to B" — 不要用 "attribute A as B"——始终用 "attribute A to B"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- attribute correctly/incorrectly
- commonly/widely attribute
- largely/mainly attribute
- be rightly/wrongly attributed


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 正确/错误地归因
- 普遍归因于
- 主要归因于
- 被正确/错误地归因
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- attribute A to B
- be attributed to...
- attribute importance / significance to
- attribute success / failure to
- directly / commonly / widely attributed to
- attribute to a factor/reason/cause


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 将A归因于B
- 归因于……
- 认为……重要/有意义
- 将成功/失败归因于
- 直接/普遍/广泛归因于
- 归因于某个因素/原因
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Many health problems can be attributed to poor diet and lack of exercise. (CET-6 2022-12)
- 考研: The economist attributed the country's rapid growth to its investment in infrastructure. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The decline in the bee population has been attributed to pesticide use and habitat loss. (TOEFL TPO 44)
- Academic: The researchers attributed the observed temperature rise to anthropogenic greenhouse gas emissions. (Nature Climate Change 2021)
- Common: She attributes her success to hard work and a supportive family.
- 介词搭配: Art historians attribute great cultural significance to the Renaissance masterpieces housed in the Uffizi Gallery in Florence.
- 介词搭配: The invention of the movable-type printing press is commonly attributed to Johannes Gutenberg of Germany.
- 介词搭配: The entrepreneur attributed her remarkable success to hard work, perseverance, and a supportive team.
- 介词搭配: Modern educators attribute great importance to developing students' critical thinking and creativity.
- 介词搭配: The increase in global temperatures is attributed to the rising concentration of greenhouse gases in the atmosphere.
- 介词搭配: The report attributes the recent economic growth to increased investment in technology and innovation.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 许多健康问题可归因于不良饮食和缺乏运动。
- 这位经济学家将该国的快速增长归因于基础设施投资。
- 蜜蜂数量的减少被归因于农药使用和栖息地丧失。
- 研究人员将观测到的温度升高归因于人为温室气体排放。
- 她把她的成功归因于努力工作和支持她的家庭。
- 艺术史学家认为存放在佛罗伦萨乌菲兹美术馆的文艺复兴杰作具有重大的文化意义。
- 活字印刷术的发明通常归功于德国的约翰内斯·古腾堡。
- 这位企业家将她的显著成功归因于努力工作、毅力和支持她的团队。
- 现代教育工作者非常重视培养学生的批判性思维和创造力。
- 全球气温升高归因于大气中温室气体浓度的上升。
- 报告将近期的经济增长归因于对技术和创新的增加投资。
</details>

### 135. auction <a id="135-auction"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *auctio* "a sale by increase," from *augere* "to increase."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *auctio*（"增价拍卖"），来自 *augere*（"增加"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to sell goods or property to the highest bidder at a public sale.
- CN: 拍卖

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi./n.

**Usage Notes:**

- As a verb: *auction sth. (off)* — 作为动词：*auction sth (off)*
- As a noun: *at auction*, *put sth. up for auction* — 作为名词：*at auction*、*put sth up for auction*
- *auction off* implies selling something, often to get rid of it — *auction off* 表示卖掉某物，常指为处理掉而卖

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- auction house / auctioneer
- online auction / charity auction
- auction publicly/privately
- auction online/offline


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 拍卖行/拍卖师
- 在线拍卖/慈善拍卖
- 公开/私下拍卖
- 线上/线下拍卖
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- auction sth. off
- put sth. up for auction
- sell sth. at / by auction
- be auctioned for charity
- auction by tender/bidding
- auction at Sotheby's/eBay
- auction to sb


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 拍卖掉某物
- 把某物交付拍卖
- 以拍卖方式出售某物
- 被拍卖用于慈善
- 通过招标/竞拍出售
- 在苏富比/易趣拍卖
- 拍卖给某人
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The painting was auctioned for a record price of over $100 million. (CET-6 2020-06)
- 考研: The bankrupt company's assets will be auctioned off to pay creditors. (考研英语 2017)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Art auctions attract wealthy collectors from around the world. (IELTS Cambridge 12)
- Academic: The spectrum auction allocated radio frequencies to telecom companies. (The RAND Journal of Economics 2020)
- Common: We decided to auction off some of our old furniture.
- 介词搭配: The valuable collection of first-edition classic novels was sold at auction for a record-breaking price.
- 介词搭配: Many celebrities generously donated their personal belongings to be auctioned for charity to support disaster relief efforts.
- 介词搭配: The antique collector decided to auction off his extensive collection of rare coins to fund his retirement.
- 介词搭配: The valuable collection of vintage watches will be auctioned at Sotheby's next spring.
- 介词搭配: The government decided to auction the oil drilling rights by sealed competitive bidding.
- 介词搭配: The estate decided to sell the valuable paintings at auction to settle the inheritance tax.
- 介词搭配: The rare collection of antique furniture was put up for auction at Christie's in New York.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这幅画以超过1亿美元的价格拍卖成交，创下了纪录。
- 这家破产公司的资产将被拍卖以偿还债权人。
- 艺术品拍卖会吸引来自世界各地的富有的收藏家。
- 频谱拍卖将无线电频率分配给电信公司。
- 我们决定拍卖掉一些旧家具。
- 这批珍贵的第一版经典小说集以破纪录的价格在拍卖会上售出。
- 许多名人慷慨捐赠他们的个人物品用于慈善拍卖，以支持救灾工作。
- 这位古董收藏家决定拍卖掉他收藏的大量稀有硬币以筹集退休资金。
- 这批珍贵的老式手表收藏将于明年春季在苏富比拍卖。
- 政府决定通过密封竞标的方式拍卖石油钻探权。
- 遗产决定在拍卖会上出售 valuable 的画作以支付遗产税。
- 这批 rare 的 antique 家具收藏在纽约佳士得被交付拍卖。
</details>

### 136. audit <a id="136-audit"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *auditus* "a hearing," past participle of *audire* "to hear." Originally referred to a hearing of financial accounts.

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *auditus*（" hearing"），*audire*（"听"）的过去分词。最初指听取财务账目的报告。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to examine and verify financial accounts or records officially; to conduct a systematic review.
- CN: 审计；查账；审查；旁听（课程）

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi./n.

**Usage Notes:**

- Primarily used in finance and accounting: *audit accounts / records / financial statements* — 主要用于金融和会计：*audit accounts/records/financial statements*
- Also used in academic contexts: *audit a course* (attend without credit) — 也用于学术语境：*audit a course*（旁听，不计学分）
- In IT: *audit log / trail* (tracking changes) — 在信息技术领域：*audit log/trail*（审计日志/追踪）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- audit financial statements / accounts
- audit a course / class
- internal / external audit
- audit trail / log
- audit thoroughly/carefully
- audit annually/regularly
- audit the books/records/accounts
- random/surprise audit
- external/independent audit


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 审计财务报表/账目
- 旁听一门课程
- 内部/外部审计
- 审计追踪/日志
- 彻底/仔细审计
- 每年/定期审计
- 审计账簿/记录/账目
- 随机/突击审计
- 外部/独立审计
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- audit for sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 为……审计/审查
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The company's accounts are audited annually by an independent firm. (CET-6 2021-06)
- 考研: An external audit revealed significant discrepancies in the financial records. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Many students choose to audit classes in subjects that interest them. (TOEFL TPO 42)
- Academic: A systematic audit of the clinical trial data confirmed the drug's efficacy. (The Lancet 2021)
- Common: As a senior citizen, I was allowed to audit university courses for free.
- 介词搭配: The independent accounting firm was hired to audit the multinational corporation for compliance with international tax regulations.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该公司的账目每年由一家独立事务所审计。
- 外部审计揭示了财务记录中的重大差异。
- 许多学生选择旁听他们感兴趣的科目。
- 对临床试验数据进行的系统审计证实了该药物的疗效。
- 作为一名老年人，我被允许免费旁听大学课程。
- 这家独立会计师事务所受雇审计这家跨国公司的国际税务合规情况。
</details>

### 137. augment <a id="137-augment"></a>
**Etymology:** From Latin *augmentare* "to increase," from *augere* "to increase, enlarge."

**词源：** 源自拉丁语 *augmentare*（"增加"），来自 *augere*（"增加，扩大"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make something larger, greater, or more intense; to supplement or add to.
- CN: 增加；增大；加强；补充

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Formal; more common in academic and technical writing than in everyday speech — 正式用语；在学术和技术写作中比日常口语更常见
- In technology: *augmented reality* (AR) is a key term — 在技术领域：*augmented reality*（增强现实）是关键术语
- The noun form *augmentation* is common in technical contexts — 名词形式 *augmentation* 在技术语境中常见

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- augment income / salary / revenue
- augment one's knowledge / skills
- augmented reality (AR)
- augment the effect / impact of
- augment greatly/significantly
- augment further/additionally
- augment capacity/efficiency


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 增加收入/工资/营收
- 增进知识/技能
- 增强现实（AR）
- 增强……的效果/影响
- 大幅增加
- 进一步/额外增加
- 提高容量/效率
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- augment with additional resources
- augment by adding/including
- augment with supplements/additions
- augment with/by sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 用额外资源补充
- 通过添加/包括来扩充
- 用补充/附加物增加
- 用……增加/扩充
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Many students take part-time jobs to augment their living expenses. (CET-6 2022-06)
- 考研: The company plans to augment its workforce with skilled engineers. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Teachers can augment traditional textbooks with digital resources. (TOEFL TPO 46)
- Academic: The treatment was augmented with chemotherapy to improve patient outcomes. (The New England Journal of Medicine 2021)
- Common: He augmented his income by working as a freelance designer on weekends.
- 介词搭配: The university plans to augment its research capacity by adding state-of-the-art laboratory facilities and hiring world-class faculty.
- 介词搭配: The employee's basic annual salary can be augmented with performance bonuses and company stock options.
- 介词搭配: The dictionary was augmented by adding over two thousand new words and phrases.
- 介词搭配: The school decided to augment its teaching program with additional online learning resources and digital tools.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 许多学生做兼职以补贴生活费用。
- 公司计划通过招聘熟练工程师来扩充员工队伍。
- 教师可以用数字资源补充传统教科书。
- 该治疗辅以化疗以改善患者预后。
- 他周末做自由设计师来增加收入。
- 该大学计划通过增加最先进的实验设施和聘请世界级教师来增强其研究能力。
- 员工的基本年薪可以通过绩效奖金和公司股票期权来增加。
- 这本词典通过增加两千多个新词和短语而得到扩充。
- 学校决定用额外的在线学习资源和数字工具来丰富其教学项目。
</details>

### 138. authorize (also authorise)<a id="138-authorize"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *autoriser*, from Medieval Latin *auctorizare*, from Latin *auctor* "master, leader, author."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *autoriser*，来自中世纪拉丁语 *auctorizare*，源自拉丁语 *auctor*（"主人，领导者，作者"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to give official permission or approval for something; to empower someone to act.
- CN: 授权；批准；许可

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Pattern: *authorize sb. to do sth.* or *authorize sth.* — 搭配：*authorize sb to do sth* 或 *authorize sth*
- Stronger than *allow* or *permit* — implies formal, documented approval — 比 *allow* 或 *permit* 语气更强——意味着正式、有文件记录的批准
- Often used in legal, financial, and governmental contexts — 常用于法律、金融和政府语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- authorize payment / a transaction / a request
- be authorized to access / use / operate
- authorized personnel / agent / representative
- authorize formally/officially
- authorize expenditure/spending
- properly/legally authorize
- authorize access/entry


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 批准付款/交易/请求
- 被授权访问/使用/操作
- 授权人员/代理人/代表
- 正式授权
- 批准支出/开支
- 适当/合法授权
- 授权进入/入场
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- authorize sb. to do sth.
- authorize the release / use of
- authorize in writing
- authorize for sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 授权某人做某事
- 批准发布/使用
- 书面授权
- 批准某事
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Only the manager is authorized to approve expenses over $1,000. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: The government authorized the release of emergency funds for disaster victims. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The research committee authorized the study after reviewing the ethical implications. (TOEFL TPO 52)
- Academic: The FDA authorized the emergency use of the new vaccine for high-risk populations. (The New England Journal of Medicine 2021)
- Common: I cannot process this refund unless you authorize it by signing here.
- 介词搭配: The finance committee must officially authorize the budget for the new infrastructure project before any construction begins.
- 介词搭配: All significant company expenditures over $10,000 must be authorized in writing by at least two senior directors.
- 介词搭配: The board of directors authorized the CEO to negotiate and sign the final merger agreement with the competing company.
- 介词搭配: The manager authorized the purchasing department to negotiate with potential suppliers.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 只有经理有权批准超过1000美元的费用。
- 政府批准拨发紧急资金给灾民。
- 研究委员会在审查了伦理影响后批准了这项研究。
- 美国食品药品监督管理局授权在高危人群中紧急使用新疫苗。
- 除非你在这里签字授权，否则我无法处理退款。
- 财务委员会必须在任何建设开始前正式批准新基础设施项目的预算。
- 所有超过一万美元的重大公司支出必须由至少两名高级董事书面授权。
- 董事会授权首席执行官与竞争公司进行谈判并签署最终合并协议。
- 经理授权采购部门与潜在供应商进行谈判。
</details>

### 139. automate <a id="139-automate"></a>
**Etymology:** Back-formation from *automation*, ultimately from Greek *automatos* "self-acting, self-moving."

**词源：** 源自 *automation* 的逆构词，最终源自希腊语 *automatos*（"自动的，自行运动的"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make a process or system operate by automatic rather than manual means.
- CN: 使自动化；自动操作

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Very common in modern technology and business contexts — 在现代技术和商业语境中非常常见
- Often used in passive: *be automated* — 常用于被动语态：*be automated*
- In manufacturing: "robots have automated many assembly line tasks" — 在制造业中："机器人已自动化了许多流水线任务"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- automate a process / task / system
- automate production / manufacturing
- automate testing / deployment
- fully / partially automated
- office automation / home automation
- automate completely/fully
- gradually/partially automate
- using technology to automate
- automate routine/tedious tasks


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 自动化流程/任务/系统
- 自动化生产/制造
- 自动化测试/部署
- 全自动/半自动
- 办公自动化/家庭自动化
- 完全自动化
- 逐步/部分自动化
- 用技术实现自动化
- 自动化日常/繁琐任务
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- automate the collection/processing of
- automate with sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 自动收集/处理
- 用……实现自动化
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Many factories have automated their production lines to increase efficiency. (CET-4 2020-12)
- 考研: The introduction of automated customer service systems has reduced the need for human operators. (考研英语 2022)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Advances in AI have made it possible to automate many complex tasks. (TOEFL TPO 54)
- Academic: The laboratory has automated the data collection process for round-the-clock monitoring. (Nature Methods 2021)
- Common: We should automate the report generation to save time and reduce errors.
- 介词搭配: The manufacturing plant plans to automate its entire assembly process with advanced industrial robotic systems.
- 介词搭配: The new software can automate the processing of massive datasets, significantly reducing the time required for data analysis.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 自动客服系统的引入减少了对人工操作员的需求。

- 实验室已经实现了数据收集过程的自动化，以进行全天候监测。
- 我们应该自动化报告生成以节省时间并减少错误。
- 这家制造工厂计划用先进的工业机器人系统使其整个组装流程自动化。
- 这款新软件可以自动化处理海量数据集，显著减少数据分析所需的时间。
</details>

### 140. avail <a id="140-avail"></a>
**Etymology:** From Middle English *availen*, from Old French *a* "to" + *valoir* "to be worth," from Latin *valere* "to be strong, be worth."

**词源：** 源自中古英语 *availen*，来自古法语 *a*（"向"）+ *valoir*（"有价值"），源自拉丁语 *valere*（"强健，有价值"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to be of use, value, or benefit; to make use of something (with "oneself of").
- CN: 有用；有益；利用

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Rarely used as an independent verb today except in fixed expressions — 现今除固定表达外很少单独用作动词
- Modern usage is almost entirely in the reflexive phrase *avail oneself of* (formal: to use or take advantage of) — 现代用法几乎完全用于反身短语 *avail oneself of*（正式：利用）
- Also common in negative constructions: *to no avail*, *without avail* — 在否定结构中也很常见：*to no avail*、*without avail*

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- to no avail / without avail / of little avail
- be of avail
- avail little / nothing
- avail fully/readily
- be of little/no avail
- without avail / to no avail


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 无用/无效/没什么用
- 有用
- 没什么用/毫无用处
- 充分/随时利用
- 几乎/完全没有用
- 徒劳无功
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- avail oneself of sth. (formal: use / take advantage of)
- not avail against
- avail oneself of an opportunity
- avail of services/resources


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 利用某物（正式用法）
- 无法抵御
- 利用机会
- 利用服务/资源
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Students are encouraged to avail themselves of all the resources available in the library. (CET-6 2021-12)
- 考研: He tried to persuade her to stay, but to no avail. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: International students should avail themselves of language support programs. (IELTS Cambridge 15)
- Academic: The researchers availed themselves of the newly developed imaging technique to study neural activity. (Science 2020)
- Common: I attempted to fix the computer myself, but all my efforts were to no avail.
- 介词搭配: College students should avail themselves of every opportunity to participate in international exchange programs and broaden their horizons.
- 介词搭配: The strongest modern defense systems did not avail against the sophisticated cyber attack launched by the highly skilled hackers.
- 介词搭配: Students should avail themselves of every opportunity to participate in international exchange programs.
- 介词搭配: Students are strongly encouraged to avail themselves of the extensive library and research facilities on campus.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 鼓励学生利用图书馆中所有可用的资源。
- 他试图说服她留下，但无济于事。
- 国际学生应该利用语言支持项目。
- 研究人员利用新开发的成像技术研究神经活动。
- 我试图自己修电脑，但所有的努力都白费了。
- 大学生应该利用每一个机会参加国际交流项目，拓宽自己的视野。
- 最强大的现代防御系统也未能抵御由技术高超的黑客发起的复杂网络攻击。
- 学生应该利用每一个机会参加国际交流项目。
- 强烈鼓励学生利用校园内丰富的图书馆和研究设施。
</details>

### 141. avenge <a id="141-avenge"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *avengier*, from Latin *vindicare* "to claim, avenge," from *ad-* "to" + *vindicare* "to claim, revenge."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *avengier*，来自拉丁语 *vindicare*（"主张，复仇"），源自 *ad-*（"向"）+ *vindicare*（"主张，报复"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to inflict harm or punishment on someone in return for a wrong or injury suffered.
- CN: 为…复仇；报…之仇

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Carefully distinguish from *revenge*: *avenge* is a verb only; *revenge* can be both noun and verb — 注意与 *revenge* 区分：*avenge* 仅为动词；*revenge* 可作名词和动词- *Avenge* implies a moral motive (justice); *revenge* implies personal retaliation — *Avenge* 暗含道德动机（正义）；*revenge* 暗含个人报复
- Pattern: *avenge sb./sth.* or *avenge oneself on sb.* — 搭配：*avenge sb/sth* 或 *avenge oneself on sb*

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- avenge sb.'s death / murder
- avenge an insult / a wrong / an injustice
- sworn to avenge
- avenge the loss / defeat
- seek to avenge
- avenge a wrong/injustice
- determined/sworn to avenge
- avenge properly/fully


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 为某人之死/谋杀报仇
- 为侮辱/冤屈/不公复仇
- 发誓报仇
- 为损失/失败雪耻
- 寻求报复
- 为冤屈/不公复仇
- 决心/发誓报仇
- 彻底报仇
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- avenge oneself on sb.
- avenge the death of
- avenge on/upon sb


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 向某人报仇
- 为……的死报仇
- 向某人报仇
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The hero vowed to avenge his fallen comrades before the battle was over. (CET-6 2019-06)
- 考研: In the novel, the protagonist seeks to avenge the murder of his family. (考研英语 2017)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Many historical conflicts were fueled by a desire to avenge past injustices. (TOEFL TPO 38)
- Academic: The concept of avenging wrongs is deeply rooted in human evolutionary psychology. (Nature Human Behaviour 2021)
- Common: He didn't want to avenge the insult; he simply wanted to move on.
- 介词搭配: In the epic tale, the exiled prince swore to avenge himself on those who had betrayed his family and seized the throne.
- 介词搭配: The ancient code of honor allowed family members to avenge the death of their kin through blood vengeance.
- 介词搭配: In the classic novel, the hero swore to avenge himself on those who had betrayed his family.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 英雄发誓在战斗结束前为阵亡的战友报仇。
- 在小说中，主人公寻求为家人的被杀报仇。
- 许多历史冲突是由为过去的不公复仇的愿望所驱动的。
- 复仇的概念深深植根于人类进化心理学。
- 他不想报复这个侮辱；他只是想继续前行。
- 在这个史诗故事中，被流放的王子发誓要向那些背叛他的家族并夺取王位的人复仇。
- 古老的荣誉法则允许家族成员通过血亲复仇来为亲人的死报仇。
- 在这部经典小说中，英雄发誓要向那些背叛他家族的人复仇。
</details>

### 142. average <a id="142-average"></a>
**Etymology:** From Middle English *averay* "damage, loss," from Old French *avarie* "damage to ship or cargo," from Arabic *ʿawār* "blemish, loss."

**词源：** 源自中古英语 *averay*（"损害，损失"），来自古法语 *avarie*（"船舶或货物损坏"），源自阿拉伯语 *ʿawār*（"瑕疵，损失"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to calculate the numerical average of; to amount to as an average; to do something typically.
- CN: 平均为；计算…的平均值；达到平均水平

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi./n./adj.

**Usage Notes:**

- As a verb: *average (out) to*, *average sth.* — 作为动词：*average (out) to*、*average sth*
- Can be both transitive ("He averaged 20 points per game") and intransitive ("The cost averages out to $10") — 可及物（"他场均得分20"）和不及物（"成本平均为10美元"）
- As a noun: *on average*, *above/below average* — 作为名词：*on average*、*above/below average*

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- on average
- above / below average
- average price / cost / temperature / score
- roughly/approximately average
- average over time/year
- average score/rate
- well above/below average


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 平均而言
- 高于/低于平均水平
- 平均价格/成本/温度/分数
- 大约平均
- 按时间/年度平均
- 平均分数/比率
- 远高于/低于平均水平
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- average (out) to / at
- average out at/to
- average at/to sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 平均为
- 平均为
- 平均为
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The students in this class average 85 percent on their final exams. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: The company's annual revenue averages around $50 million over the past five years. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: On average, women live longer than men in most countries. (IELTS Cambridge 14)
- Academic: Global sea surface temperatures have averaged significantly higher over the past decade. (Nature Climate Change 2022)
- Common: I average about seven hours of sleep per night.
- 介词搭配: Although monthly sales figures fluctuate considerably, they average out at approximately 500 units per month over the year.
- 介词搭配: The daily commute time for residents living in this suburban area averages to nearly two hours due to heavy traffic.
- 介词搭配: The daily temperature in this region averages out at approximately twenty-five degrees Celsius throughout the year.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这个班的学生期末考试平均分达到85分。
- 该公司过去五年的年均收入约为5000万美元。
- 在大多数国家，女性平均寿命比男性长。
- 过去十年全球海面温度平均显著升高。
- 我平均每晚睡大约七个小时。
- 尽管月度销售额波动较大，全年平均每月约为500件。
- 由于交通拥堵，居住在这个郊区的居民每天的通勤时间平均接近两小时。
- 该地区的 daily 温度全年平均约为25摄氏度。
</details>

### 143. avert <a id="143-avert"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *avertir* "to turn, direct," from Latin *avertere* "to turn away, turn aside," from *ab-* "away" + *vertere* "to turn."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *avertir*（"转向，指引"），来自拉丁语 *avertere*（"转开，避开"），源自 *ab-*（"离开"）+ *vertere*（"转向"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to prevent something undesirable from happening; to turn away (one's eyes, gaze, or thoughts).
- CN: 避免；防止；转移（目光、注意力等）

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Two primary senses: (1) prevent, (2) turn away — 两种主要含义：（1）防止，（2）转移- *avert one's eyes/gaze* — literal turning away of the eyes — *avert one's eyes/gaze*——字面意义上的移开视线- *avert a disaster/crisis/conflict* — preventing something bad — *avert a disaster/crisis/conflict*——阻止坏事发生
- More formal than *prevent* or *avoid* — 比 *prevent* 或 *avoid* 更正式

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- avert a disaster / crisis / catastrophe
- avert conflict / war / danger
- narrowly avert (barely prevent)
- help to avert
- avert successfully/effectively
- narrowly/barely avert
- take action/measures to avert
- avert a crisis/catastrophe
- avert one's eyes/gaze/face


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 避免灾难/危机/大灾难
- 避免冲突/战争/危险
- 侥幸避免
- 有助于避免
- 成功/有效避免
- 侥幸避免
- 采取行动/措施避免
- 避免危机/灾难
- 转移目光/视线/脸
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- avert one's eyes / gaze / attention (from)
- avert from sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 转移目光/视线/注意力
- 从……转移开
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Quick action by the fire department averted a major disaster. (CET-6 2020-06)
- 考研: Diplomatic efforts were made to avert a trade war between the two countries. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The pilot's quick thinking helped avert a potentially fatal accident. (TOEFL TPO 40)
- Academic: Vaccination campaigns have averted millions of deaths worldwide. (The Lancet 2021)
- Common: She averted her eyes when the gruesome scene appeared on the screen.
- 介词搭配: The shy student could not avert his gaze from the fascinating physics demonstration conducted by the professor.
- 介词搭配: The government tried to avert public attention from the growing economic crisis by announcing popular new welfare programs.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 消防部门的迅速行动避免了一场大灾难。
- 为避免两国之间的贸易战做出了外交努力。
- 飞行员的快速反应帮助避免了一场可能致命的事故。
- 疫苗接种运动已在全球避免了数百万人的死亡。
- 当屏幕上出现可怕的场景时，她转移了视线。
- 那个害羞的学生无法将目光从教授进行的有趣物理演示上移开。
- 政府试图通过宣布受欢迎的新福利计划来转移公众对日益严重的经济危机的注意力。
</details>

### 144. avoid <a id="144-avoid"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *esvuidier* "to empty out, get rid of," from *es-* "out" + *vuidier* "to empty," from Latin *vacuus* "empty."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *esvuidier*（"清空，摆脱"），来自 *es-*（"向外"）+ *vuidier*（"清空"），源自拉丁语 *vacuus*（"空的"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to keep away from or stop oneself from doing something; to prevent something from happening.
- CN: 避免；回避；躲开

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Pattern: *avoid + noun/pronoun/gerund* — NEVER *avoid + infinitive* — 搭配：*avoid + 名词/代词/动名词*——绝不能接不定式
- Common mistake: "avoid to do" is WRONG; must be "avoid doing" — 常见错误："avoid to do" 是错误的；必须用 "avoid doing"
- Stronger than *prevent* — implies active staying away — 比 *prevent* 语气更强——意味着主动避开
- In academic writing: *avoid bias*, *avoid confusion* — 在学术写作中：*avoid bias*、*avoid confusion*

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- avoid doing sth.
- avoid sb./sth. like the plague
- avoid confusion / conflict / embarrassment
- avoid a problem / disaster / mistake
- avoid detection / capture / scrutiny
- deliberately/intentionally avoid
- carefully/studiously avoid
- avoid unnecessary risk/expense
- avoid the issue/question/problem


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 避免做某事
- 像躲瘟疫一样避开某人/某物
- 避免混淆/冲突/尴尬
- 避免问题/灾难/错误
- 避免被发现/被捕获/被审查
- 故意/有意避免
- 小心/刻意避免
- 避免不必要的风险/开支
- 回避问题
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- avoid at all costs
- avoid by doing


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 不惜一切代价避免
- 通过做某事来避免
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: To stay healthy, you should avoid eating too much processed food. (CET-4 2022-06)
- 考研: The company took measures to avoid any potential conflicts of interest. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Many animals avoid predators by blending into their surroundings. (TOEFL TPO 35)
- Academic: To avoid bias, the study was conducted as a double-blind randomized controlled trial. (The BMJ 2021)
- Common: I try to avoid driving during rush hour because of the heavy traffic.
- 介词搭配: In academic writing, plagiarism must be avoided at all costs as it can permanently ruin a researcher's professional reputation.
- 介词搭配: The construction company managed to avoid substantial financial losses by carefully planning every phase of the complex project.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 为了保持健康，你应该避免吃太多加工食品。
- 公司采取措施避免任何潜在的利益冲突。
- 许多动物通过融入环境来避开捕食者。
- 为避免偏见，该研究采用双盲随机对照试验的方式进行。
- 我尽量避免在高峰时段开车，因为交通太拥堵了。
- 在学术写作中，必须不惜一切代价避免抄袭，因为它会永久毁掉研究者的职业声誉。
- 这家建筑公司通过精心规划复杂项目的每个阶段，成功避免了重大的经济损失。
</details>

### 145. await <a id="145-await"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old North French *awaitier*, from *a-* "to" + *waitier* "to watch," from Frankish *wahtōn* "to guard, watch."

**词源：** 源自古诺曼法语 *awaitier*，来自 *a-*（"向"）+ *waitier*（"观看"），源自法兰克语 *wahtōn*（"守卫，观察"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to wait for someone or something (formal); to be in store for; to be ready for.
- CN: 等候；等待；期待

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- More formal than *wait for*; common in literature, news, and formal writing — 比 *wait for* 更正式；常见于文学、新闻和正式写作
- Unlike *wait*, *await* takes a direct object WITHOUT a preposition — 与 *wait* 不同，*await* 直接接宾语，不需要介词
- Common mistake: "await for" is redundant; just use "await" or "wait for" — 常见错误："await for" 是冗余的；用 "await" 或 "wait for" 即可
- Can also mean "to be in store for" (e.g., "a surprise awaits you") — 也可表示"等待着"（例如："一个惊喜等着你"）

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- await the results / outcome / decision
- await approval / confirmation / instructions
- await sb.'s arrival / return / reply
- a surprise / fate / future awaits
- eagerly / anxiously await
- await patiently/impatiently
- await eagerly/calmly
- await further notice/developments
- await sb's arrival/departure
- await a response/reply/decision


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 等待结果/结局/决定
- 等待批准/确认/指示
- 等待某人的到来/返回/回复
- 惊喜/命运/未来等待着
- 热切/焦急地等待
- 耐心/不耐烦地等待
- 热切/平静地等待
- 等待进一步通知/进展
- 等待某人的到达/离开
- 等待回复/决定
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The candidates are anxiously awaiting the results of the election. (CET-6 2022-12)
- 考研: The proposal is currently awaiting approval from the regulatory authorities. (考研英语 2020)
- TOEFL/IELTS: International students often eagerly await their acceptance letters. (IELTS Cambridge 16)
- Academic: The data is awaiting further analysis before the paper can be submitted for publication. (Nature 2021)
- Common: A warm meal and a comfortable bed awaited us at the end of the long hike.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 候选人正在焦急地等待选举结果。
- 该提案目前正在等待监管部门的批准。
- 国际学生常常热切地等待录取通知书。
- 数据正在等待进一步分析，然后才能提交论文发表。
- 长途徒步结束时，热腾腾的饭菜和舒适的床在等着我们。
</details>

### 146. awake <a id="146-awake"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old English *awæcnan* "to arise, come to life," from *a-* (intensive prefix) + *wæcnan* "to wake."

**词源：** 源自古英语 *awæcnan*（"起来，苏醒"），来自 *a-*（加强前缀）+ *wæcnan*（"醒来"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to stop sleeping; to become conscious or aware of something; to rouse from sleep.
- CN: 醒来；唤醒；意识到

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi./adj.

**Usage Notes:**

- As a verb: *awake* (past: *awoke*, past participle: *awoken*) — 作为动词：*awake*（过去式：*awoke*，过去分词：*awoken*）
- As an adjective: *awake* (predicative only — "I am awake") — 作为形容词：*awake*（仅作表语——"I am awake"）- *Awake to sth.* = become aware of (figurative): "awake to the dangers" — *Awake to sth* = 意识到（比喻）："意识到危险"
- Distinguish from *wake*, *waken*, *awaken* — 区分 *wake*、*waken*、*awaken*

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**
</details>
**General Collocations (English):**

- lie / stay awake
- wide awake (fully conscious)
- awake suddenly/gradually
- fully/half awake
- stay awake all night
- be wide/fully awake


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 躺着/保持清醒
- 完全清醒的
- 突然/逐渐醒来
- 完全/半醒
- 整夜保持清醒
- 完全清醒
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- awake from / out of a dream / sleep
- awake to sth. (become aware of)
- awake sb. from / with
- awake from a deep sleep/nightmare


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从梦中/睡眠中醒来
- 意识到某事
- 把某人从……中唤醒
- 从沉睡/噩梦中醒来
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: She awoke to the sound of birds singing outside her window. (CET-4 2020-06)
- 考研: The accident finally awoke him to the importance of road safety. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Many people awake during the night due to stress. (TOEFL TPO 43)
- Academic: The study awoke public awareness about the long-term effects of microplastics. (Science 2022)
- Common: I awoke suddenly from a nightmare, covered in sweat.
- 介词搭配: The patient slowly awoke from a deep sleep after the six-hour surgery and asked for a glass of water.
- 介词搭配: The persistent ringing of the telephone finally awoke her from a peaceful afternoon nap.
- 介词搭配: The documentary about climate change finally awoke the public to the severity of the environmental crisis facing our planet.
- 介词搭配: The patient slowly awoke from a deep sleep after the complex surgical procedure.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 她在窗外鸟儿歌唱的声音中醒来。
- 那次事故终于使他意识到道路安全的重要性。
- 许多人因为压力而在夜间醒来。
- 这项研究唤起了公众对微塑料长期影响的意识。
- 我从噩梦中突然惊醒，满身是汗。
- 病人在六小时手术后慢慢从沉睡中醒来，要了一杯水。
- 电话持续不断的铃声终于把她从平静的午后小睡中唤醒。
- 这部关于气候变化的纪录片终于使公众意识到我们的星球面临的环境危机的严重性。
- 患者在复杂的外科手术后慢慢从沉睡中醒来。
</details>

### 147. award <a id="147-award"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *eswarder* "to decide, judge, award," from *es-* "out" + *warder* "to watch, guard," from Frankish *wardōn* "to guard."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *eswarder*（"决定，裁决，授予"），来自 *es-*（"向外"）+ *warder*（"观察，守卫"），源自法兰克语 *wardōn*（"守卫"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to give an official prize, payment, or judgment to someone; to grant based on merit.
- CN: 授予；给予；判给

**Part of Speech:** vt./n.

**Usage Notes:**

- As a verb: *award sb. sth.* or *award sth. to sb.* — 作为动词：*award sb sth* 或 *award sth to sb*
- As a noun: *win / receive / get an award* — 作为名词：*win/receive/get an award*
- In law: *award damages / compensation* — 在法律中：*award damages/compensation*（判给损害赔偿/补偿）
- Note: *Reward* is for return for service; *award* is for merit or achievement — 注意：*Reward* 是对服务的回报；*award* 是对功绩或成就的授予

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- award a prize / medal / trophy / scholarship
- award a contract / grant / funding
- award damages / compensation
- win / receive / accept an award
- award posthumously
- award jointly/shared
- be awarded first prize/honors
- award-winning
- decently/substantially awarded


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 颁发奖品/奖章/奖杯/奖学金
- 授予合同/资助金/资金
- 判给损害赔偿/补偿
- 赢得/收到/接受奖项
- 追授
- 共同获得
- 被授予一等奖/荣誉
- 获奖的
- 获得的奖励丰厚/可观
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- award sb. sth. / award sth. to sb.
- award to sb
- award for sth


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 授予某人某物
- 授予某人
- 因……授予
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The university awards scholarships to students with outstanding academic records. (CET-4 2022-12)
- 考研: The Nobel Prize in Physics was awarded to three scientists for groundbreaking research. (考研英语 2021)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The judge awarded the plaintiff $2 million in damages. (TOEFL TPO 47)
- Academic: The researcher was awarded a prestigious grant to continue her work on renewable energy. (Nature 2021)
- Common: She was awarded Employee of the Month for her exceptional performance.
- 介词搭配: The distinguished scientist was awarded the Nobel Prize in Chemistry for her groundbreaking research on gene editing technology.
- 介词搭配: The lucrative government contract was awarded to the company that submitted the most competitive and comprehensive bid.
- 介词搭配: The committee decided to award the prestigious scholarship to the most deserving candidate.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 大学向学业成绩优异的学生颁发奖学金。
- 诺贝尔物理学奖被授予三位进行开创性研究的科学家。
- 法官判给原告200万美元的损害赔偿。
- 这位研究人员获得了一笔著名的资助金，以继续她在可再生能源方面的工作。
- 她因表现优异被授予月度最佳员工。
- 这位杰出的科学家因其在基因编辑技术方面的开创性研究而被授予诺贝尔化学奖。
- 这份利润丰厚的政府合同被授予了提交了最具竞争力和最全面报价的公司。
- 委员会决定将这项著名的奖学金授予最符合条件的候选人。
</details>

### 148. amount <a id="148-amount"></a>
**Etymology:** From Old French *amont* "upward," from phrase *a mont* "to the hill," from Latin *ad montem* "to the mountain."

**词源：** 源自古法语 *amont*（"向上"），来自短语 *a mont*（"向山上"），源自拉丁语 *ad montem*（"向山"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to add up to or reach a total; to be equivalent or equal in significance, effect, or value.
- CN: 总计；等于；相当于

**Part of Speech:** vi./n.

**Usage Notes:**

- As a verb: always intransitive — *amount to sth.* — 作为动词：始终是不及物的——*amount to sth*
- Very common in the phrase *amount to* which has two senses: (1) numerical total, (2) figurative significance — 短语 *amount to* 很常见，有两种含义：（1）总计，（2）比喻意义上的等于
- As a noun: *a large/small amount of + uncountable noun* — 作为名词：*a large/small amount of + 不可数名词*
- Common mistake: "a large amount of people" is WRONG; use "a large number of people" — 常见错误："a large amount of people" 是错误的；应用 "a large number of people"

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- a large / small / significant amount of
- total amount / full amount
- amount roughly/approximately
- amount finally/eventually
- amount in total/sum
- a considerable/substantial amount


<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 大量/少量/可观数量的
- 总量/全额
- 大约总计
- 最终达到
- 总计
- 相当/可观的数量
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- amount to sth. (total / be equivalent to)
- not amount to much / anything
- amount to the same thing
- amount to nothing/little


<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 总计/相当于
- 没什么大不了/毫无意义
- 结果相同
- 没什么大不了/没什么价值
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The total cost of the project amounts to over $10 million. (CET-4 2021-06)
- 考研: His contributions to the field amount to a complete rethinking of the traditional approach. (考研英语 2019)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The amount of rainfall in the region has decreased significantly over the past decade. (IELTS Cambridge 13)
- Academic: The amount of carbon dioxide in the atmosphere has reached levels not seen in millions of years. (Nature 2020)
- Common: All her hard work amounted to nothing in the end.
- 介词搭配: Whether you choose to pay by credit card, cash, or check, the total cost amounts to the same thing in the end.
- 介词搭配: Without adequate funding and proper expert guidance, this ambitious research project may not amount to much.
- 介词搭配: Without proper funding and experienced guidance, the research project will not amount to much.
- 介词搭配: The total amount of donations for the disaster relief fund amounts to over one million dollars.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该项目的总成本超过1000万美元。
- 他对该领域的贡献相当于对传统方法的彻底反思。
- 该地区的降雨量在过去十年中显著减少。
- 大气中的二氧化碳含量已达到数百万年来未见的水平。
- 她所有的努力最后都化为乌有。
- 无论你选择用信用卡、现金还是支票支付，最终的总成本都是一样的。
- 如果没有充足的资金和适当的专家指导，这个雄心勃勃的研究项目可能不会有多大成果。
- 如果没有足够的资金和经验丰富的指导，这个研究项目不会有多大成果。
- 救灾基金的捐款总额超过一百万美元。
</details>
